# Fact sheet 05 - Effects, shaders, compiled effects, CNAEXT and advanced graphics

Prepared for the libcna.com Phase 1 documentation refresh. Read-only research; no website page was edited by this work.

- **CNA TARGET**: `009d40f5dd085c4e674d3479675fac84b12b3e0a` (2026-09-24, "merge(OpenGL4ModernGraphics): integrate opengl4-modern-graphics into next"), read from the immutable worktree `/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`.
- **CNA BASE** (what the deployed site was last synchronised to): `1bb2145d99ed572dd4eb15009c34e2e5f410fcf0` (v0.1.0-alpha.1), worktree `/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-base`.
- Evidence order: implementation -> build config/headers -> tests -> generated reports -> CNA Markdown (untrusted, flagged where it disagrees with code) -> old website. Citations are TARGET-relative `path:line` unless prefixed `BASE:`.
- Nothing was built, compiled or executed. "Supported" means: code path exists, is wired to the stated CMake option, and a conformance/example test is registered for it.
- Contents: Verified facts | Effects & compiled-effect matrix | Advanced graphics facts | CNAEXT | Corrections to existing site claims | Tutorial audit (31-70, 110-117, 128) | Proposed new pages/tutorials | Open questions.

**Headline.** (1) 25 renderer identities over 21 families, not 50/46. (2) Compiled XNA effects run on 14 of 25 identities (FNA3D always; nine opt-in options) instead of the site's four configurations; `.fx` source can now be compiled at build time through an external legacy fxc, never at runtime. (3) `CNA_CNAEXT` is still OFF by default but the engine layer grew from 12 to 98 headers. (4) TARGET enforces XNA graphics-profile ceilings and defaults to Reach, so many tutorial snippets (MRT, occlusion queries, 32-bit indices, float targets) throw as written. (5) Nearly every old renderer-support claim (WebGPU, DirectX 12, SDL_GPU, Software, [retired] formats, [retired]) is stale.


## Verified facts

Method: implementation first (headers, `.cpp`, CMake), then tests' registration, then generated reports; CNA Markdown only to orient and flagged where it disagrees with code. **Nothing was built or executed** (rules forbid it): "supported" means the code path exists, is wired to the stated CMake option and has a registered conformance test; executed pass counts quoted from `plans/plan_fx.md` or `docs/*.md` are CNA's own claims and are marked as such. All paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`); `BASE:` paths are in `/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-base`.

HdrBlendable`) for Texture2D and render targets. Per-renderer detail is in the capability-matrix section. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/Texture.cpp:110-172,204-211`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3784-3803`; `grep -rln ClassifyTexture2DFormatEXT modules/renderers` |
| V24 | **Source-breaking API change in the effect surface (BASE -> TARGET):** the integer subscript of `EffectPassCollection`, `EffectTechniqueCollection`, `EffectParameterCollection` and `EffectAnnotationCollection` returns a **pointer** (null when out of range, XNA semantics) instead of a reference. So `getPassesProperty()[0].Apply()` and `auto& technique = effect->getTechniquesProperty()[0]` compiled at BASE and do not compile at TARGET; write `getPassesProperty()[0]->Apply()` / `EffectTechnique* technique = ...[0]`. Range-for over the collections still yields references (CNAEXT `begin()/end()`). Commit `e55945cd7` (2026-09-09, SOFTWARE-255). | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/EffectPassCollection.hpp:87-95` (BASE: `EffectPass& operator[](int)`); same lines in the three sibling headers |
| V25 | **Default `GraphicsProfile` is Reach and TARGET enforces it on every renderer** (BASE enforced Reach ceilings only on DIRECTX9): 32-bit indices, more than 65,535 primitives per draw, more than one render target, `OcclusionQuery`, Texture3D, cubes above 512 or non-power-of-two, textures above 2048, float/HDR/packed formats, separate alpha blend and `GetBackBufferData` throw or degrade under Reach; `GraphicsDeviceManager::setGraphicsProfileProperty(GraphicsProfile::HiDef)` (or `CNA::SetProjectGraphicsProfileEXT`) lifts them. Many tutorial snippets (MRT, occlusion, terrain/procedural 32-bit indices, float bloom) therefore throw as written. | `modules/runtime/src/ProjectGraphicsProfile.cpp:12`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1347,5368-5392`; `IndexBuffer.cpp:43-57`; `OcclusionQuery.cpp:18-24`; table in section H1 |
| V26 | PBR: nine renderer families implement the metallic-roughness model - eight with full `KHR_materials_specular` texture sampling (DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, EasyGL x5 identities, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU = 12 identities), METAL factor-only (refuses vertex-colour PBR) - plus SOFTWARE as a reduced CPU cross-check (base colour x diffuse only); FNA3D and PORTABLEGL refuse PBR draws by name. Direct glTF loading assigns `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect` to metallic-roughness materials, so `dynamic_cast<BasicEffect*>` loops skip glTF parts. Shadow *sampling* and IBL are honoured only by EasyGL x5, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU. | `modules/content/tests/CNA/Internal/GltfImport/GltfRendererPbrFallbackPolicyTests.cpp:108-188,1374-1391,2419-2444`; `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:2573-2580,3383-3387`; F/G sections |
| V27 | New behaviour worth documenting: a bound resource cannot be rewritten (`SetData` on a currently bound vertex/index buffer or sampler-bound texture throws `InvalidOperationException` unless `SetDataOptions::Discard/NoOverwrite` on a `DynamicVertexBuffer`); `SpriteBatch` never restores blend/sampler/depth/rasterizer state; `Game` presents in `EndDraw` (a manual `Present()` in `Draw()` presents twice); XNA front faces are clockwise so the default `CullCounterClockwise` culls CCW-wound meshes; `PrimitiveType::PointListEXT` is the only point list. | `VertexBuffer.cpp:386-402`; `IndexBuffer.cpp:411-423`; `Texture.cpp:228-254`; `SpriteBatch.cpp:169-177`; `Game.cpp:729-734`; `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:392-408`; `RasterizerState.hpp:19-20,117`; `PrimitiveType.hpp:9-18` |


## Effects & compiled-effect matrix

Paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`); `BASE:` = alpha.1 (`1bb2145d`) tree at `/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-base`.

### E1. The effect classes that exist at TARGET

| Type | XNA 4.0? | Base / interfaces | Always compiled? | Facts (cited) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `BasicEffect` | yes | `Effect`, `IEffectMatrices`, `IEffectFog`, `IEffectLights`, `IShadowReceiverEXT` (EXT, new) | yes | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/BasicEffect.hpp:15-90`; public fields `World/View/Projection/VertexColorEnabled` plus `get/setXProperty()`; `EnableDefaultLighting()` direction/colour constants are XNA's (`modules/graphics/src/Xna/BasicEffect.cpp:468-487`). |
| `AlphaTestEffect` | yes | `IEffectMatrices`, `IEffectFog` | yes | `AlphaTestEffect.hpp`: `setAlphaFunctionProperty(CompareFunction)`, `setReferenceAlphaProperty(int)`, `setDiffuseColorProperty`, `setVertexColorEnabledProperty`. |
| `DualTextureEffect` | yes | `IEffectMatrices`, `IEffectFog` | yes | `DualTextureEffect.hpp`: `setTextureProperty`, `setTexture2Property`. Uses **two UV sets** (`aUV`, `aUV1`) and doubles the first texture (`base.rgb*=2.0`) exactly like XNA: `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/GlStockShaderSources.hpp:790-838`. |
| `EnvironmentMapEffect` | yes | `IEffectMatrices`, `IEffectLights`, `IEffectFog` | yes | `EnvironmentMapEffect.hpp`: `setEnvironmentMapProperty(TextureCube*)`, `setEnvironmentMapAmountProperty`, `setEnvironmentMapSpecularProperty`, `setFresnelFactorProperty`. |
| `SkinnedEffect` | yes | `IEffectMatrices`, `IEffectLights`, `IEffectFog`, `IShadowReceiverEXT` | yes | `SkinnedEffect.hpp`: `MaxBones = 72`; `setWeightsPerVertexProperty(1\|2\|4)` else `ArgumentOutOfRangeException` (`modules/graphics/src/Xna/SkinnedEffect.cpp:292-298`); `SetBoneTransforms(const std::vector<Matrix>&)` (`:300-308`). |
| `SpriteEffect` | yes | plain `Effect` - **not** `IEffectMatrices` | yes | `SpriteEffect.hpp` (`class SpriteEffect : public Effect`). |
| `PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect` | no (CNAEXT) | `Effect`, `IEffectMatrices/Fog/Lights`, `IShadowReceiverEXT` | yes | `PbrEffect.hpp`: base colour, normal, metallic-roughness, emissive, occlusion maps + KHR-style specular; `setImageBasedLightEXT`, shadow receiver members. Skinned variant up to 72 bones. Constructor `explicit PbrEffect(GraphicsDevice&)`. |
| `ShaderEffect` | no (CNAEXT) | `Effect`, `IEffectMatrices` | ctor `(device, vertSrc, fragSrc)` always; `ShaderCodeEXT`/`ShaderPackageEXT` overloads, texture-array/storage-texture binding only with `CNA_CNAEXT` | `ShaderEffect.hpp:46-72, 98-131, 179, 223`; uniform setters `SetUniformMat4/Vec4/Vec3/Vec2/Float/Int/...Array`, `GetCompileErrorEXT()`, `GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT()`, `GetSelectedShaderLanguageEXT()`, `DeclareUniformBlockEXT()`. |
| `ColorMatrixEffect` | no (CNAEXT) | `Effect` | yes | CPU RGBA colour matrix for `SpriteBatch` on **GDI and SOFTWARE only** (`ColorMatrixEffect.hpp:12-27`); unchanged since BASE. |
| `Effect` (compiled) | yes | base class | yes | `Effect(GraphicsDevice&, const std::vector<SharpRuntime::bytecs>&)` - see E2. Also `CNAEXT Effect::Apply()` (`Effect.hpp:120`, doc comment "FNA has no public Effect::Apply()"). |
| `EffectMaterial` | yes | | yes | XNB reader `EffectMaterialReader` (`modules/content/src/Xnb/EffectMaterialContentTypeReaders.cpp:94-157`). |
| `CRTEffect`, `DepthEffect` | no (CNAEXT, **gated** by `CNA_CNAEXT`) | derive from `ShaderEffect` | no | `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/CRTEffect.hpp:48`, `DepthEffect.hpp:38`. |
| `AsciiPostProcessEffect` | no (CNAEXT, gated) | **not** an `Effect`; CPU read-back + `SpriteBatch` | no | `AsciiPostProcessEffect.hpp` ("Portable CPU implementation (v1): `Draw()` reads the source texture back to the CPU"); header text unchanged BASE -> TARGET. |
| `ClusteredForwardEffect` | no (CNAEXT, gated) | standalone class wrapping a `ShaderEffect` | no | `ClusteredForwardEffect.hpp:61`. |
| `CNA::Graphics::EffectPass` | no (CNAEXT, gated) | `PostProcessPass`; runs any XNA `Effect` as a fullscreen pass | no | `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/EffectPass.hpp:35` (name clashes with `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::Graphics::EffectPass`). |

Stock-effect **set is unchanged** between BASE and TARGET: six XNA stock effects (Basic, AlphaTest, DualTexture, EnvironmentMap, Skinned, Sprite); the five non-Sprite ones have XNB readers `BasicEffectReader ... SkinnedEffectReader` (`modules/content/src/Xnb/StockEffectContentTypeReaders.cpp:156-170`) that work on every 3D renderer with no compiled-effect capability. Public signature diff of the six stock effects BASE -> TARGET is limited to: `IShadowReceiverEXT` on Basic/Skinned/Pbr/SkinnedPbr, `BasicEffect::get/setVertexColorEnabledProperty`, copy-constructor visibility, `PbrEffect::setImageBasedLightEXT` (checked by comment-stripped `diff` of the headers).

**Correct C++ spelling** (site pages mostly use invented names): properties are `getXProperty()/setXProperty()` (e.g. `effect->setWorldProperty(m)`, `setTextureProperty(tex)`, `setTextureEnabledProperty(true)`, `setAlphaFunctionProperty(CompareFunction::Greater)`, `setReferenceAlphaProperty(128)`, `setTexture2Property`, `setEnvironmentMapProperty`, `setWeightsPerVertexProperty`); `Effect::getCurrentTechniqueProperty()` returns `EffectTechnique*` (`Effect.hpp:72`), `EffectTechnique::getPassesProperty()` returns `EffectPassCollection&` (`EffectTechnique.hpp:50`), `EffectPassCollection::operator[]` returns `EffectPass*` (`EffectPassCollection.hpp:87`), so `effect->getCurrentTechniqueProperty()->getPassesProperty()[0]->Apply()` (**source-breaking since BASE**, where these subscripts returned references: V24); range-for `for (auto& pass : passes) pass.Apply();` also works (CNAEXT iterators `EffectPassCollection.hpp:112-139`). There is no `CurrentTechnique`, `Passes`, `Parameters` member or `SetWorld()`-style setter. `EffectParameter` has `GetValueSingle/GetValueMatrix/GetValueVector3/...` and overloaded `SetValue(...)`, no `GetValue<T>()` template (`EffectParameter.hpp:36-120`); `effect->getParametersProperty()["Name"]` returns `EffectParameter*` (null when absent, `EffectParameterCollection.hpp:103`). `DirectionalLight` has `get/setDirectionProperty/DiffuseColor/SpecularColor/Enabled` and `Direction` is the direction the light travels (default key light `(-0.53,-0.57,-0.63)`, `BasicEffect.cpp:474`), not "toward the light source".

### E2. Compiled XNA Effect Framework bytecode - the pipeline at TARGET

**What is accepted / refused** (`modules/graphics/src/Xna/Effect.cpp`):
- Constants: 64 MiB ceiling `kMaximumCompiledEffectBytes` (`:18`), Effect Framework token `0xFEFF0901` (`:19`) and XNA 4 wrapper token `0xBCF00BCF` (`:20`); structure/reflection preflight `EffectFrameworkPreflight(...).Validate()` (`:572-575`).
- Ctor `Effect(GraphicsDevice&, const std::vector<SharpRuntime::bytecs>&)` (`Effect.hpp:33-58`, impl `Effect.cpp:780-822`). Order of checks and exceptions: empty -> `System::ArgumentException("Compiled effect bytecode must not be empty.")`; > 64 MiB -> `ArgumentException("... exceeds CNA's 64 MiB safety limit.")`; first four bytes `MGFX` -> `System::NotSupportedException("The supplied bytes are a MonoGame MGFX effect ...")`; header not structurally valid -> `ArgumentException("... does not contain a structurally valid XNA Direct3D 9 Effect Framework header.")`; then `device.SupportsCapability(CompiledEffects)` false -> `NotSupportedException("The active graphics renderer does not support compiled XNA/FNA Effect Framework bytecode (GraphicsCapability::CompiledEffects is false).")`; runtime creation returns null -> `NotSupportedException("... advertised compiled effects but failed to create a compiled-effect runtime.")`. So malformed input is judged **before** the renderer is consulted (malformed input throws `ArgumentException` on every renderer; a valid binary on an incapable renderer throws `NotSupportedException`).
- **The constructor is not "still throwing"**: it constructs and reflects a real object graph wherever `CompiledEffects` is true (E3). `Effect::Clone()`, technique/pass selection, `EffectPass::Apply()` exact-pass semantics, dirty-tracked parameter upload and SpriteBatch use are implemented (tests: `tests/support/CNA/TestSupport/CompiledEffectConformance.hpp` shared gate, called by the ten per-renderer files in E3).
- XNB: `Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Content.EffectReader` (`modules/content/src/Xnb/EffectContentTypeReader.cpp:23-58`) reads an Int32 length (0..64 MiB else `ContentLoadException("... declares an invalid bytecode length")`), an exact payload (`ReadBytesExactOrThrow`), builds `std::make_shared<Effect>(device, bytes)`, sets `Name` to the asset name, and wraps every non-`ContentLoadException` failure (including the `NotSupportedException` of an incapable renderer) in `ContentLoadException("'<asset>': EffectReader could not create the compiled effect", inner)`. Registered by `RegisterEffectXnbReader()` (`:60-65`). Load as `content.Load<std::shared_ptr<Effect>>("Effects/Bloom")`.
- Capability query: `GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability(CNA::GraphicsCapability::CompiledEffects)` answers from the dedicated renderer hook `IGraphicsRenderer::SupportsCompiledEffects()` (default **false**, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3055`), never from a renderer's legacy `SupportsCapability` switch (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3402-3409`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3780-3790`). Richer query: `RendererFeature::CompiledXnaEffects` (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:56`) via `GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()`. C ABI: `cna_effect_create_compiled` and `CNA_GRAPHICS_CAPABILITY_COMPILED_EFFECTS` (`modules/c-api/include/CNA/C/effects.h:1245`, `modules/c-api/src/CnaCApiGraphics.cpp:208-210`; the C header prose still lists only FNA3D/SDL_GPU/Vulkan/EasyGL - stale).
- `CompiledEffects` is **separate** from `CustomEffects` (ShaderEffect source pair): `GraphicsCapability.hpp:111-119`.
- Still NOT accepted: MGFX/`.mgfxo` (`Effect.cpp:794-800`), HLSL `.fx` source and raw DXBC/GLSL/SPIR-V/Metal as an Effect Framework payload (structural check). No runtime HLSL compiler exists in CNA for this path.

**Translator / compiler work at TARGET (all new or extended since BASE):**
- Shader translation for compiled effects is done by a pinned MojoShader (`FNA3D` commit `3240147`, MojoShader `6333f74dbd5644789a63e903816441b16c1e8b60`, `cmake/ThirdPartyFNA3D.cmake:21-54`) carrying **118 managed patches** in `cmake/patches/` (`ls cmake/patches | grep -c mojoshader` = 118 at TARGET, 2 at BASE): parser robustness/validation (fuzz-driven), GLSL fragment `highp`, ES vertex-colour clamp, centroid handling, legacy bump/texkill/depth semantics, etc. Dialect per renderer: EasyGL `glsl120` (OPENGL33) / `glsles` (ES2, WebGL1) / `glsles3` (ES3, WebGL2) - never `glspirv` (`modules/renderers/easygl/src/EasyGLCompiledEffect.cpp:43-61`); OPENGL4 `glsl120` (`modules/renderers/opengl4/src/OpenGL4CompiledEffects.cpp:260-264`); SDL_GPU/Vulkan/WebGPU SPIR-V profile (`VulkanCompiledEffect.cpp:52`, `WebGPUCompiledEffect.cpp:53-56`); DirectX 11 MojoShader's D3D11 adapter; DirectX 12 HLSL profile then `D3DCompile` to SM4 DXBC (`D3D12CompiledEffect.cpp:45`); DirectX 9 parses via the HLSL profile for reflection only and feeds the **original D3D9 token streams** to the native device (`D3D9CompiledEffect.cpp:66`); SOFTWARE runs the SM token streams in a CPU interpreter (`modules/renderers/software/src/SoftwareCompiledEffect.cpp`, 872 lines).
- WebGPU: SPIR-V -> `SplitCombinedImageSamplers` -> native `WGPUShaderSourceSPIRV`; browser build translates the SPIR-V to **WGSL** in CNA code (`modules/renderers/common/mojoshader/src/SpirvToWgsl.cpp`, 1,609 lines; `SpirvCombinedSamplerSplit.cpp`; `SpirvSamplerLodBias.cpp`). `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:352-364`.
- SDL_GPU: `CNA_SDL_GPU_SHADERCROSS` (default **ON on Windows and Apple**, OFF elsewhere, `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:472-490`) translates the SPIR-V stock shaders (and compiled effects, `:497-503`) to DXBC/MSL through SDL_shadercross, so at TARGET SDL_GPU is no longer "Vulkan-only" (BASE: `SDL_GPU_SHADERFORMAT_SPIRV` only, `BASE:modules/renderers/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:1293`).
- **Build-time `.fx` route (new, content pipeline)**: `cna-content` (`cmake/ToolContentPipeline.cmake:20`, executable name `cna-content`) registers `CNA.CompiledEffectImporter` (`.fxb`, no compiler needed; `modules/content/src/Pipeline/EffectContentPipeline.cpp:25-147`) and `CNA.EffectSourceImporter`/`Processor` (`.fx` source; `modules/content-pipeline/src/EffectSourceContentPipeline.cpp:1-40,66-67`), output written by `CNA.XnbEffectWriter` **only with `--format xnb`** (`modules/content/src/Pipeline/XnbOutputContentPipeline.cpp:379-398`; `XnbAssetTypeWriters.cpp:1243-1259`, refuses an empty payload and non-verified platform payloads via `RequireVerifiedPlatformPayload`). `.fx` source is compiled by an **external, legacy `fxc` at profile `fx_2_0`** (`modules/content-pipeline/src/EffectCompilerService.cpp:60`; comment block `:1-45` records why: `fx_2_0` delegates to d3dx9 and a modern `d3dcompiler_47` cannot write a legacy effect: "E5017 ... Write pass assignments"); discovery order `--fx-compiler` > `CNA_FXC` env > `-DCNA_FXC_EXECUTABLE` (cache var, `modules/content-pipeline/CMakeLists.txt:110-120`) > `fxc`/`fxc.exe` on PATH, launcher (`wine`) via `--fx-compiler-launcher`/`CNA_FXC_LAUNCHER` (`EffectCompilerService.cpp:960-990` is the failure text; orientation `docs/content-pipeline.md:412-470`). Output that is not an Effect Framework 9.1 container is rejected, never embedded (`EffectSourceContentPipeline.cpp:12-16`). CNA does **not** vendor or embed an HLSL compiler; the route is untested against genuine Microsoft `fxc` (`docs/content-pipeline.md:467-470`, a CNA doc - not code-verified). The pipeline's `EffectImporter`/`EffectProcessor` XNA-shaped API (`modules/content-pipeline/src/Xna/EffectImporter.cpp`) reads `.fx` text into `EffectContent` and compiles through the same service.
- Offline authoring tools (not runtime): `tools/shader_package/generate_shader_package.py` (GLSL -> SPIR-V through libshaderc, emits checked-in `*.generated.hpp` shader packages; `--check` reproducibility gate; runtime never links shaderc/DXC), `modules/renderers/directx9/src/shaders/compile_shaders_sm2.py` (fxc for D3D9 blobs), `modules/renderers/sdl-gpu/src/shaders/compile_shaders.py`. Runtime GLSL -> SPIR-V compile exists only for SDL_GPU `ShaderEffect` (libshaderc found and target not Windows/Apple/Emscripten: `modules/renderers/sdl-gpu/CMakeLists.txt:30-70`).

### E3. Which renderer supports compiled effects, and behind which CMake option

Source of truth: `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake` (option lines), each renderer's `SupportsCompiledEffects()` (guarded by the same macro), and a conformance test file per family (all built on `tests/support/CNA/TestSupport/CompiledEffectConformance.hpp`). **Every option defaults OFF except FNA3D, which has no option.** With the option OFF the renderer reports `false` and `Effect(device, bytes)` throws `NotSupportedException`.

| Identity (family) | `CompiledEffects` | CMake option (`cmake/RendererSelection.cmake`) | Capability impl (guard macro) | Conformance tests | Added in BASE..TARGET? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `FNA3D` | **true, always** | none (`cna_configure_fna3d()` `:539`) | `modules/renderers/fna3d/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Fna3d/Fna3dRenderer.hpp:1394`; `Fna3dRenderer.cpp:982-983` | `.../fna3d/tests/.../Fna3dCompiledEffectTests.cpp` | no (BASE) |
| `OPENGLES2`, `OPENGLES3`, `OPENGL33`, `WEBGL1`, `WEBGL2` (EasyGL) | true iff option | `CNA_EASYGL_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:310-316` | `EasyGLRenderer.hpp:1771-1797` (`#if defined(CNA_EASYGL_COMPILED_EFFECTS)`) | `.../easygl/tests/.../EasyGLCompiledEffectTests.cpp` | BASE (opt-in) |
| `SDL_GPU` | true iff option | `CNA_SDL_GPU_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:495-504` | `SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:2807-2832` | `SdlGpuCompiledEffectTests.cpp` | BASE (opt-in) |
| `VULKAN` | true iff option | `CNA_VULKAN_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:331-337` | `VulkanRenderer.hpp:2782-2796` | `VulkanCompiledEffectTests.cpp` | BASE (opt-in) |
| `WEBGPU` | true iff option | `CNA_WEBGPU_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:352-364` (buildable on every target incl. Emscripten) | `WebGPURenderer.hpp:1857-1867` | `WebGPUCompiledEffectTests.cpp`, `webgpu_browser_compiled_effect_test.cpp` | **new** (`d39ac0362`, 2026-09-06) |
| `DIRECTX11` | true iff option | `CNA_DIRECTX11_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:401-407` | `DirectX11Renderer.hpp:465-471` | `DirectX11CompiledEffectTests.cpp` | **new** (`ef3df83cf`, 2026-09-09) |
| `DIRECTX12` | true iff option | `CNA_DIRECTX12_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:416-422` | `DirectX12Renderer.hpp:327-332`; `.cpp:2899-2900` | `DirectX12CompiledEffectTests.cpp` | **new** (`13cba6d28`, 2026-09-10) |
| `DIRECTX9` | true iff option | `CNA_DIRECTX9_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:455-461` | `DirectX9Renderer.hpp:282-288` | `DirectX9CompiledEffectTests.cpp` | **new** (`4e55e1c25`, 2026-09-10) |
| `SOFTWARE` | true iff option **and** not the GDI 2D-only build | `CNA_SOFTWARE_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:380-386` | `modules/renderers/software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:429-437` | `SoftwareCompiledEffectConformanceTests.cpp` | **new** (`3ecaf141e`, 2026-09-11) |
| `OPENGL4` | true iff option | `CNA_OPENGL4_COMPILED_EFFECTS` `:519-525` | `OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:1049-1075` | `OpenGL4CompiledEffectTests.cpp` | **new** (`d02bda259`, 2026-09-24) |
| `METAL` | false | none | no `CreateCompiledEffect` override (grep of `modules/renderers/metal` finds no compiled-effect code) | none | - (plan `FX-066`, needs macOS) |
| `HEADLESS`, `STUB`, `PORTABLEGL`, `SDL_RENDERER`, `DIRECT2D`, `CANVAS`, `HTML_DOM`, `FREEDIRECT`, `GDI`, `SVG_DOM` | false | none | inherit `IGraphicsRenderer::SupportsCompiledEffects()` = false (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3055`) | - | - |

Counts: at BASE 4 families (FNA3D + 3 opt-in = 8 identities: FNA3D, five EasyGL, SDL_GPU, VULKAN); at TARGET **10 families = 14 of the 25 identities** can report true (1 unconditional + 13 behind an option), 11 identities never can (METAL + 10 above). `plans/plan_fx.md` (status banner) and `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md` §10 lag the code: they list seven backends and omit WEBGPU, SOFTWARE and OPENGL4; `plans/plan_fx.md:1311` still classes SOFTWARE as "unsupported by design". The option definitions and `SupportsCompiledEffects()` overrides are the truth.

**Renderer-specific refusals that are declared in code** (an executed pixel gate exists per family, but this audit did not run any test - it read the code and the tests' registration):
- Vertex-stage texture sampling from a compiled effect: refused by name on Vulkan (`modules/renderers/vulkan/src/VulkanCompiledEffect.cpp:709-711`), WebGPU (`WebGPUCompiledEffect.cpp:775`), SDL_GPU/EasyGL (plan `FX-109`, refusal text), D3D11 binds vertex samplers via its adapter but has no differential oracle (plan). Renderer-wide: no CNA renderer routes `GraphicsDevice.VertexTextures` at all.
- Volume (`Texture3D`) pixel sampler: supported on Vulkan (`VulkanCompiledEffect.cpp:781-783` `IVulkanVolumeSamplable`; the header comment at `VulkanRenderer.hpp:2790` still says it is refused - stale comment), refused on SDL_GPU (SDL_GPU samples `Texture3D` nowhere) and on EasyGL's GLSL ES profiles (MojoShader emits `sampler3D` without precision). A texture whose dimension differs from the declared sampler is refused by name at draw.
- EasyGL only: GL-context recreation invalidates a live effect (retained bytecode rebuild is implemented per plan `FX-107`); sampling a `RenderTarget2D` being drawn into is refused; on ES2/WebGL1 correcting a render target's row order needs `glBlitFramebuffer` and is refused; `MipMapLevelOfDetailBias` unrepresentable on GL ES; `AddressW` only ES3+ (`plans/plan_fx.md:1360-1400`, `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md:150-270` - CNA docs, orientation).
- SDL_GPU: `SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:2807-2832` says multi-stream and instanced compiled draws are implemented; the plan text that says SDL_GPU has no instancing is older. `MultiStreamVertexInput` is `true` in `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2684` at TARGET (BASE: renderer-wide false, `BASE:modules/renderers/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:5352` comment).
- `DIRECTX9`: one renderer-wide multi-stream case skipped in the shared suite (plan `FX-070`).

**BASE vs TARGET (what the site says vs truth)**:

| Topic | Site (BASE, alpha.1) | TARGET truth |
|---|---|---|
| Renderers with compiled effects | FNA3D default; EasyGL, SDL_GPU, Vulkan opt-in; "four renderer configurations" (`docs/effects.html`) | FNA3D always; opt-in EasyGL family (5 identities), SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU, OPENGL4, SOFTWARE, DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 = 14 of 25 identities; METAL and the 2D/no-pixel renderers never |
| `Effect(GraphicsDevice&, bytes)` | works on qualified builds | unchanged behaviour; same exception taxonomy (E2) |
| HLSL `.fx` source | "not accepted; CNA embeds no HLSL compiler" | Runtime: still not accepted. **Build time: `cna-content build ... --format xnb` compiles `.fx` via an external legacy `fxc` (fx_2_0), optionally under Wine**; `.fxb` route needs no compiler; DXBC and MGFX still rejected |
| XNB authoring | "CNA does not author XNB files" | An XNB writer exists (`XnbEffectWriter`, texture/model/etc. writers under `modules/content/src/Xnb/XnbAssetTypeWriters.cpp`; `cna-content --format xnb`). Cross-reference the content fact sheet. |
| SDL_GPU "Vulkan-only device today" | as stated | SPIR-V native (Vulkan), plus D3D12/Metal devices through SDL_shadercross when `CNA_SDL_GPU_SHADERCROSS` is ON (default ON on Windows/Apple) |
| `ShaderEffect` renderers | "EasyGL and Vulkan are the verified paths" | see E4 |
| "[retired]/..." effect rows | present | those renderers are removed (E5) |

### E4. Custom-effect authoring paths at TARGET

| Path | API | Input | Capability | Renderers (code-derived, see fact-sheet section 'Advanced graphics') |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stock effects | `BasicEffect` ... | none | ThreeD (2D renderers throw) | every 3D renderer |
| Compiled XNA effect | `Effect(device, bytes)`, XNB `EffectReader` | Effect Framework 9.1 binary (+XNA 4 wrapper) | `CompiledEffects` | E3 |
| `ShaderEffect(device, vertSrc, fragSrc)` | CNAEXT, always compiled | renderer-native text: GLSL desktop / GLSL ES (EasyGL, OPENGL4), SPIR-V words (Vulkan, SDL_GPU), WGSL (WebGPU), HLSL (DX9/11/12 compile with `D3DCompile`) | `CustomEffects` **and** `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` to actually run source | EasyGL family, OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, [retired renderers] (+DIRECTX9 compiles HLSL although it reports `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT=false`), SDL_GPU reports `CustomEffects` true only in builds that found libshaderc (Linux/Android, `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2688-2693`; GLSL text is then compiled at run time, SPIR-V words are accepted by the typed route whenever a device exists: `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2938-2954`); FNA3D/METAL/PORTABLEGL report `CustomEffects=false`; SOFTWARE/HEADLESS report true but never run the source (`ShaderEffect.hpp:82-95`, `PostProcessPass.cpp:11-21`) |
| `.cnj` `"type":"Effect"` | `Load<std::shared_ptr<Effect>>` returns a `ShaderEffect` | JSON with `vertex`/`fragment` sidecar files (`modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:1078-1108`); also stock-effect types `BasicEffect`, `AlphaTestEffect`, `DualTextureEffect`, `EnvironmentMapEffect`, `SkinnedEffect` (`:1074-1077`) | CustomEffects | as above |
| Shader code / package | `CNA::Graphics::ShaderCodeEXT`, `ShaderPackageEXT`, `ShaderEffect(device, package)`, `ShaderEffectFactory` (need `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`) | multi-language package (GlslDesktop, GlslEs, SpirV, Wgsl payloads; `Hlsl/Msl/Dxil` enum values exist but no engine package ships them) selected per live device via `GraphicsDevice::SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(language, stage)` | per language/stage | `ShaderEffect.hpp:60-72`; `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/ShaderPackageEXT.hpp`; offline generator `tools/shader_package/README.md` |
| Compute | `CNA::Graphics::ComputeShader`, `StorageBuffer`, `StorageTexture2D`, `ConstantBuffer`, `GpuTimer` (gated) | source per language | `ComputeShaders` (EasyGL: runtime GL >= 4.3 / ES >= 3.1, never WebGL: `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6798-6806`) | see capability matrix |
| Fullscreen pass over an `Effect` | `CNA::Graphics::EffectPass`, `PostProcessChain`, `RenderPipeline::addUserPass` (gated) | any `Effect` incl. compiled | CustomEffects/CompiledEffects of the effect used | engine layer |

### E5. The samples claim (CNA-side facts only)

- "Effect bytecode is the single largest XNA gap" is the wording of `plan.md:268` in this website repo (not of a published page). Published callouts saying the same thing: `demos.html` "Why 23 samples do not build" and `showcase.html` ("they ship custom .fx shaders, and CNA cannot compile HLSL bytecode ... Close it and most of them come along"). At TARGET the CNA side of that claim is stale in two ways: (1) compiled effect bytecode (`.fxb` / XNB `Effect`, the thing XNA samples actually ship after their content build) loads on 14 identities (E3) - the blockage is no longer "CNA does not support compiled .fx bytecode"; (2) `.fx` **source** can be built to an `.xnb` at build time through `cna-content` + external legacy fxc (E2).
- CNA-side evidence that shader-driven XNA samples are being exercised through this path (commit subjects in `BASE..TARGET`, untrusted narrative but real commits): `b718f950a` SAMPLE-028 (ColorReplacementSample_4_0 depth), `23fb7de64` SAMPLE-031 (render-target row order; BloomSample_4_0), `e6836cdc8` FX-121 (NormalMappingSample_4_0, fragment `highp`), `ab79b22d5` FX-122 (PerPixelLightingSample_4_0, vertex-colour clamp), `6a85149e2` SAMPLE-053 (external XNB resource types), and the SAMPLE-014 note at the top of `plans/plan_fx.md` (Spacewar Evolved `ship.fx` on WEBGL2 and OPENGLES3). Whether the sample repository's count of 23 non-building samples changed is a `cna-samples` fact and is **not derivable from CNA source** (open question O-2).


## Advanced graphics facts

Sections A-G below come from the per-renderer capability-matrix work (code-derived: `SupportsCapability` switches, dedicated hooks, format classifiers, descriptors and tests). Section H collects feature semantics found while auditing tutorials 31-70. Renderer cells describe what the code declares for a **HiDef** device unless stated (the default profile is Reach).


All paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`). Evidence order used: renderer
implementation (SupportsCapability switches, dedicated hooks, format classifiers) -> tests/snapshots
-> docs (only to orient). Nothing was compiled or run; a cell says what the code declares, not what a
GPU measured, except where a checked-in test snapshot is cited.

Renderer set at TARGET: 25 public identities over 21 families (`cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-43`,
`docs/removed-renderers.md`; families = directories under `modules/renderers/` except `common`).
`[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired renderers]`, `[retired]/2`, `OPEN[retired]`,
`[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]`, `[retired]` no longer exist.

### A. Capability matrix

#### A.0 How the public answer is derived

`GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability` (`modules/graphics/src/Xna/GraphicsDevice.cpp:3403-3438`) does NOT
simply forward to the renderer switch. Seven of the 19 members are answered by dedicated hooks and the
renderer's own switch is never asked for them:

| Member | Answered by | Line |
|---|---|---|
| `CompiledEffects` | `IGraphicsRenderer::SupportsCompiledEffects()` (base default false, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3055`) | GraphicsDevice.cpp:3408 |
| `FloatRenderTargets` | `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT(Vector4)` = profile rule AND `ClassifyRenderTargetFormatEXT` (Defer means "Color only" => false) | :3416, :3784-3805 |
| `HalfFloatRenderTargets` | same, format `HdrBlendable` | :3418 |
| `HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering` | `SupportsHalfFloatTextureLinearFilteringEXT()` (base default false, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2999`) | :3422 |
| `ComputeShaders` | `SupportsComputeShadersEXT()` (default false, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3129`) | :3425 |
| `IndirectDraw` | `SupportsIndirectDrawEXT()` (default false, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3136`) | :3428 |
| `MultipleRenderTargets` | renderer switch AND `GetMaxRenderTargetsForProfileEXT(profile) > 1` (default: Reach 1, HiDef 4, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2655-2658`) | :3433-3437 |

Consequences worth stating on the site:

* **Default GraphicsProfile is Reach** (`modules/runtime/src/ProjectGraphicsProfile.cpp:12`,
  `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:52`; `CNA::SetProjectGraphicsProfileEXT` / `GraphicsDeviceManager::setGraphicsProfileProperty`).
  On a Reach device `MultipleRenderTargets` is **false on every renderer**, float/half-float render targets
  are refused (Reach excludes 11 formats, `graphics/src/Xna/Texture.cpp:110-146`) and `Texture3D` volume
  extent is 0 (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2641-2646`). Cells below are the answer for a **HiDef** device.
* The base default (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3775-3800`) is true for everything except StencilBuffer
  (delegates to `SupportsStencilBuffer()` -> `SupportsDepthStencil()`, default true, `:3410`),
  MultiStreamVertexInput (false), CompiledEffects (opt-in) and the float entries (false). A renderer with
  no override inherits it. **DIRECTX9 has no capability code at all** (`grep GraphicsCapability
  modules/renderers/directx9` is empty), so its row is the inherited default, not a measured device answer.
* The renderer switches of SDL_GPU and OPENGL4 return `false` for the derived members
  (`sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2710-2715`, `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1563-1572`) while the
  public `GraphicsDevice` answer is `true`/conditional. Only a caller holding an `IGraphicsRenderer*`
  directly (C ABI internals, renderer tests) would see the switch value.
* `RendererCapabilityProfile` (`graphics/include/CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:25-92`) has **32**
  features (not the "30" in `docs/renderer-capability-profiles.md:14`); the 19 legacy entries map 1:1
  (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3493-3560`). The C ABI carries all 19 (`c-api/src/CnaCApiGraphics.cpp:196-272`).

#### A.1 GraphicsCapability x 25 identities

Legend: **Y** always true in the code path; **n** false; **c** conditional (condition in A.3). HiDef profile
assumed for MRT / float RTs. Column key: 3D=ThreeD, DS=DepthStencilBuffer, MSAA=MultiSampleAntiAliasing,
MRT=MultipleRenderTargets, Anis=AnisotropicFiltering, Wire=WireFrame, Occl=OcclusionQuery, Cust=CustomEffects,
T3D=Texture3D (storage only), MStr=MultiStreamVertexInput, Inst=Instancing, Sten=StencilBuffer,
AddB=AdditiveBlending, CmpFX=CompiledEffects, F32/F16=Float/HalfFloatRenderTargets, F16L=HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering,
Cmpt=ComputeShaders, Indir=IndirectDraw.

| Identity (family) | 3D | DS | MSAA | MRT | Anis | Wire | Occl | Cust | T3D | MStr | Inst | Sten | AddB | CmpFX | F32 | F16 | F16L | Cmpt | Indir |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SDL_RENDERER (sdl-renderer) | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | n | Y | n | n | n | n | n | n |
| OPENGLES2 (EasyGL) | Y | Y | n | n | c | c | n | Y | n | n | n | Y | Y | c | n | n | c | c | c |
| OPENGLES3 (EasyGL) | Y | Y | c | Y | c | c | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | c | c | c | Y | c | c |
| OPENGL33 (EasyGL) | Y | Y | c | Y | c | c | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | Y | c | c | c | Y | c | c |

Evidence per row (switch location; `Y` cells in a switch `default:` arm are "inherited true"):

* SDL_RENDERER `sdl-renderer/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/SdlRenderer/SdlRenderer.hpp:196-200` (only AdditiveBlending).
* EasyGL five identities `easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6118-6203` (`default: return true`; ES2-generation = OPENGLES2 + WEBGL1,
  `easygl/include/.../GlProfile.hpp:141-169`; hooks at :6581-6771 classifiers, :6773 dialect, :6799 compute, :6811 indirect,
  :6826 timer, :6852 compute-image, :7016 half-float filtering; header :2136-2148).
* VULKAN `vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3042-3148` (exhaustive, no `default:`; comment :3045-3058); checked-in measured snapshot on llvmpipe, 2026-09-10,
  `vulkan/examples/vulkan_capability_snapshot_test.cpp:110-155`.
* WEBGPU `webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:10707-10754` (falls through to base default; MultiStream true :10738; MSAA = 4x probe :10751-10752).
* HEADLESS `headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-741`.
* SOFTWARE `software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:438-481` (Custom false :454-458; MRT :465-469; F32/F16 true :470-474; everything else default true);
  half-float linear true :424-427; compiled effects `#if CNA_SOFTWARE_COMPILED_EFFECTS && !CNA_SOFTWARE_2D_ONLY` :429-436.
* STUB `stub/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Stub/StubRenderer.hpp:175-178` (always false).
* DIRECTX11 `directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1293-1326`; DIRECTX12 `directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2877-2909` (Compute/Indirect are not overridden => base false).
* DIRECT2D `direct2d/src/Direct2DRenderer.cpp:324-348` (only Anis). CANVAS `canvas/include/.../CanvasRenderer.hpp:93-98`. FREEDIRECT
  `freedirect/include/.../FreeDirectRenderer.hpp:177-181`. HTML_DOM `html-dom/src/HtmlDomRenderer.cpp:731-743` and SVG_DOM
  `svg-dom/src/SvgDomRenderer.cpp:777-789` (AdditiveBlending only when `__EMSCRIPTEN__` and the browser has `plus-lighter`).
* DIRECTX9: no override; inherited `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3775-3800`; `MaxRenderTargetsForProfileEXT` `directx9/src/DirectX9Renderer.cpp:1598`.
* SDL_GPU `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2676-2718`; hooks in `sdl-gpu/include/.../SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:2832 (compiled), 2859-2880 (depth/stencil), 2981 (compute), 2996 (half float), 3052 (indirect)`.
* OPENGL4 `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1538-1572`; hooks `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Modern.cpp:909 (compute), 1265 (timer), 1304 (indirect), 1309 (base instance)`, `OpenGL4Formats.cpp:946` (half float, always true).
* GDI `gdi/src/GdiRenderer.cpp:881-890`. METAL `metal/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Metal/MetalPolicy.hpp:171-190` (13 members enumerated; the six appended members fall to `return false`).
* FNA3D `fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:971-1002` (CustomEffects false :979-981; Compiled :982; Instancing :988; MultiStream true :994; MSAA :997; Stencil :1000).
* PORTABLEGL `portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1679-1704` (only 3D, DS, Stencil, AddB, Wire; reasons in the comment).

#### A.2 Compiled-effect option dependency (CmpFX column)

`c` in CmpFX means "true only in a build configured with the family's opt-in (all default OFF)":
EasyGL x5 `CNA_EASYGL_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, SDL_GPU `CNA_SDL_GPU_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, VULKAN `CNA_VULKAN_COMPILED_EFFECTS`,
WEBGPU `CNA_WEBGPU_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, SOFTWARE `CNA_SOFTWARE_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, DIRECTX9 `CNA_DIRECTX9_COMPILED_EFFECTS`,
DIRECTX11 `CNA_DIRECTX11_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, DIRECTX12 `CNA_DIRECTX12_COMPILED_EFFECTS`, OPENGL4 `CNA_OPENGL4_COMPILED_EFFECTS`
(`cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:308-525`; the `SupportsCompiledEffects() { return true; }` overrides sit inside
`#if defined(CNA_*_COMPILED_EFFECTS)` in each renderer header, e.g. `directx12/.../DirectX12Renderer.hpp:327-332`,
`webgpu/.../WebGPURenderer.hpp:1857-1867`). FNA3D is unconditional (`fna3d/.../Fna3dRenderer.hpp:1394`). METAL, PORTABLEGL, HEADLESS,
STUB, every 2D-only renderer: no override => false (grep `CompiledEffect` in `metal/`, `portablegl/`, `headless/`, `stub/` finds no runtime).
Ten families therefore have a compiled-effect runtime (FNA3D + nine opt-in). **The 2D-only SOFTWARE archive linked into GDI never has it** (`!CNA_SOFTWARE_2D_ONLY`).

#### A.3 Conditions behind every `c`

* **EasyGL** (a runtime GL context decides, not the profile alone): MSAA `GL_MAX_SAMPLES>1`, false on ES2 generation (:6122-6137);
  Anis needs `GL_EXT_texture_filter_anisotropic` (:6139-6140); WireFrame needs a native polygon-mode API (:6141-6145);
  F32/F16 RT = 1x1 framebuffer-completeness probe, always false on ES2 generation (:7040-7075); F16L on ES2 needs
  `GL_OES_texture_half_float_linear` (:7016-7024), true elsewhere; Compute ES>=3.1 / GL>=4.3, never WebGL (:6799-6808); IndirectDraw ES>=3.1 / GL>=4.0,
  never WebGL (:6811-6822); MRT/Occl/T3D/Inst/MStr false on ES2 generation (:6146-6199). OcclusionQuery is exact (`GL_SAMPLES_PASSED`) on desktop
  and 0/1 only via `GL_ANY_SAMPLES_PASSED` on ES/WebGL 2 (`PixelCountIsPreciseEXT`, :2812-2815).
* **VULKAN** physical-device properties: MSAA = colour AND depth sample masks (:3081-3093); MRT `maxColorAttachments>1` (:3064-3067); Anis/Wire from device features
  (:3060-3063); Stencil = chosen depth format has stencil (:3072-3080); F32/F16 per-format image/format properties
  (`ClassifyRenderTargetFormatEXT` :3596-3622); F16L linear-filter bit (:3624-3635); Compute needs compute queue, storage-buffer limits (:18216-18228);
  IndirectDraw needs `drawIndirectFirstInstance` (:21726-21730). Snapshot (llvmpipe) records all these `true` (`vulkan_capability_snapshot_test.cpp:113-131`).
* **WEBGPU**: MSAA `Supports4xMsaa()` scratch-texture probe (:10751-10752, `Supports4xMsaa` :12149); F32/F16 render-target probe (:10812-10832); IndirectDraw needs `IndirectFirstInstance`
  (`WebGPURenderer.hpp:2150-2153`). F16L is **not overridden => false** although the WebGPU spec allows filtering `rgba16float` (grep of `webgpu/` finds no override).
* **SDL_GPU**: DS = `depthStencilFormat_ != INVALID` (:2694); Stencil = `backbufferHasStencil_` (:2696); MSAA = any 2/4/8x survives `SDL_GPUTextureSupportsSampleCount`
  (:2698-2703, ClampSampleCount :1589-1608); **Cust true only if the build found libshaderc** (`sdl-gpu/CMakeLists.txt:38-62`; never on Windows/Apple/Emscripten); F32/F16
  `SDL_GPUTextureSupportsFormat` (:4542-4557); F16L = HalfVector4 renderable (:2790-2799).
* **OPENGL4**: Anis `maxAnisotropy_>1` (:1548); Compute needs GL>=4.3 core + native compute + SSBO (`OpenGL4Modern.cpp:909-918`); Indirect/BaseInstance native flags
  (:1304-1312); F32/F16 RT probe (`OpenGL4Formats.cpp:908-927`).
* **[retired renderers]**: MSAA = `ClampBackBufferMultiSampleCount(device, R8G8B8A8, 4) > 1` (`DirectX11Renderer.cpp:89-105`, :1312-1314); F32/F16 RT via
  `CheckFormatSupport` (TEXTURE2D+RENDER_TARGET+SHADER_SAMPLE) (:1257-1274; DX12 :2842-2860); F16L = `R16G16B16A16_FLOAT` SHADER_SAMPLE (:1357-1363; DX12 :2833-2840).
* **FNA3D**: MSAA `FNA3D_GetMaxMultiSampleCount(...,4)>1`, Instancing `FNA3D_SupportsHardwareInstancing` (Instancing :988; MSAA :997) - answer depends on which FNA3D driver was selected at run time.
* **HTML_DOM / SVG_DOM**: AdditiveBlending only in an Emscripten build whose browser exposes `plus-lighter` (`HtmlDomRenderer.cpp:734-738`).

#### A.4 Detailed-profile features (`RendererFeature`) that are not legacy capabilities

Built in `GraphicsDevice::BuildRendererCapabilityProfileEXT` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3480-3690`) from renderer hooks whose base default is false
(`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3109-3160, 3200`). Only these families override them (grep of `override` in every renderer header/`.mm`):

| Renderer | ShaderEffectSourceExecution | Dialect declared | ShadowSampling | ImageBasedLighting | GpuTimers | Texture3DSampling | BaseInstanceDrawing | ComputeImageBinding |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EasyGL x5 | Y (`EasyGLRenderer.hpp:2136`) | GlslDesktop (OPENGL33) / GlslEs (the other four) (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6773-6777`) | Y (:2137) | Y (:2138) | c: desktop GL>=3.3 or timer ext; ES needs `GL_EXT_disjoint_timer_query`; WebGL never (:6826-6845) | Y (:2142) but AND Texture3D cap, so false on OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 | n | c: desktop GL only (:6852-6857) |
| OPENGL4 | Y (`OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:629`) | GlslDesktop (:615) | Y (:639) | Y (:645) | c: timestamp counter bits (`OpenGL4Modern.cpp:1265-1280`) | Y (:631) | c: native flag (`OpenGL4Modern.cpp:1309`) | c (`:920-925`) |
| VULKAN | **n by design** (base comment `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3097-3108`: takes SPIR-V, not GLSL text; snapshot :155) | SpirV (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:3151-3156`) | Y (`VulkanRenderer.hpp:2884`) | Y (:2876) | c: queue timestamp bits (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:18416-18420`) | Y (:2896) | Y (:4527) | c: compute + storage images (`VulkanRenderer.hpp:4283-4286`) |
| SDL_GPU | Y whenever a device exists (`SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:2928`) | SpirV (:2904-2907) | Y (:3006) | Y (:3014) | n | Y (:2940-2943) | Y (:3054-3057) | Y (:3029-3032) |
| WEBGPU | Y (`WebGPURenderer.hpp:1972`) | Wgsl (:1975-1978) | Y (:2050) | Y (:2061) | c: adapter timestamp-query feature (:2234) | Y (:2133) | n | Y (:2209) |
| DIRECTX11 | Y (`DirectX11Renderer.hpp:167`) | Hlsl (:179-182) | n | n | n | n | n | n |
| DIRECTX12 | Y (`DirectX12Renderer.hpp:151`) | Hlsl (:163-166) | n | n | n | n | n | n |
| everything else (DX9, FNA3D, METAL, SOFTWARE, HEADLESS, STUB, PORTABLEGL, 2D-only) | n (accepts or refuses; source does not drive pixels) | Unknown | n | n | n | n | n | n |

Quirks a documentation writer must not paper over:

1. **Vulkan and SDL_GPU report all six `ShaderDialect*` features Unsupported** even though they execute caller SPIR-V, because the profile only compares the declared dialect with
   GlslDesktop/GlslEs/GlslVulkan/Hlsl/Msl/Wgsl (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3565-3586`); their declared dialect is `SpirV`, which has no feature entry. The truthful query is
   `GraphicsDevice::SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(ShaderLanguageEXT::SpirV, stage)` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3388-3401`). Confirmed by the recorded snapshot
   (`vulkan_capability_snapshot_test.cpp:145-155`).
2. **DIRECTX9 declaration lags implementation.** `CustomEffects` is inherited-true and no dialect / `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT` is declared, so the profile says
   "ShaderEffect objects are accepted, but the supplied source text does not determine rendered pixels" (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3702-3706`), yet `D3D9EffectRenderer::CompileProgram`
   runs `D3DCompile` (vs_2_0/ps_2_0, or _3_0 on HiDef) on the caller's HLSL (`directx9/src/D3D9EffectRenderer.cpp:31-60`) and `D3D9SpriteBatch` executes it
   (`D3D9SpriteBatch.cpp:252-288`). No `DirectX9Renderer.cpp` 3D draw path consumes a custom effect renderer (grep `customEffect` finds only the SpriteBatch). No DX9 capability test exists.
3. The engine-layer capability name table lacks `IndirectDraw` (`graphics-ext/src/RequireCapability.cpp:14-40` lists 18 of 19), so a refusal on that member would print
   "an unrecognised capability".

### B. SurfaceFormat and texture/target formats

**Enum**: `SurfaceFormat` has 27 values, identical to BASE (no diff): the 20 XNA 4.0 values (Color, Bgr565, Bgra5551, Bgra4444, Dxt1, Dxt3, Dxt5, NormalizedByte2,
NormalizedByte4, Rgba1010102, Rg32, Rgba64, Alpha8, Single, Vector2, Vector4, HalfSingle, HalfVector2, HalfVector4, HdrBlendable) plus 7 CNAEXT values
(ColorBgraEXT, ColorSrgbEXT, Dxt5SrgbEXT, Bc7EXT, Bc7SrgbEXT, ByteEXT, UShortEXT) (`graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/SurfaceFormat.hpp`).
`DepthFormat` has 4 values: None, Depth16, Depth24, Depth24Stencil8 (`DepthFormat.hpp`).

**The old site claim "Texture2D is Color-only except on [retired]" is wrong at TARGET, and the truth is a two-gate rule** (`graphics/src/Xna/Texture2D.cpp:105-150`, REMED-GFX-242):

1. **Profile gate (new since BASE, 2026-08-30 `9d7f64fe5`)**: `Texture::IsFormatAllowedByProfileEXT` (`Texture.cpp:110-146`). Reach (the default) refuses Rgba1010102, Rg32, Rgba64, Alpha8, Single, Vector2,
   Vector4, HalfSingle, HalfVector2, HalfVector4, HdrBlendable with `NotSupportedException`; HiDef refuses none of the 20 XNA formats. Cube: never SNORM (`:148-165`).
   Volume: HiDef only, 15 uncompressed formats, no DXT/SNORM (`:167-193`). Render target: never DXT (`:195-202`).
2. **Renderer gate**: `ClassifySurfaceFormatEXT` returns Supported / Unsupported (throws `runtime_error` "has not passed the renderer's promotion gate") / Defer. **Defer falls back to
   `Texture::ValidateFormat`, which admits `Color` only** (`Texture.cpp:204-211`).

At BASE only [retired] and [retired] overrode the classifier (`cna-base/modules/renderers`, grep `ClassifySurfaceFormatEXT`); at TARGET eight families do: DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, EasyGL, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU, VULKAN, SOFTWARE. Per-renderer Texture2D truth:

| Renderer | Texture2D formats accepted (after the profile gate) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 | all 20 XNA formats incl. DXT, asked of the device (`CheckFormatSupport`; B4G4R4A4 etc. optional) | `directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1170-1198`, `directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2767-2789` |
| VULKAN | all 20 XNA formats mapped to VkFormats; DXT1/3/5 need `textureCompressionBC` enabled; Bgra4444 needs `VK_EXT_4444_formats`; then per-format `VkFormatProperties` | `vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3377-3533` |
| SOFTWARE | all 20 supported (CPU storage); cube 18 (no SNORM); RT: Color, Rgba1010102, Rg32, Rgba64 and the 7 float/half formats, but NOT packed16/DXT/SNORM/Alpha8 | `software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:245-274, 276-307, 342-372` |
| OPENGL4 | all 20 supported | `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Formats.cpp:799-832` |
| OPENGLES3 / WEBGL2 / OPENGL33 (EasyGL) | Color, Alpha8, DXT1/3/5 (decoded on CPU if no S3TC), packed16, SNORM, Rgba1010102, float/half; Rg32/Rgba64 only with 16-bit-norm texture ext | `easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6581-6631` |
| OPENGLES2 / WEBGL1 (EasyGL) | Color, Alpha8, DXT only; packed16, SNORM, 1010102, float/half refused; Rg32/Rgba64 need ext | same |
| SDL_GPU | Color, Bgr565, Bgra5551, Bgra4444, Dxt1/3/5, NormalizedByte2/4 supported; Rgba1010102, Rg32, Rgba64, Alpha8 and all float/half **Unsupported** for Texture2D (though float/half render targets exist) | `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:4389-4426` |
| WEBGPU | Color; DXT/BC when the device has `TextureCompressionBC`; NormalizedByte2/4 native; **the three packed 16-bit formats refused by name**; everything else Defer => refused (Color-only rule) | `webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:10859-10878`, text `:11019-11036` |
| FNA3D, DIRECTX9, METAL, PORTABLEGL, HEADLESS, STUB, SDL_RENDERER, GDI, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, SVG_DOM, FREEDIRECT | **Color only** (no classifier override). FNA3D's own driver flags (DXT1/S3TC/BC7) exist but are reachable only through the renderer contract; a public `Texture2D` still refuses non-Color (`fna3d/tests/.../Fna3dSurfaceFormatTests.cpp:5-8`, `fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:322-342`). Metal states it: `MetalPolicy.hpp:197-201` | grep of `Classify*FormatEXT` |

The seven CNAEXT formats (ColorBgraEXT ... UShortEXT) are Defer on every renderer that has a classifier, hence refused for Texture2D; the Vulkan snapshot records every one as
not renderable (`vulkan_capability_snapshot_test.cpp:181-200`). Stale comments still say "only [retired] answers this" (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2698-2704`, `Texture2D.cpp:107-110`, `Fna3dSurfaceFormatTests.cpp:5-8`).

**Render targets**: `RenderTarget2D(..., preferredFormat)` silently selects `Color` when the profile forbids the format or the renderer answers Unsupported/Defer
(`RenderTarget2D.cpp:46-62`) - the caller asks with `GraphicsDevice::SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3784-3805`). The four "HDR" answers therefore
mean: Y on SOFTWARE; device/probe-conditional on DX11, DX12, EasyGL (ES3 generation and desktop), OPENGL4, Vulkan, SDL_GPU, WebGPU; n on FNA3D (has `CreateRenderTarget2DEXT`,
`fna3d/src/Fna3dResources.cpp:1164`, but no classifier so the public query is false), DX9, Metal, PortableGL, Headless, Stub and every 2D-only renderer.

**Texture3D**: capability = storage (`GraphicsCapability.hpp:56-63`); sampling in a custom shader is a separate feature (A.4). Volume formats are classified by DX11, DX12, EasyGL (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6676-6733`),
OPENGL4, Vulkan, Software; other renderers Defer (Color-only). Volume storage: false on ES2/WebGL1 (EasyGL `Texture3D` :6176-6182), HEADLESS (:730-733), PORTABLEGL; true for Metal, DX9 (inherited), SDL_GPU, Vulkan,
WebGPU, DX11/12, OPENGL4, FNA3D (with a run-time read-back probe, `Fna3dRenderer.cpp:381-410`), SOFTWARE ("no classic stock effect samples volume textures", `docs/graphics-renderer-feature-matrix.md:55`).
Volumes are HiDef only: `GetMaxVolumeExtentForProfileEXT` default Reach 0 / HiDef 256 (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2641-2646`).

**TextureCube**: cube classifiers exist for EasyGL (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6633-6674`), OPENGL4, Vulkan (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:3535-3542`), SDL_GPU, WebGPU (`WebGPURenderer.cpp:10880-10893`), Software; the rest are Color-only.
Signed-normalized formats are excluded from cubes at both profiles (`Texture.cpp:148-165`).

**DepthFormat as applied** (what `RenderTarget2D.DepthStencilFormat` reports): Vulkan always creates a backbuffer depth buffer and reports the format it chose even if the game asked for None (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:16544-16556`);
PortableGL always reports Depth24Stencil8 (`PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1027-1030`); DX12 maps the request or reports None (`DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2698-2702`); SDL_GPU normalises to a supported native format (`SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:4207-4216`);
GDI, Direct2D, SVG_DOM report None (`GdiRenderer.hpp:79`, `Direct2DRenderer.hpp:417,554`, `SvgDomRenderer.hpp:96`).

### C. 2D-only renderers

**7 identities have ThreeD == false by design**: SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, FREEDIRECT, GDI, SVG_DOM (A.1 rows; each switch returns false for `ThreeD`). At alpha.1 there were 13; the six removed
are [retired] (`docs/removed-renderers.md`). Add STUB (everything false, all calls no-ops, `StubRenderer.hpp:116-178`) if a writer lists "cannot draw 3D".
PORTABLEGL and METAL are 3D-capable but narrow (below).

Refusal behaviour when 3D is called (`Unsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior::Throw` is the default; `WarnAndStub` is opt-in via `GraphicsDevice::SetUnsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior`,
`graphics/include/CNA/Unsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior.hpp:15-32`, `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3812-3822`; policy plumbing `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3906-3926`):

| Renderer | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, FREEDIRECT | `HandleUnsupported3DCall`: throws `runtime_error("<renderer> does not support 3D: <method>")` under Throw; logs once per method and returns a null-object stub under WarnAndStub (`sdl-renderer/src/SdlRenderer.cpp`, `direct2d/src/Direct2DRenderer.cpp:3143-3155`, `canvas/src/CanvasRenderer.cpp:327-348`, `freedirect/src/FreeDirectRenderer.cpp`) |
| HTML_DOM, SVG_DOM | always throw via `NotYetImplemented("HTML_DOM"/"SVG_DOM", ...)`; **no WarnAndStub path** (`html-dom/src/HtmlDomRenderer.cpp:591-594, 1034-1037`, `svg-dom/src/SvgDomRenderer.cpp:407-410`) |
| GDI | always throws `System::NotSupportedException("GDI (Win32 2D) does not support <operation>.")`; no WarnAndStub path (`gdi/src/GdiRenderer.cpp:216-220`). GDI additionally offers a 2D stencil mask, wireframe SpriteBatch quads and a 4x CPU MSAA backbuffer (`:881-890`) |
| STUB | 3D calls are silent no-ops (no throw, no log) |
| PORTABLEGL (3D-capable) | refuses by name everything but unlit `VertexPositionColor`/`VertexPositionTexture` BasicEffect: second texture, lighting, fog, DualTexture, EnvironmentMap, Skinned, Pbr, compiled custom effect, alpha test, instancing, ColorMatrixEffect (`portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:414-451`) |

Deliberate 2D features on these renderers: `ColorMatrixEffect` (GDI, SOFTWARE CPU SpriteBatch only, `graphics/include/.../ColorMatrixEffect.hpp:16-27`), 2D `SpriteBatch` on all of them.
None of the 7 accepts `CustomEffects`, compiled effects, or any programmable pipeline.

### D. Limits (MSAA / MRT / instancing / streams)

| Item | Truth at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| MRT ceiling | XNA's 4 simultaneous targets is enforced by `GraphicsDevice` for every renderer; profile Reach = 1, HiDef = 4 (default hook) | `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2647-2658`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3433-3437` |
| MRT attachment limit reported | SDL_GPU 4 (`SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:3119`); Vulkan min(4, device) (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:18380-18387`); WebGPU min(4, device) (`WebGPURendererModern.cpp:367-374`); OPENGL4 `maxMrtTargets_` (`OpenGL4Modern.cpp:1010`); Software 4 ordered CPU targets (`SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:465-469`); others use the default | as cited |
| MRT unavailable | Metal, PortableGL, GDI, Stub, all 2D-only, and EasyGL on OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 (no draw-buffers) | A.1 |
| MSAA sample counts | all device-clamped, none has a static declared maximum: SDL_GPU clamps 8->4->2 against colour and depth (`SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:1589-1608`); Vulkan `PickSampleCount` over colour AND depth masks (2..64 mapped, `VulkanRenderer.cpp:365-381`); DX11/12 halve from the request until a quality level exists, the capability probes a request of 4 (`DirectX11Renderer.cpp:89-105`); **WebGPU 4x only** (`WebGPURenderer.cpp:12149, 10751-10752`); **Software 0 or 4 only, other counts rejected** (`SoftwareFramebufferAllocation.cpp:45-49`); GDI 4x CPU opt-in; OPENGL4 "GL 4.x core guarantees GL_MAX_SAMPLES >= 4" (`OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1545`); EasyGL `GL_MAX_SAMPLES` (false on ES2 generation); FNA3D `FNA3D_GetMaxMultiSampleCount` | as cited |
| MSAA unavailable | Metal (`MetalPolicy.hpp:177`), PortableGL (one sample/pixel, `PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1690-1692`), all 2D-only except GDI | |
| Instancing | not on Metal, PortableGL, ES2/WebGL1, 2D-only, Stub; FNA3D by driver query; the engine layer's `InstancedRendererEXT` needs Instancing AND MultiStreamVertexInput (`graphics-ext/src/InstancedRendererEXT.cpp:142-143`) | A.1 |
| SDL_GPU shader-effect instancing | "CNAEXT ShaderEffect instancing is not implemented" (declared limitation text) | `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2956-2963` |
| Multi-stream vertex input | false by default; true on EasyGL (not ES2/WebGL1), OPENGL4, Vulkan, SDL_GPU, WebGPU, DX11, DX12, FNA3D, SOFTWARE; false on DX9 (inherited), Metal, PortableGL, HEADLESS. Max streams: default 16 (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:1648, 3829`), Vulkan = device `maxVertexInputBindings` (16 on the recorded snapshot), WebGPU = min(device-1, `kMaxStockVertexStreamsEXT`) (`WebGPURenderer.cpp:10757-10768`) | `GraphicsCapability.hpp:58-81` |
| Base-instance / first-instance drawing | only SDL_GPU, Vulkan, OPENGL4 (conditional native flag); hook default false | A.4 |
| Max texture dimension | default 16384 (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3842-3845`); overridden by Vulkan, Direct2D, GDI, FNA3D (keeps default) | grep `GetMaxTextureDimension` |
| Occlusion query | absent on SDL_GPU (vendored SDL_gpu 3.5.0 has no occlusion query; declared text), Metal, PortableGL, ES2/WebGL1, 2D-only, Stub. Exact counts: desktop GL, OPENGL4, WebGPU (exact, `webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:10725-10731`), Software (exact), DX11/12/9/FNA3D; 0/1 only on GLES3/WebGL2 (`PixelCountIsPreciseEXT`); Vulkan exactness needs the precise-occlusion feature | `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2956-2963`, `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2812-2815`, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:237-239` |

### E. Declared limitation texts

Only **two** renderers implement `GetAdditionalLimitationsTextEXT()` (grep across `modules/renderers`): SDL_GPU and WebGPU. Every other boundary lives in the capability switch / code comments (one line each below).
The shared report also appends generic text: no-3D pipeline, "ShaderEffect objects are accepted, but the supplied source text does not determine rendered pixels", and compute-without-image-binding
(`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3686-3719`).

* **SDL_GPU** (`SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2956-2963`): all sixteen XNA vertex-stream bindings and four independently writable mixed-format colour targets; ShaderEffect instancing not implemented; OcclusionQuery unavailable (no query API in vendored SDL_gpu 3.5.0).
* **WEBGPU** (`WebGPURenderer.cpp:10996-11085`, composed lazily): Bgr565/Bgra5551/Bgra4444 refused (no 16-bit packed GPU format); NormalizedByte2/4 native (not renderable, not in cubes); `MipMapLevelOfDetailBias` honoured on all stock 3D effects and
  compiled effects but ignored by custom ShaderEffect and by PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect, clamped to about +/-16; `Rgba64` render target unavailable without `TextureFormat16bitNorm`/RENDER_ATTACHMENT; without `Float32Filterable` a filtering sampler on a 32-bit float texture is refused; without `Float32Blendable` a non-opaque blend on a 32-bit float target is refused.
* SDL_RENDERER / DIRECT2D / CANVAS / HTML_DOM / SVG_DOM / FREEDIRECT / GDI: 2D only, no shader stage (A.1, C).
* STUB: nothing renders; every capability false (`StubRenderer.hpp:175-178`).
* HEADLESS: draws are validated and traced, nothing rasterised; Texture3D, AdditiveBlending, MultiStream false ("fabricate success" rationale, `HeadlessRenderer.cpp:730-737`); ShaderEffect accepted, source ignored (base comment `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3097-3103`).
* SOFTWARE: CPU rasteriser; CustomEffects false (accepts source, never executes it, `SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:454-458`); PBR is a "reduced CPU cross-check" (F); MSAA 4x only; compiled effects opt-in.
* PORTABLEGL: unlit BasicEffect subset only (C); no MSAA/MRT/Texture3D/occlusion/anisotropy (`PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1690-1703`).
* METAL: MSAA, MRT, occlusion, custom effects, multi-stream, instancing all false (`MetalPolicy.hpp:177-185`); factor-only PBR (F); Color-only textures; cannot be compiled on this repo's Linux CI.
* FNA3D: CustomEffects false because "FNA3D's only shader entry point is FNA3D_CreateEffect, which takes a compiled Direct3D 9 Effect binary" (`Fna3dRenderer.cpp:973-981`); PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect draws refused (`fna3d/src/Fna3dDraw.cpp:80-120`); instancing by driver.
* DIRECTX9: no declared limitations (inherited defaults; see A.4 quirk 2).
* [retired renderers]: no text; no shadow/IBL sampling, no compute, no GPU timers, no Texture3D shader sampling (A.4).
* EasyGL / OPENGL4 / Vulkan: no text; all conditions are the run-time ones in A.3.

### F. PBR / IBL / shadows

**Who has a real metallic-roughness PBR path** (`PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect`): the source-owned audit of "every renderer directory that mentions `pbrNormalMap`" is exactly nine
(`modules/content/tests/CNA/Internal/GltfImport/GltfRendererPbrFallbackPolicyTests.cpp:108-188` kAudits, `987-1017`, plus SOFTWARE added at :2434-2436):

| Class | Renderers (identities) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **Full PBR, samples both `KHR_materials_specular` maps** (8 families, 12 identities) | DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, EasyGL (OPENGLES2, OPENGLES3, OPENGL33, WEBGL1, WEBGL2), OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU | test `:1374-1376` `sampling{...8...}`; stock HLSL variants `common/d3d/include/.../D3DShaderCache.hpp:68-82` (Pbr3d, Pbr3dDualUv, PbrSkinned3d, PbrSkinned3dDualUv, PbrSkinned3dDualUvColor); DX9 `directx9/src/shaders/cna/Pbr3D.hlsl`, `PbrSkinned3D.hlsl`; Vulkan/SDL_GPU `pbr3d*.glsl`; EasyGL `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:9728, 9804` |
| **Factor-only** | METAL: reads 14 of the 20 PBR draw parameters, none of the six specular-texture inputs, refuses `COLOR_0` vertex-colour PBR draws | test `:1383, 1296-1312, 2441-2442`; `metal/.../MetalSelectPipelineKind.hpp` (Pbr48, SkinnedPbr68) |
| **Reduced CPU cross-check** | SOFTWARE: no BRDF, no lights; a PbrEffect draw comes out as vertexColor x diffuseColor x texture0; consumes only the base-colour map's UV selection/transform | test `:2419-2432` |
| **Refuses PBR draws by name** | FNA3D (`fna3d/src/Fna3dDraw.cpp:80-120`, `RequirePbrShadingSupportEXT`, `VertexColourPbrSupport.hpp:107-123`), PORTABLEGL (`PortableGLRenderer.cpp:436-437`) | |
| No PBR (no pixels / no 3D) | HEADLESS (validates only), STUB, the 7 2D-only renderers | |

At BASE the site/audit counted "16 full families, 14 sampling both maps, Metal/[retired] factor-only" (`audit/alpha1-delta.md`); at TARGET the renderers behind those extra rows ([retired], OPEN[retired]) are gone. `docs/graphics-renderer-feature-matrix.md:90, 105` still says "15-PBR-renderer inventory" / "other eleven PBR implementations".
A future writer should say: 8 full families (12 identities), Metal factor-only, Software reduced.

**Shadow and IBL *receiving*** (always-compiled, not gated by `CNA_CNAEXT`): `IShadowReceiverEXT` on BasicEffect, SkinnedEffect, PbrEffect, SkinnedPbrEffect (`graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/BasicEffect.hpp`, `IShadowReceiverEXT.hpp:1-40`); `ShadowCascadeStateEXT` (up to 4 cascades), `PunctualLightEXT` (point/spot, cube or 2D shadow), `ImageBasedLightEXT` {Irradiance cube, PrefilteredSpecular cube, BrdfLut, PrefilteredMipCount, Intensity} on PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect.
A renderer without a shadow/IBL shader **accepts the state and ignores it** (unshadowed image, no error): `IShadowReceiverEXT.hpp` comment, `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3111-3123`. Honoured only where `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT` / `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT` are true (A.4):
**EasyGL x5, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU** (5 families / 9 identities). Not honoured on [retired renderers] (`grep shadowMap|shadowsEnabled` in `directx9`, `directx11`, `directx12`, `common/d3d`, `metal`, `fna3d`, `software` finds nothing), Metal, FNA3D, Software.

**Shadow / IBL *generation*** (`CNA::Graphics`, `CNA_CNAEXT=ON` only): `ShadowMap` (`graphics-ext/src/ShadowMap.cpp:105-130`), `CascadedShadowMap` (`CascadedShadowMap.cpp:204-215`), `CubeShadowMap`, `SpotShadowMap` need
`ThreeD` AND `CustomEffects` AND a `ShaderPackageEXT` variant that `selectFor(device)` accepts; on failure they log and the frame renders unshadowed (`ShadowMap.cpp:118-130`). `selectFor` requires the renderer to accept the variant's language/stage
(`ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:310-345`, `device.SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`). The engine packages ship only GLSL ES 3.00, desktop GLSL 330 and Vulkan GLSL 450 (compiled to SPIR-V) variants
(`graphics-ext/src/shaders/shadow_caster/package.json`, `directional.es.frag.glsl:1`, `.desktop.` `#version 330 core`, `.vulkan.` `#version 450`; no `.wgsl/.hlsl/.metal` under `graphics-ext`).
Therefore the engine layer's shadow casters (and every package-based pass) can run on: EasyGL (GLSL; ES2/WebGL1 use GLSL ES 1.00 so a `#version 300 es` payload cannot be expected to compile there - not tested here), OPENGL4, VULKAN, and SDL_GPU (only when libshaderc made `CustomEffects` true, and note `selectFor` demands `CustomEffects` for graphics stages, `ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:312-315`);
they cannot select a variant on WEBGPU (WGSL only), DX11/DX12 (HLSL only), DX9, Metal, FNA3D, Software, Headless. `EnvironmentProcessor` (equirect->cube, irradiance, prefilter) is CPU-only and needs no capability (`EnvironmentProcessor.hpp` comment: "has to work on the renderers that have none of them").

`IShadowReceiverEXT` members on Basic/Skinned (BASE-vs-TARGET header diff).

### Flags: places where TARGET docs and code disagree (for the parent's correction table)

1. `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md` section 10 and `docs/shader-effect-vs-fx-bytecode.md` list SDL_GPU, EasyGL, Vulkan, DirectX 11 (+ DX12 in one place) and call DX9 "planned"; the code has opt-in compiled-effect runtimes for **OPENGL4, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE and DIRECTX9 as well** (A.2; `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:352-364, 380-388, 455-462, 519-526`), and `plans/plan_fx.md:8-10` already counts DX9.
2. `docs/renderer-capability-profiles.md:14` says 30 features; code has 32 (`RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:25-92`).
3. `docs/graphics-renderer-feature-matrix.md:90,105` "15-PBR-renderer / other eleven"; source audit has 9 directories (F).
4. Stale "only [retired] classify formats" comments (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2698`, `Texture2D.cpp:107-110`, `Fna3dSurfaceFormatTests.cpp:5-8`); TARGET has eight classifying renderers (B).
5. `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3771-3774` doc says the default is true "except multi-stream input, compiled effects and the float render-target entries"; StencilBuffer, HalfFloatLinear, Compute and IndirectDraw are also non-default (A.0).
6. Vulkan/SDL_GPU report zero `ShaderDialect*` features though they run SPIR-V (A.4 q.1); DX9 declares no source execution though it compiles HLSL for SpriteBatch (A.4 q.2); `RequireCapability.cpp` lacks IndirectDraw (A.4 q.3).


### H. Feature semantics from the tutorial 61-70 audit (profile ceilings, per-renderer MRT/occlusion/MSAA/stencil, buffers)


#### H1. Enforced GraphicsProfile ceilings (renderer-independent, new since BASE except DIRECTX9)
Default profile is Reach (`modules/runtime/src/ProjectGraphicsProfile.cpp:12`, `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:52`; the manager copies it into `gdi` at `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:616`, propagated to an existing device at `:672`). Ceilings live as virtuals on `IGraphicsRenderer` (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2615-2657`), overridable per renderer (only DIRECTX9 overrides: `directx9/src/DirectX9Renderer.cpp:1593-1612`):

| Rule | Reach | HiDef | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Texture2D / RenderTarget2D edge | 2048 | 4096 | `Texture2D.cpp:63-72`, `RenderTarget2D.cpp:65-78` |
| TextureCube / RenderTargetCube edge | 512, power-of-two only | 4096 | `TextureCube.cpp:67-80,110-125`, `RenderTargetCube.cpp:73-88` |
| Texture3D | refused ("does not support volume (3D) textures at all") | 256 per axis; formats limited | `Texture3D.cpp:74-88`, `Texture.cpp:167-192` |
| Simultaneous render targets | 1 | 4 (also global cap 4) | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:5368-5392` |
| 32-bit index buffer / user indices | refused | allowed | `IndexBuffer.cpp:43-48`, `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1295-1305` |
| Primitives per draw | 65,535 | 1,048,575 | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1293` |
| OcclusionQuery construction | refused | allowed if capability | `OcclusionQuery.cpp:18-24` |
| SurfaceFormat for textures/RTs | Color, Bgr565, Bgra5551, Bgra4444, Dxt1/3/5, NormalizedByte2/4 only; float/HDR/Rgba1010102/Rg32/Rgba64/Alpha8 refused (RT request silently becomes Color) | all XNA formats (renderer probes RT support) | `Texture.cpp:110-140,167-201`; `RenderTarget2D.cpp:46-63` |
| Separate alpha blend, SourceAlphaSaturation as destination | refused | allowed | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1327-1345` |
| Vertex/index buffer bytes | 67,108,863 (all profiles) | same | `VertexBuffer.cpp:202-208`, `IndexBuffer.cpp:50-56` |
| `GetBackBufferData` | refused | allowed | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:5146-5149` |
