| METAL | yes | no (throws) | no (count > 1 throws) | n/a | no | yes | no | Color only | `metal/include/.../MetalPolicy.hpp:171-186`, `MetalRenderer.mm:3389-3394,3465-3468` |
| FNA3D | yes | yes (FNA3D query) | default true (up to 4) | supported (descriptor handled) | yes if `FNA3D_GetMaxMultiSampleCount > 1` | `SupportsStencilBuffer()` | **no** (`CustomEffects` false; compiled effects only) | Color only (no classifier) | `fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:971-1002`, `Fna3dResources.cpp:1097-1100,1263-1330` |
| PORTABLEGL | yes | no | no (no render targets at all) | n/a | no | yes | no | n/a | `portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1042-1050,1679-1705` |

Everything false-by-default in the base: MultiStreamVertexInput, CompiledEffects, FloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatRenderTargets (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3775-3800`); `HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering`, `ComputeShaders`, `IndirectDraw` are derived from renderer virtuals whose defaults are false (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3400-3440`).

#### H3. Occlusion query semantics at TARGET
- Construct: NotSupportedException if Reach or capability false or null factory (`OcclusionQuery.cpp:11-27`).
- `Begin()` ObjectDisposed/InvalidOperation rules (`:72-90`); `End()` requires Begin (`:92-103`); `getIsCompleteProperty()` records the observation, returns false before Begin (`:33-45`); `getPixelCountProperty()` throws if not complete (`:52-62`); `isPixelCountPreciseEXT()` (`:64-71`).
- Precision: EasyGL desktop precise, ES3/WebGL2 boolean (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2745-2817`); OPENGL4 always precise (`OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:218`); Vulkan feature-dependent (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:4636-4642,14856,19119`); DX9/11/12/FNA3D/WebGPU/Software real queries.

#### H4. MRT semantics at TARGET
`SetRenderTargets` unchanged-set early-out (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:5346-5366`), max 4 then profile ceiling (`:5368-5392`), null/disposed/other-device checks and RenderTarget2D arraySlice must be 0 (`:5400-5440`); renderer-level checks: matching size and applied sample count, no duplicate subresource, slot 0 owns depth (EasyGL `:7373-7420`, OPENGL4 `:2405-2470`). Stock effects write attachment 0 only; custom ShaderEffect fans out (WebGPU comment `WebGPURenderer.cpp:10711-10716`; `mrt_stock_effect_contract_test.cpp`). `GetMaxColorAttachmentsEXT` public numeric limit via `RendererLimit::MaxColorAttachments`.

#### H5. Multisampling
`PreferMultiSampling` -> request 8 (`GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:606-614`), no `MultiSampleCount` member on `GraphicsDeviceManager` (`GraphicsDeviceManager.hpp:83-245`); `RenderTarget2D` takes `preferredMultiSampleCount` (rounded by `ClosestMSAAPower`, `RenderTarget2D.cpp:30-44`); applied-count write-back only on 6 renderers (F above in tutorial 65 row); MRT MSAA needs matching sample counts.

#### H6. Post-processing / engine layer (CNA_CNAEXT ON required; default OFF `CMakeLists.txt:176`)
`RenderPipeline` degrades to direct-to-backbuffer when no float targets/custom effects (`RenderPipeline.hpp:60-80`); `BloomPass`, `FxaaPass::isSupported()` = `CustomEffects` && shader valid (`FxaaPass.cpp:80-85`); packages carry SPIR-V/WGSL/GLSL payloads (`shaders/post_process/PostProcessShaderPackage.generated.hpp`) selected through `GraphicsDevice::SupportsShaderLanguageEXT` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3378-3396`); dialect per renderer in F2/G3. HDR chain gated by `FloatRenderTargets`/`HalfFloatRenderTargets`. CNAEXT docs' per-renderer matrix (`docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md:210-346`, UNTRUSTED, stale on counts) lists VULKAN/EasyGL/SDL_GPU as fully measured, WEBGPU/DX11/OPENGL4 partially.

#### H7. Buffers, indices, dynamic data
- `IndexElementSize::ThirtyTwoBits` refused on Reach (`IndexBuffer.cpp:43-48`); `CreateIndexBuffer32` base throws "not supported by this renderer" (base default throws runtime_error "32-bit index buffers are not supported by this renderer", `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3525-3531`); implemented by SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU, DX9/11/12, EasyGL, FNA3D, METAL, HEADLESS, SOFTWARE, OPENGL4, PORTABLEGL, STUB; GDI throws (`GdiRenderer.cpp:934-937`).
- `DynamicVertexBuffer` is the only class with `SetDataOptions` overloads (`DynamicVertexBuffer.hpp:96-232`); `BufferUsage::WriteOnly` forbids `GetData` (`VertexBuffer.cpp:100`).
- Per-buffer 67,108,863-byte limit, new since BASE (BASE grep found none).

#### H8. Stencil
Back buffer stencil requires `PreferredDepthStencilFormat = Depth24Stencil8` (default Depth24: `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:58`); `Clear` with Stencil/Depth flags on a plane-less device throws InvalidOperationException (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:747-767`; not in BASE); RT stencil via `DepthFormat::Depth24Stencil8` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:670-710`, `HasRealStencilBuffer`).

#### H9. State-object semantics (G8) and API surface differences discovered
- `RenderTargetBinding(RenderTarget2D*)` implicit, `(RenderTargetCube*, CubeMapFace)`; Texture* forms CNAEXT.
- `SpriteBatch::Begin` state parameters are pointers.
- No `VertexPosition` type; no `GraphicsDeviceManager::setMultiSampleCountProperty`; no `gd.BlendState` member.
- `ShaderEffect` diagnostics: `GetCompileErrorEXT`, `GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT`, `GetSelectedShaderLanguageEXT`.
- `GraphicsDevice::GetShaderDialectEXT()` and `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(language, stage)` (`GraphicsDevice.cpp:3378-3396`).

#### H10. Open questions for the parent
1. Whether the site should document the new HiDef requirement as a site-wide rule ("Graphics profile" prerequisite box) - it affects at least tutorials 61, 62, 67, 68, 70, 64 (cube size), 66 (float RT) and probably 38/39/40, 110-117 (glTF 32-bit indices) handled by other forks.
2. `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` is not overridden on VULKAN (default false) although VULKAN's SPIR-V ShaderEffect runs; the profile's `ShaderEffectSourceExecution` therefore reads false there. Worth a note or a check of `BuildRendererCapabilityProfileEXT` at `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3470-3490` before publishing per-feature tables built on that flag.
3. DIRECTX9 answers were derived from base defaults plus factory code; no Windows/Wine execution was possible here (declared source-only).
4. Cube-face MRT on EasyGL has a code path but no dedicated cube-MRT test was found in `modules/renderers/easygl/examples`; OPENGL4 and VULKAN do have tests.


### I. Facts from the tutorials 31-40 audit


1. **Renderer 3D/2D partition.** 25 public identities. 2D-only (ThreeD capability false, every 3D call throws by default): SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, FREEDIRECT, GDI, SVG_DOM. STUB: all capabilities false. HEADLESS: 3D calls accepted and traced, Texture3D/AdditiveBlending/MultiStream false. Rasterising 3D: OPENGLES2/3, OPENGL33, WEBGL1/2 (EasyGL), OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU, [retired renderers], METAL, FNA3D, PORTABLEGL, SOFTWARE (CPU rasteriser with real MRT (4 targets) and exact occlusion queries, `SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:438-470`). Evidence in X1.
2. **GraphicsProfile enforcement (Reach default)** is new since BASE (2026-09-09, SOFTWARE-206/208/209/219/247). Full list in X5; also `OcclusionQuery` and MRT profile limits (`OcclusionQuery.cpp:22`, `GraphicsDevice.cpp:5387`), `Texture3D` not on Reach (`Texture3D.cpp:81`), vertex declaration profile limits (`VertexDeclaration.cpp:208`).
3. **Bound-resource write rules (new):** `SetData` on a `VertexBuffer`/`IndexBuffer` currently bound, or on a `Texture` bound to a (pixel or, under HiDef, vertex) sampler slot, throws `InvalidOperationException("... resource is in use.")` unless `SetDataOptions::Discard/NoOverwrite` is passed; a `RenderTarget` must be resolved before data transfer. `VertexBuffer.cpp:386-402`, `IndexBuffer.cpp:411-423`, `Texture.cpp:228-254`.
4. **SpriteBatch does not restore device state** (blend, sampler 0, depth-stencil, rasterizer): `SpriteBatch.cpp:169-177`.
5. **Frame loop:** `Game` presents after `Draw` (`Game.cpp:729-734`); no manual `Present()` needed.
6. **Winding convention:** XNA/CNA front face = clockwise; default `CullCounterClockwise`. `PrimitiveType::PointListEXT` is the fifth (CNAEXT) primitive; stock shaders draw 1 px points.
7. **BasicEffect facts:** vertex colour and texture combine (`BasicEffect.cpp:202-215`); `EnableDefaultLighting` XNA rig and ambient (`:468-487`); premultiplies diffuse by alpha (`:303-310`); `DirectionalLight` default ctor is CNAEXT; `setTextureProperty(Texture2D*)` takes a pointer; `SetOwnedTexture(shared_ptr)` CNAEXT.
8. **New always-compiled CNAEXT lighting surface on stock/PBR effects (not gated by CNA_CNAEXT):** `IShadowReceiverEXT` (shadow map, light view-projection, enable, depth bias, filter radius, cascade state `ShadowCascadeStateEXT`) and `setPunctualLightEXT(PunctualLightEXT)` (one Point/Spot light with optional shadow cube / spot map, "one per draw" is a deliberate ceiling) on `BasicEffect`, `SkinnedEffect`, `PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect`; `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect` add `setImageBasedLightEXT`. Files: `BasicEffect.hpp`, `SkinnedEffect.hpp`, `PbrEffect.hpp`, `SkinnedPbrEffect.hpp`, `IShadowReceiverEXT.hpp`, `PunctualLightEXT.hpp`, `ImageBasedLightEXT.hpp`. Shader-side consumers found for punctual light: EasyGL, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, Vulkan, WebGPU. `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT()`/`SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT()` return true only for SDL_GPU, EasyGL, Vulkan, OPENGL4, WEBGPU (false default elsewhere: `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3118-3122`; overrides: `SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:3006,3014`, `EasyGLRenderer.hpp:2137-2138`, `VulkanRenderer.hpp:2876,2884`, `OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:639,645`, `WebGPURenderer.hpp:2050,2061`).
9. **Many lights:** `CNA::Graphics::ClusteredForwardEffect` (`CNA_CNAEXT` only) supports up to `kMaxLightsPerFragment = 128` lights per fragment (`ClusteredForwardEffect.hpp:71`), `isSupported()` runtime query.
10. **ShaderEffect additions since BASE:** `GetCompileErrorEXT`, `GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT`, `GetSelectedShaderLanguageEXT`, `SetUniformVec3Array/Mat4Array`, and (with `CNA_CNAEXT`) `ShaderCodeEXT`/`ShaderPackageEXT` constructors, texture-array and storage-texture binding; `CNA::ShaderLanguageEXT` = Unknown, GlslDesktop, GlslEs, GlslVulkan, Hlsl, Msl, Wgsl, SpirV, Dxil (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/ShaderLanguageEXT.hpp`, `ShaderEffect.hpp:46-72,165`).
11. **glTF -> effect mapping:** metallic-roughness (default) -> `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect`; `KHR_materials_unlit` -> `BasicEffect`/`SkinnedEffect`; a glTF with no lights gets `EnableDefaultLighting()` by import policy (`GltfImportCore.cpp:2919-2935`, `ContentManager.cpp:2383-2420`). `Model::Draw` sets matrices through `IEffectMatrices` (`Model.cpp:433-464`).
12. **Content ladder & tools:** `Load<T>`: `.xnb` -> `.cnb` -> `.cnj` -> reader-declared extensions. New tools `cna-content`, `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnj_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnb_info`; `gltf_to_cnj` unchanged and unconditional (`CMakeLists.txt:391-398`). `cna-content` compiles `.fx` source only through an external fxc-compatible compiler (`fx_2_0`, `--fx-compiler`, `CNA_FXC`, `-DCNA_FXC_EXECUTABLE`, wine launcher), never embeds HLSL; `.fxb` route needs no compiler (`modules/content-pipeline/src/EffectCompilerService.cpp:1-50,960-1000`; `modules/content-pipeline/CMakeLists.txt:104-120`; `docs/content-pipeline.md:412-470` untrusted, matches code). Runtime has no FBX/.x loader.
13. **Texture2D loading:** loose formats `.png .jpg .jpeg .bmp .gif .tga .tif .tiff .qoi`; no KTX/DDS for Texture2D at runtime; loose images have one mip level; no `GenerateMipMaps` API (`ContentManager.cpp:756`, `ImageLoader.cpp`, `Texture2D.hpp:56-91`).
14. **Stock effects at TARGET (unchanged set):** BasicEffect, AlphaTestEffect, DualTextureEffect, EnvironmentMapEffect, SkinnedEffect, SpriteEffect plus CNAEXT PbrEffect, SkinnedPbrEffect, ShaderEffect and CNAEXT `ColorMatrixEffect` (fixed CPU colour matrix for SpriteBatch on GDI/SOFTWARE only, `ColorMatrixEffect.hpp:13-40`, present at BASE too). All stock-effect public APIs are unchanged from BASE except the added CNAEXT shadow/punctual/IBL members (BASE/TARGET header diff).
15. **Mouse:** relative mode is CNAEXT (`Mouse::setIsRelativeMouseModeEXTProperty`), backed by `SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode` on SDL3 (`Sdl3InputServices.cpp:344`); `MouseState` exposes only `getXProperty()/getYProperty()`.
16. **Capability profile API:** `GraphicsDevice::GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()` / `GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT()` with 32 `RendererFeature`s incl. ShadowSampling, ImageBasedLighting, Texture3DSampling, BaseInstanceDrawing (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:25-92`; `GraphicsDevice.hpp:1561,1599`).


### J. Facts from the tutorials 51-60 audit (effects, shaders, shadows, instancing)


Verified against TARGET; items marked (untrusted) were cross-checked in code.

#### J1. Renderer set and 2D-only identities
- 25 public identities: SDL_RENDERER OPENGLES2 OPENGLES3 OPENGL33 WEBGL1 WEBGL2 VULKAN WEBGPU HEADLESS SOFTWARE STUB DIRECTX11 DIRECTX12 DIRECT2D CANVAS HTML_DOM FREEDIRECT DIRECTX9 SDL_GPU OPENGL4 GDI METAL FNA3D SVG_DOM PORTABLEGL (`cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-26`); 21 implementation families.
- Seven 2D-only identities, each `ThreeD == false`: SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, FREEDIRECT, GDI, SVG_DOM (evidence in C1). STUB reports every capability false.
- `IGraphicsRenderer::SupportsCapability` default is true except MultiStreamVertexInput and CompiledEffects (dedicated hooks) and the float RT entries; `GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability` derives CompiledEffects, FloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering, ComputeShaders, IndirectDraw and MRT (also profile-limited) (`IGR:3775-3796`; `GS/GraphicsDevice.cpp:3405-3445`). `GraphicsCapability` has 19 members (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/GraphicsCapability.hpp:14-207`).

#### J2. ShaderEffect (custom source) support matrix at TARGET
`CustomEffects` = capability answer; string ctor payload = what `ShaderEffect(device, vertSrc, fragSrc)` accepts; typed = `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT` (used by ShaderCodeEXT/ShaderPackageEXT ctors); executes = `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()`.

| Renderer | CustomEffects | String-ctor payload | Typed ctors (language) | Executes source (query) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| OPENGLES2, OPENGLES3, OPENGL33, WEBGL1, WEBGL2 (EasyGL) | true | GLSL, verbatim to driver (profile-specific version) | GlslDesktop on OPENGL33, GlslEs on ES/WebGL (compute if GL 4.3/ES 3.1) | true | `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2287-2316,6779-6795`; `EasyGLRenderer.hpp:2136` |
| OPENGL4 | true | GLSL desktop | GlslDesktop | true | `R/opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1555,1577-1585`; `OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:629` |
| VULKAN | true | SPIR-V words only | SpirV | false (SPIR-V, not GLSL text) | `R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3105,3150-3171` |
| SDL_GPU | true only if `CNA_SDL_GPU_SHADER_EFFECTS` (libshaderc found: Linux/Android) | SPIR-V (any build) or GLSL via libshaderc | SpirV | true when device exists | `R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2688-2693,2938-2954,12975-13045`; `R/sdl-gpu/CMakeLists.txt:30-65` |
| WEBGPU | true (default) | WGSL | Wgsl | true | `R/webgpu/src/WebGPURendererModern.cpp:215-222`; `WebGPURenderer.hpp:1972` |
| DIRECTX9 | true (default) | HLSL (D3DCompile SM2/SM3, `main`) | none | false (no override; inconsistent with reality, see Open questions) | `R/directx9/src/D3D9EffectRenderer.cpp:26-52` |
| DIRECTX11 / DIRECTX12 | true | HLSL (vs/ps_5_0, `main`) | none | true | `R/directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1305`; `R/directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2889`; `..Renderer.hpp:167 / :151` |
| METAL | false | throws NotSupportedException | none | false | `R/metal/include/.../MetalPolicy.hpp:182`; `R/metal/src/MetalRenderer.mm:3382-3388` |
| FNA3D | false | null effect renderer (IsEffectValid false) | none | false | `R/fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:979,1025-1029` |
| SOFTWARE | false | accepts, never executes | none | false | `R/software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:454-458` |
| HEADLESS | true (default) | accepts, records, never executes | none | false | `R/headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-741` |
| PORTABLEGL | false | null | none | false | `R/portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1685-1710` |
| GDI, HTML_DOM, SVG_DOM | false | throw | none | false | see 52 table |
| SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, FREEDIRECT, STUB | false | null effect renderer | none | false | headers in C1 |

New query surface since BASE (all on `GraphicsDevice`): `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()`, `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(language, stage)`, `GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT()`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT()`, `DrawInstancedPrimitivesBaseInstanceEXT` (`G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:1491-1638`); `GetShaderDialectEXT()` existed at BASE. `ShaderLanguageEXT` has Unknown, GlslDesktop, GlslEs, GlslVulkan, Hlsl, Msl, Wgsl, SpirV, Dxil; no renderer accepts GlslVulkan/Hlsl/Msl/Dxil in the typed route (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/ShaderLanguageEXT.hpp`).

#### J3. Compiled XNA effects (cross-reference only; the parent owns the matrix)
Opt-in options: EASYGL (5 ids), SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE, OPENGL4, DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 (`cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:308-525`); FNA3D always (`R/fna3d/include/.../Fna3dRenderer.hpp:1394`). `Effect(GraphicsDevice&, bytes)` no longer throws unconditionally: it rejects empty/oversize/MGFX/malformed input, refuses when `CompiledEffects` is false (NotSupportedException), otherwise builds a reflected graph (`GS/Effect.cpp:780-820`). Build-time `.fx` source route via external fxc exists (see 52).

#### J4. Stock effects
- Public API of BasicEffect, AlphaTestEffect, DualTextureEffect, EnvironmentMapEffect, SkinnedEffect is unchanged from BASE except CNAEXT additions: `IShadowReceiverEXT` (BasicEffect, SkinnedEffect, PbrEffect, SkinnedPbrEffect), `ImageBasedLightEXT` on the two PBR effects, protected non-explicit copy constructors, `VertexColorEnabled` accessors on BasicEffect. ColorMatrixEffect (CNAEXT, GDI/SOFTWARE SpriteBatch only) also exists.
- DualTextureEffect: RGB of first texture doubled; independent UV0/UV1; vertex-colour variant works; null textures sample opaque black.
- EnvironmentMapEffect: 3 directional lights, per-vertex clamped Fresnel weight, alpha-channel-driven specular, defaults Amount 1.0, Fresnel 1.0 (0 disables), Specular zero.
- AlphaTestEffect: reference/255 with half-step thresholds (FNA-derived).
- SkinnedEffect: `MaxBones` 72, WeightsPerVertex in {1,2,4}; EasyGL uses a loose `uBones[72]` uniform array.
- `EffectParameterCollection::operator[](int)` changed from reference (BASE) to pointer (TARGET).
- Stock parameters are mostly outputs of property setters; compiled-effect parameters are real reflected storage.

#### J5. Vertex API
- `VertexBuffer::SetData<TVertex>` templates (new since BASE); `SetDataRaw` retained; polymorphic `IVertexType` structs are not trivially copyable and carry a vptr, so custom vertices should be plain PODs plus an explicit `VertexDeclaration(stride, {...})` (`G/VertexBuffer.hpp:388-460,560`; tests cited in 51).
- Seven shipped vertex types; VertexElementFormat 12 values; VertexElementUsage 13 values; declaration limits 255 bytes / 16 elements / usage index 0..15.
- Stock draws resolve inputs from (usage, usageIndex) semantics on the adopting renderers (`StockVertexSemantics.hpp`; included by SDL_GPU, WebGPU, OPENGL4; EasyGL has its own `ConfigureDeclarationForStockProgramEXT` path with `RequireDeclarationFitsStockProgramEXT`) rather than by byte stride alone; a custom ShaderEffect keeps element-index locations.

#### J6. Normal mapping
No stock XNA effect has a normal map. PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect support tangent-space normal maps (`setNormalMapProperty`, `setNormalScaleEXTProperty`) on DX9/11/12, EasyGL, METAL, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU; FNA3D and PORTABLEGL refuse. Vulkan PbrEffect requires vertex stride 48 or 60 (SkinnedPbr 68/76/80; stock SkinnedEffect 52 or 56, `VulkanRenderer.cpp:3233`) (`R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3176-3186`). glTF import synthesises tangents when absent.

#### J7. Shadows
As in 59. Renderers that sample shadow state in stock lit shaders: EasyGL (5 ids), OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU; others ignore it. `ShadowMap::isSupported()` requires `ThreeD` + `CustomEffects` + a usable portable shader package for the device (`GX/src/ShadowMap.cpp:105-135`); caster packages carry GlslEs, GlslDesktop, SpirV and Wgsl variants (`GX/src/ShadowCasterShaderPackages.cpp:75-97`), so SOFTWARE/HEADLESS/D3D/METAL/FNA3D get "shadows unavailable" (logged, frame renders unshadowed). Storage is a colour render target holding light-space distance (Single when available, packed Color otherwise); the engine layer never samples a depth attachment.

#### J8. Instancing
Capability answers per renderer in 60. Key extras: `DrawInstancedPrimitivesBaseInstanceEXT` needs `RendererFeature::BaseInstanceDrawing`; multi-stream per-vertex input needs `MultiStreamVertexInput` (EasyGL except ES 1.00 profiles, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, DX11, DX12, WEBGPU, FNA3D, SOFTWARE; false for DX9 default, METAL, PORTABLEGL, HEADLESS, STUB); SDL_GPU documents ShaderEffect instancing as not implemented and OcclusionQuery as unavailable (`R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2956-2963`).

#### J9. Surface formats and render targets (as they affect these tutorials)
`Texture::ValidateFormat` is Color-only and applies only where the renderer defers; EasyGL (runtime-probed), VULKAN, SDL_GPU, OPENGL4, WEBGPU, DX11, DX12, SOFTWARE classify formats themselves; profile rule (Reach/HiDef) is checked first (`GS/Texture2D.cpp:104-130`). WebGPU documents its own boundaries in `GetAdditionalLimitationsTextEXT` (16-bit packed colour formats refused, Rgba64 RT conditional, Float32Filterable/Blendable conditional) (`R/webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:10996-11080`). SDL_GPU: OcclusionQuery unavailable, four MRT targets.

#### J10. Oracle corpus
`tools/xna-oracle/scenes/*.scene` = 39 at BASE and TARGET (+7 in `null-texture/` at TARGET, from the GSC-0004 fix); only DIRECTX9 has the Wine+DXVK diff CTest.

#### J-open. Open questions from the 51-60 slice

1. DIRECTX9 does not override `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` (reports false) although `CreateEffectRenderer` compiles HLSL via D3DCompile. Confirm intent before the site states which renderers "execute source".
2. Custom ShaderEffect + per-instance stream on [retired renderers] was not verified (only EasyGL, OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU-negative).
3. Tutorial 53: whether `SetValue` on a stock effect's `DiffuseColor` is ignored in every stock effect (BasicEffect path read; others by pattern) needs a pixel test.
4. Tutorial 56: default cube sampler filtering/mip behaviour on each renderer not established.
5. Tutorial 51 GCC "declaration changes meaning" diagnostic and tutorial 60 transpose analysis are derived, not compiled or rendered.
6. Tutorial 55: whether glTF `TEXCOORD_1` can feed a DualTextureEffect (import path builds PBR/Skinned materials) was not traced.
7. Vulkan `SkinnedEffect`/`PbrEffect` stride requirements (48/60, 68/76/80, 52/56) came from runtime messages; the tangent+colour variants were not enumerated.


## CNAEXT

Derived from headers, implementation, CMake and tests by the CNAEXT inventory work; two rows were corrected against the capability matrix where noted. Section 1 = policy and mechanism; 2 = build options; 3 = inventory; 4 = new since BASE; 5 = version/stability; 6 = renderer requirements.


All paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`) unless prefixed `BASE:`. BASE = alpha.1 `1bb2145d`.
Method: headers / implementation / CMake / tests. CNA Markdown used only to orient and is flagged where it disagrees with code.

### 1. Policy and mechanism

1. **Two unrelated things share the name.** (a) The `CNAEXT` *marker macro* and `*EXT` suffix convention; (b) the `CNA_CNAEXT` *CMake option* that gates the `CNA::Graphics` engine layer. Stated by the layer's own master header: `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/CNAEXT.hpp:12-16` ("The whole layer is opt-in ... this gate applies only to the `CNA::Graphics` engine layer -- the `CNAEXT`-marked extension members inside `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::Graphics` (PbrEffect, ShaderEffect, morph targets, ...) are always compiled and are not affected by this option").
2. **The macro.** `modules/core/include/CNA/CNAHelper.hpp:22-26`: `#ifdef CNA_STRICT_XNA_API` then `#define CNAEXT [[deprecated("CNAEXT: not part of the XNA 4.0 API surface")]]`, else `#define CNAEXT` (empty). Documentation/lint marker only; never a compile guard.
3. **CNA_STRICT_XNA_API is a preprocessor define, not a CMake option.** Nothing in CMake declares it (grep of `CMakeLists.txt`, `cmake/*.cmake`: only `target_compile_definitions(... PRIVATE CNA_STRICT_XNA_API)` on two harness targets, `cmake/Harnesses.cmake:197,238`). It is per translation unit; the library is not rebuilt.
4. **Verification harness (positive + negative), Devices/Sensors surface only.**
   - `cna_strict_xna_api_check` compiles `tools/devices/StrictXnaApiSurfaceCheck.cpp` with the define plus `-Werror=deprecated-declarations`, ctest `StrictXnaApiSurfaceCheck_Compile_Run` (`cmake/Harnesses.cmake:186-198,228`; guarded by `CNA_BUILD_TESTS` and GNU/Clang).
   - `cna_strict_xna_api_leak_check` (`EXCLUDE_FROM_ALL`, `tools/devices/StrictXnaApiSurfaceLeakCheck.cpp:31-36` calls `Accelerometer::InjectSyntheticSensorUpdate`, a CNAEXT member) must FAIL to build; ctest `StrictXnaApiSurfaceLeakCheck_MustFailToCompile` with `WILL_FAIL TRUE` (`cmake/Harnesses.cmake:230-249`).
   - **Scope caveat (site-relevant):** both sources include only `Microsoft/Devices/*`, `Microsoft/Devices/Sensors/*` plus Matrix/Quaternion/Vector3/TimeSpan (`tools/devices/StrictXnaApiSurfaceCheck.cpp:33-48`). There is no strict-mode harness over the graphics/content/input XNA surface. Identical at BASE (`BASE:cmake/Harnesses.cmake:184-226`).
5. **Naming convention is a convention, not a rule enforced across the tree.**
   - `EXT` suffix is widely used: `SetDisplayColorSpaceEXT`, `GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT`, `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`, `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`, `DrawIndexedPrimitivesIndirectEXT`, `EnableRealRenderingEXT` (`modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/GraphicsDevice.hpp` CNAEXT members; `modules/gamer-services/.../AvatarRenderer.hpp:165-199`).
   - Counter-examples (CNAEXT-marked, no suffix): `BasicEffect::SetOwnedTexture`, `BasicEffect::GetTypeName`, `BasicEffect::FillGpuDrawParams`, `Effect::Apply()` (`.../Graphics/BasicEffect.hpp`; `Effect.hpp:120` `CNAEXT void Apply();`, doc: "FNA has no public Effect::Apply()"), `ShaderEffect` (class name), `ColorMatrixEffect`. So "any new member on an XNA type gets EXT" (as tutorial 115 states) is not uniformly true.
6. **Namespace rule holds:** engine layer = `CNA::Graphics` (98 headers), device extensions = `CNA::Devices` (17 headers, `modules/devices-ext`), per-object shading extensions = `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::Graphics` (`PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect`, `ShaderEffect`, `ColorMatrixEffect`, `ImageBasedLightEXT`, `AreaLightEXT`, `PunctualLightEXT`, `ShadowCascadeStateEXT`, `IShadowReceiverEXT`, `MorphTargetEXT`, `SkinnedModelEXT`). Cross-cutting value/enum types live directly in `CNA::` (`RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp`, `ShaderLanguageEXT.hpp`, `ShaderDiagnosticEXT.hpp`, `DisplayColorSpace.hpp`, `GraphicsImageAccess.hpp`, `GraphicsMemoryBarrier.hpp`, `IndirectDrawArguments.hpp` under `modules/graphics/include/CNA/`). `CNA::CNAEXT` cannot be a namespace (macro). `CNA::Graphics::VertexTypeRegistryEXT` (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/Graphics/VertexTypeRegistryEXT.hpp:23`) is in the always-compiled graphics module despite the `CNA::Graphics` namespace; note the namespace does NOT imply the `CNA_CNAEXT` gate.
7. **Engine-layer-specific API style differs from the XNA layer** (important for tutorial code): verbs are lowerCamelCase and accessors are `getX()/setX()/isX()`, NOT `getXProperty()`. Rule + enforcement: `scripts/check_cnaext_naming.py:1-15` ("verbs are lowerCamelCase"; every UpperCamelCase function must be a named exception - XNA base-class overrides, System.Object mirrors, `AsciiPostProcessEffect::Draw/GetLastGridDimensions` because C ABI). Examples: `RenderPipeline::getSettings/begin/end/addUserPass` (`.../RenderPipeline.hpp:106-147`), `BloomPass::getThreshold/setThreshold` (`src/BloomPass.cpp:304-305`). The only `...Property()` accessors in `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/` are `EngineException::getSubsystemProperty/getRequirementProperty/getRendererNameProperty` (`EngineException.hpp:55-61`; grep of all 98 headers).
8. **Lint / validators (all new since BASE; none exist at BASE `scripts/`):**
   | ctest | Script | What it enforces |
   |---|---|---|
   | `CNAEXT_GuardDiscipline` | `scripts/check_cnaext_guards.sh` | every production file/header of `modules/graphics-ext` wrapped in `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT ... #endif // CNA_CNAEXT` (runs in EVERY config incl. OFF) |
   | `CNAEXT_NamingRule` | `scripts/check_cnaext_naming.py` | lowerCamelCase verbs in `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/`; stale exemptions fail |
   | `CNAEXT_AccessorConventions` | `scripts/check_cnaext_nodiscard.py` | every `get/is/has` accessor `[[nodiscard]]`; zero-arg getters `const` |
   | `CNAEXT_DoxygenGroup` | `scripts/check_cnaext_doxygen.sh` | one Doxygen module page (`@defgroup cnaext_engine`), no undocumented public member (`docs/Doxyfile.cnaext`, WARN_AS_ERROR); SKIP 77 without doxygen |
   | `CNAEXT_MatrixCompleteness` | `scripts/check_cnaext_matrix.py` | every `GraphicsRendererType` enumerator has a row in `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md` "### Every renderer identity" |
   | `CNAEXT_RendererDescriptorsParse`, `CNAEXT_NoPosixSetenv` | `scripts/check_renderer_descriptors.py`, `scripts/check_no_posix_setenv.py` | unrelated hygiene grouped under the `CnaExt` label |
   Registered in `modules/graphics-ext/examples/CMakeLists.txt:255-330`, under `CNA_BUILD_TESTS` only.
   **No repo-wide "every non-XNA public symbol is marked" validator exists.** `tools/audit_xna_runtime_surface.py` matches `CNAEXT` only as a token when parsing class declarations (`:42`). The generated `docs/xna-4-runtime-member-coverage.md` (100.00% of 3627 documented XNA members represented; generated report, unverified here) is about XNA presence, not extension marking.
9. **CNA_NOXNA / NOXNA spelling is gone from live code.** No macro, option or target uses it. Residual mentions are historical comments (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:368-369`, `modules/graphics-ext/CMakeLists.txt:3-4`), test comments citing `input_noxna.md` (`modules/input/tests/...`), a doxygen `PREDEFINED = CNAEXT= NOXNA= ...` line (`tools/c-api/generate_coverage_inventory.py:422`), root-level planning files (`input_noxna.md`, `noxna_devices.md`) and rename tooling under `modularization/`. The CMake target `cna_noxna` / `CNA::NoXna` survives only as the renamed umbrella `cna_cnaext` / `CNA::CnaExt` (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:374-376`). Same rename already at BASE (`BASE:modules/CMakeLists.txt:163`).

### 2. Build options and gating

| Option | Default | Cited | Gates |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CNA_CNAEXT` | **OFF** | `CMakeLists.txt:176` (BASE:97, unchanged) | `CNA::Graphics` engine layer (`modules/graphics-ext`), a few `ShaderEffect` overloads, C-API engine-layer *implementation* |
| `CNA_DEVICES` | **OFF** | `CMakeLists.txt:177` | `CNA::Devices` (`modules/devices-ext`): Camera, Clipboard, DisplayInfo, FileDialog, Locale, MessageBox, PowerInfo, SystemInfo, SystemTray, UrlLauncher |
| `CNA_STRICT_XNA_API` | not a CMake option; undefined | `CNAHelper.hpp:22` | makes `CNAEXT` `[[deprecated]]` in the consuming TU only |

Mechanics:
- Definitions are attached publicly to `cna_build_config`: `$<$<BOOL:${CNA_CNAEXT}>:CNA_CNAEXT>`, `$<$<BOOL:${CNA_DEVICES}>:CNA_DEVICES>` (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:238-239`), so every consumer of `CNA::BuildConfig` sees them.
- **Both extension modules are always added and always compiled:** `add_subdirectory(devices-ext)` / `add_subdirectory(graphics-ext)` unconditionally (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:360-361`); `graphics-ext` globs `src/*.cpp` (`modules/graphics-ext/CMakeLists.txt:10-13`). With the option OFF each TU compiles to nothing because the file body is inside `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT` / `#ifdef CNA_DEVICES` (enforced by `check_cnaext_guards.sh`). Headers stay includable and empty: a missing type gives "no type named X", not "missing header" (tutorial 115's "trap" is still accurate).
- Umbrella: `cna_cnaext` INTERFACE = `cna_graphics_ext` + `cna_devices_ext`; `CNA::CnaExt` alias (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:368-376`); the runtime umbrella `CNA` lists both extension modules plus `cna_cnaext` (`:384-390`). `cmake/SharedRuntimeLibrary.cmake:145-146` comment says they "exist only with CNA_CNAEXT" - stale (targets always exist), harmless.
- **Always-compiled CNAEXT surface (no `CNA_CNAEXT` needed)** - grep of `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT` in `modules/graphics/{include,src}` hits only `ShaderEffect.hpp` (lines 16, 50, 210, 340, 380). Everything else CNAEXT in the graphics module is unconditional: `PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect`, `ColorMatrixEffect` (GDI/SOFTWARE-only CPU colour transform), `ShaderEffect(device, vertSrc, fragSrc)` (`ShaderEffect.hpp:46-49`), `MorphTargetEXT`, `SkinnedModelEXT`, `AnimationPlayer` + `ModelAnimationsEXT` (`AnimationPlayer.hpp:200`), `Model::getSkinsEXTProperty` (`Model.hpp:285`), `GltfImportReportEXT`, `IShadowReceiverEXT`/`ShadowCascadeStateEXT`/`PunctualLightEXT`/`ImageBasedLightEXT`/`AreaLightEXT` (headers document they are deliberately NOT gated: `ImageBasedLightEXT.hpp:26`, `AreaLightEXT.hpp:35` "an effect's public surface must not depend on a build flag"), the `GraphicsDevice` EXT queries (`SupportsRendererFeatureEXT`, `GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT`, `GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT`, `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`, `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT`, `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT`, `Get/SetDisplayColorSpaceEXT`, indirect/base-instance draws, compute work-group limits), `VertexTypeRegistryEXT`, glTF import in `modules/content`. Instancing (`DrawInstancedPrimitives`) is real XNA 4.0 API (unmarked).
- **`ShaderEffect` split:** basic GLSL-string ctor always available; `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT` adds `ShaderEffect(device, ShaderCodeEXT, ShaderCodeEXT)`, `ShaderEffect(device, ShaderPackageEXT)`, texture-array and storage-texture binding, private portable-payload plumbing (`ShaderEffect.hpp:50-56,210,340,380`). `GetCompileErrorEXT()`, `GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT()`, `GetSelectedShaderLanguageEXT()`, `SetUniformVec3Array/Mat4Array` are unconditional (BASE had none of them).
- **Build-graph placement of engine-layer tests/examples:** tests need `CNA_BUILD_TESTS AND CNA_CNAEXT` (`modules/graphics-ext/examples/CMakeLists.txt:84`); the Effect demos need `CNA_BUILD_EXAMPLES AND CNA_CNAEXT` and renderer `OPENGLES3|OPENGL33|VULKAN` (`:36,59`). The only CMake preset that enables it is `cnaext` (OPENGLES3, Debug, tests on, examples off; `CMakePresets.json:232-247`).
- **CI:** no workflow enables `CNA_CNAEXT` (`grep -rli cnaext .github` hits only `devices-tests.yml`, which is about `CNA_DEVICES`); 20 workflow files at TARGET vs 21 at BASE. The 967 static `TEST*` macros in `modules/graphics-ext/tests` (92 files; grep count) therefore run only on a developer's `cnaext` preset build - the engine layer has no automatic CI at TARGET.
- **Shared library / C API:** `libcna` shared build names both modules when present (`cmake/SharedRuntimeLibrary.cmake:145-149`). The C API keeps ONE exported ABI regardless of `CNA_CNAEXT`: `modules/c-api/include/CNA/C/engine_layer.h:20-35` ("Every declaration here exists in every build ... routes that need a native engine-layer object return `CNA_RESULT_NOT_SUPPORTED` when the layer is absent"); 858 `CNA_C_API` routes in that header alone (`grep -c '^CNA_C_API'`), `CNA_ENGINE_LAYER_VERSION` 18 (`:42`), implementation `modules/c-api/src/CnaCApiEngineLayer.cpp` guarded by `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT`. C ABI at TARGET is **0.29.0** (`modules/c-api/include/CNA/C/abi.h:34-40`); note `docs/cnaext-engine-changelog.md` still says 0.26.0 and `docs/removed-renderers.md` says 0.28.0 (docs stale vs header).

### 3. Inventory (grouped)

Legend: **G** = `#ifdef CNA_CNAEXT`-gated (every header under `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/` is G; verified by scanning all 98 for the guard) ; **U** = always compiled. Requirements come from the subsystem's own `isSupported()/canRaster/canCompile` code. The dominant rule: a shader-based subsystem needs `GraphicsCapability::CustomEffects` **and** a usable `ShaderPackageEXT` selection for the live device (packages ship GlslDesktop, GlslEs, SpirV, WGSL payloads; no HLSL/MSL variant): e.g. `src/ShadowMap.cpp:105-118`, `src/Skybox.cpp:145-150`, `src/BloomPass.cpp:295-302`. The base `PostProcessPass::isSupported` additionally requires `GraphicsDevice::ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` (`src/PostProcessPass.cpp:20-21`). An unsupported pass copies its input through rather than failing (`RenderPipeline.hpp:291`, `EngineException.hpp:25-28`).

#### 3.1 Foundation and pipeline (G)
`CNAEXT.hpp` (master include), `RenderPipeline.hpp` (frame-level HDR+post orchestrator: `begin/end`, skybox, shadow, transparency, statistics, GPU timing), `RenderPipelineSettings.hpp`, `RenderQuality.hpp`, `ShadowQuality.hpp`, `TonemappingMode.hpp`, `PostProcessPass.hpp`, `PostProcessChain.hpp` (ping-pong chain), `PostProcessContext.hpp`, `FullscreenPass.hpp`, `RenderTargetPool.hpp`, `ScopedRenderTarget.hpp`, `EffectPass.hpp` (runs any XNA `Effect` as a fullscreen pass; NB name clashes with `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::Graphics::EffectPass`), `EngineException.hpp`, `EngineLayerVersion.hpp`, `RequireCapability.hpp` (`detail::requireCapability`). Scene format chosen HdrBlendable > Vector4 > Color via `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT` (`src/RenderPipeline.cpp:110-124`); falls back to plain Color if none.

#### 3.2 Post-process passes (G) - `PostProcessPass` subclasses
`BlitPass`, `BloomPass` (extract/blur/upsample/combine, quality presets 2/3/5/7 iterations `src/BloomPass.cpp:~280`), `TonemapPass` (+`TonemappingMode`), `FxaaPass`, `SsaoPass`, `SsrPass`, `DepthOfFieldPass`, `MotionBlurPass`, `ColorGradePass` + `CubeLut` + `LutInterpolation`, `ChromaticAberrationPass`, `FilmGrainPass`, `LensFlarePass`, `HeightFogPass`, `VolumetricFogPass`, `LightShaftPass`, `AerialPerspectivePass`, `SpatialUpscalePass`, `DecalPass`, `ContactShadowPass`, `HdrDisplayOutput` (+ `CNA::DisplayColorSpace`, U), `AutoExposureEXT` (compute), `DepthNormalPrepass` + `DepthEncoding` (needs `MultipleRenderTargets` and `ThreeD`, `src/DepthNormalPrepass.cpp:169,217,501`), `ThinFilmIridescence`, legacy effects that predate the layer and are still in it: `AsciiPostProcessEffect`, `AsciiPass`, `AsciiQuantizeMode`, `CRTEffect`, `CRTMaskType`, `DepthEffect`, `DepthEffectMode`, `DitherMode`. Transparency: `TransparencyMode`, `TransparentDrawList`, `WeightedBlendedTransparency` (needs `MultipleRenderTargets` + `CustomEffects`, `src/WeightedBlendedTransparency.cpp:90-97`). Requirement for all shader passes: `CustomEffects` + package/effect valid (`src/*Pass.cpp` `isSupported`); listed capability greps: `FxaaPass.cpp`, `TonemapPass.cpp:132`, `SsaoPass.cpp:240`, etc.

#### 3.3 Shadows (G, plus U receiver interface)
`ShadowMap` (directional), `CascadedShadowMap` (atlas RenderTarget2D; `ShadowCascadeStateEXT` max cascades in U header), `SpotShadowMap`, `CubeShadowMap` (point light, six faces), `ContactShadowPass` (screen-space), `ClusteredShadowPolicyEXT` (which lights get maps). Requirement: `ThreeD` **and** `CustomEffects` **and** usable caster package (`ShadowMap.cpp:105-118`; same in `CascadedShadowMap.cpp:204-209`). Receiving side is U: `IShadowReceiverEXT` implemented by `BasicEffect`, `SkinnedEffect`, `PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect` (`setShadowMapEXT`, `setLightViewProjectionEXT`, `setShadowCascadesEXT`, `setPunctualLightEXT` ...). Whether a renderer actually samples the state is a separate query: `IGraphicsRenderer::SupportsShadowSamplingEXT()` default false (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3118`).

#### 3.4 Lighting and IBL
G: `DirectionalLightEXT`, `PointLightEXT`, `SpotLightEXT`, clustered forward (`ClusteredForwardEffect`, `ClusteredLightAssignment` (CPU), `ClusteredLightCompute` (GPU, needs `ComputeShaders`, `src/ClusteredLightCompute.cpp:105`), `ClusteredLightGrid`, `ClusteredLightBuffer`, `ClusteredLightEXT`, `ClusteredLightSetEXT`, `ClusteredLightType`, `ClusteredShadowPolicyEXT`), area lights (`AreaLightShading`, `AreaLightBrdfTable`; U type `AreaLightEXT`), light probes (`LightProbeEXT`, `LightProbeVolumeEXT`, `LightProbeBaker`), `EnvironmentProcessor` (equirect->cube, irradiance + prefiltered specular + BRDF LUT). U: `ImageBasedLightEXT` (three split-sum products), `PunctualLightEXT`. `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect` take `setImageBasedLightEXT`. IBL honoured only where `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT()` (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3123`).

#### 3.5 Sky / environment / fog
G: `Skybox` (CustomEffects + package, `Skybox.cpp:145-150`), `AtmosphericSky` (procedural sun sky), `AerialPerspectivePass`, `HeightFogPass`, `VolumetricFogPass`, `LightShaftPass`.

#### 3.6 Materials
G: `PbrMaterial` (7 texture slots `PbrTextureSlot`; 86 changed public-shape lines vs BASE), `PbrMaterialExtensions` (glTF extensions beyond `PbrEffect`), `MaterialBinding` (material -> `PbrEffect`), `GltfMaterialBridge` (imported glTF material -> `PbrMaterial`). U: `PbrEffect`, `SkinnedPbrEffect` (up to 72 bones), `ModelMaterialVariantsEXT` (internal), glTF importer in `modules/content`.

#### 3.7 Geometry throughput
G: `InstancedRendererEXT` (needs `Instancing` AND `MultiStreamVertexInput`, `src/InstancedRendererEXT.cpp:142-143`), `LodGroupEXT`, `FrustumCullerEXT`, `GpuInstanceCuller` (`ComputeShaders` + `IndirectDraw` + `CustomEffects`, `src/GpuInstanceCuller.cpp:111-115`), `ParticleSystem` (`ComputeShaders` + `Instancing` + `CustomEffects`, `src/ParticleSystem.cpp:265-273`), `DebugDraw`, `DebugGizmos`. U: `GraphicsDevice::DrawInstancedPrimitivesBaseInstanceEXT`, `DrawPrimitivesIndirectEXT`, `DrawIndexedPrimitivesIndirectEXT`, `CNA::IndirectDrawArguments` (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/IndirectDrawArguments.hpp`).

#### 3.8 Compute / storage / GPU resources
G: `ComputeShader`, `StorageBuffer` (+`StorageBufferT<T>`, roles incl. Constant), `ConstantBuffer` (`ConstantBufferT<T>`), `StorageTexture2D`, `Texture2DArray`, `GpuTimer`. U: `GraphicsCapability::ComputeShaders`, `IndirectDraw`, `CNA::GraphicsImageAccess`, `CNA::GraphicsMemoryBarrier`, compute work-group limits on `GraphicsDevice`.

#### 3.9 Shader authoring surface
G: `ShaderCodeEXT`, `ShaderPackageEXT` (multi-language package + `selectFor(device)`; binding kinds SampledTexture2D/Cube/3D/2DArray, StorageBuffer, StorageTexture2D, ConstantBuffer), `ShaderEffectFactory` (compile-once-by-name cache), `ShaderDiagnostics` (log). U: `CNA::ShaderLanguageEXT` (Unknown, GlslDesktop, GlslEs, GlslVulkan, Hlsl, Msl, Wgsl, SpirV, Dxil; `modules/graphics/include/CNA/ShaderLanguageEXT.hpp:14-32`), `CNA::ShaderStageEXT` (Vertex/Fragment/Compute), `CNA::ShaderDiagnosticEXT`, `ShaderCompilationExceptionEXT`.

#### 3.10 Capability introspection (U, new since BASE)
`CNA::RendererCapabilityProfile` (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp`): 32 `RendererFeature` entries (0..31, `Count = 32`; includes CompiledXnaEffects, ShadowSampling, ImageBasedLighting, GpuTimers, six shader dialects, Texture3DSampling, BaseInstanceDrawing), 22 `RendererLimit` entries, per-`SurfaceFormat` usage masks; `GraphicsDevice::GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()` and `GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT()` (`GraphicsDevice.hpp:1561,1599`); `GraphicsCapability` enum has 19 members (`CompiledEffects`, `FloatRenderTargets`, `HalfFloatRenderTargets`, `HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering`, `ComputeShaders`, `IndirectDraw` appended; `modules/graphics/include/CNA/GraphicsCapability.hpp:14-207`).

#### 3.11 Devices extension - `modules/devices-ext` (CNA::Devices, all G by `CNA_DEVICES`)
`Camera` (+`CameraDeviceInfo`, `CameraPosition`, `CameraState`), `Clipboard`, `DisplayInfo`, `FileDialog` (+`FileDialogFilter`), `Locale` (+`LocaleInfo`), `MessageBox` (+`MessageBoxType`), `PowerInfo` (+`PowerState`), `SystemInfo`, `SystemTray`, `UrlLauncher` = 17 headers (`modules/devices-ext/include/CNA/Devices/`). Backed by `IPlatform*` services of the selected `CNA_PLATFORM`. Identical header set to BASE; only `src/DisplayInfo.cpp` changed (22 insertions, `git diff --stat`).

#### 3.12 "Nova-3D" and avatar
- **Nova-3D is not code in this repository.** `grep -ril nova3d` finds only `misc/CNAEXT.md`, `plans/plan_modern.md`, `plans/plan_renderer_cleanup.md` and `docs/cnaext-nova3d.md`. It is a downstream/planned consumer; `docs/cnaext-nova3d.md` (dated 2026-08-18) says only OPENGLES3/OPENGL33 are reliable - stale versus the renderer table in section 6. There is no `Nova3D` symbol, target or module.
- **Avatar rendering** is `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::GamerServices::AvatarRenderer` extension members (`EnableRealRenderingEXT`, `IsRealRenderingEnabledEXT`, `SetAppearanceEXT`, `DrawRealEXT`, `PartTintEXT`; `modules/gamer-services/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/GamerServices/AvatarRenderer.hpp:165-226`), always compiled, XNA's own `Draw()` overloads remain no-ops (`:155`). Uses `SkinnedModelEXT`/`AnimationPlayer`. Unchanged BASE->TARGET (header diff is empty; same 8 `demo_avatar*` example dirs and 3 avatar integration tests).
- Not part of the engine layer: it neither depends on `CNA_CNAEXT` nor on `CNA::Graphics`.

#### 3.13 Animation / skinning / morph / glTF (U, no API change since BASE)
`SkinnedModelEXT.hpp` (keyframes/bone tracks, skeleton + clip data), `AnimationPlayer.hpp` (+`ModelAnimationsEXT` for unskinned rigid clips), `MorphTargetEXT.hpp` (`MorphTargetDataEXT`, weight keyframes), `Model::getSkinsEXTProperty` (`Model.hpp:285`), `GltfImportReportEXT.hpp` (severity + diagnostics). Header diff BASE vs TARGET ignoring comments is empty for `SkinnedModelEXT`, `MorphTargetEXT`, `AnimationPlayer`, `GltfImportReportEXT`, `Model`, `ModelMesh`, `ModelMeshPart`. glTF behaviour changes live in `modules/content` (other fact sheets).

### 4. New since BASE

Crude metrics (public `include/` trees):
| Metric | BASE | TARGET |
|---|---|---|
| `graphics-ext` public headers `include/CNA/Graphics/*.hpp` | 12 | **98** (+86, none removed) |
| `graphics-ext` `src/*.cpp` | 7 | 83 |
| `graphics-ext` test `.cpp` files | 3 | 92 (967 static TEST macros) |
| `graphics-ext` example `.cpp` files | 7 | 32 |
| `graphics-ext` packaged shader dirs (`src/shaders/*`) | 0 | 11 dirs (atmospheric_sky, auto_exposure, clustered_forward, clustered_light_compute, depth_normal_prepass, gpu_instance_culler, particle_system, post_process, shadow_caster, skybox, volumetric_fog), 199 shader source files |
| `devices-ext` headers | 17 | 17 (unchanged) |
| All `*.hpp/*.h` under module `include/` (excl. renderer internals) | see per-module | graphics 154->181, core 15->22, content 38->99, math 23->24, runtime 20->23, devices 31->33 |
| `CNAEXT` token in public headers (crude, incl. comments) | 2231 | 3063 (graphics 568->780, content 92->120, math 21->67, runtime 42->58, graphics-ext 6->27) |
| Modules absent at BASE | - | `content-pipeline` (107 headers), `design` (16), `phone` (9), `inspector` (3), `diagnostics` (2) |

BASE's `graphics-ext` was exactly: `AsciiPostProcessEffect`, `AsciiQuantizeMode`, `CRTEffect`, `CRTMaskType`, `DepthEffect`, `DepthEffectMode`, `DitherMode`, `PbrMaterial`, `RenderPipelineSettings`, `RenderQuality`, `ShadowQuality`, `TonemappingMode` (12 files; `RenderPipelineSettings` was a configuration bag "the consumer it never had", no orchestrator). Tutorial 115/116's "the three post-process effects are what ships today" is BASE truth and now wrong.

New headers in always-compiled modules: `CNA/DisplayColorSpace.hpp`, `CNA/GraphicsImageAccess.hpp`, `CNA/GraphicsMemoryBarrier.hpp`, `CNA/IndirectDrawArguments.hpp`, `CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp`, `CNA/ShaderDiagnosticEXT.hpp`, `CNA/ShaderLanguageEXT.hpp`, `CNA/Graphics/VertexTypeRegistryEXT.hpp`, `Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/{AreaLightEXT,ImageBasedLightEXT,IShadowReceiverEXT,PunctualLightEXT,ShadowCascadeStateEXT}.hpp`, `Microsoft/Xna/Framework/{XmlSerializationEXT,Input/XmlSerializationEXT,FileDropEventArgsEXT,TextDropEventArgsEXT}.hpp`, `CNA/ProjectGraphicsProfile.hpp`, `Microsoft/Devices/{DeviceType,Environment}.hpp`, `CNA/AssemblyInfo.hpp`, `CNA/Diagnostics/*`, `CNA/Inspector/{Agent,Client,Protocol}.hpp`, `Microsoft/Phone/{Notification,Shell}/*` (WP7 API, not CNAEXT).
Removed: `CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/FixedFunctionArrayLayoutSupport.hpp` (renderer internal; retired fixed-function renderers).
New members on existing XNA types (diff of `CNAEXT` lines in `GraphicsDevice.hpp`): `SetDisplayColorSpaceEXT`, `GetDisplayColorSpaceEXT`, `SupportsDisplayColorSpaceEXT`, `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT`, `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`, `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT`, `SupportsRendererFeatureEXT`, `GetRendererFeatureSupportEXT`, `GetRendererLimitEXT`, `GetRendererSurfaceFormatSupportEXT`, `GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT`, `GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT`, `GetMaxComputeWorkGroup{Count,Size,Invocations}EXT`, `DrawPrimitivesIndirectEXT`, `DrawIndexedPrimitivesIndirectEXT`, `DrawInstancedPrimitivesBaseInstanceEXT`, `NotifyContentLostResourcesEXT`, `GetLogicalSizeEXT`. On effects: `BasicEffect/SkinnedEffect/PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect` now implement `IShadowReceiverEXT` (shadow map, cascades, punctual light), `PbrEffect/SkinnedPbrEffect` add `setImageBasedLightEXT`; `ShaderEffect` gains portable-payload ctors, diagnostics, uniform arrays, texture-array/storage-texture bindings; `BasicEffect` gains `getVertexColorEnabledProperty/set...` (`diff` of headers above).

Dating (author dates of the commits that ADDED each header; `git log --diff-filter=A` over `BASE..TARGET`): 2026-08-17 (`26f5a39f2` RenderPipeline, `3dfb43bc1` BloomPass, `aa3e1c9b8` PostProcessChain/FullscreenPass/BlitPass/RenderTargetPool, `76d2f849e` CascadedShadowMap, `805a225bf` ShadowMap); 2026-08-18 (`dd5902ff4` point/spot/cube shadows, `720ca5327` EnvironmentProcessor, `7665f1321` Skybox, `15fbbc62f` ComputeShader/StorageBuffer, `27f40fb98` EngineLayerVersion, `3ac8e87bc` instancing/LOD/culling); 2026-08-19 (clustered lighting `7e578d581`, `a9d3f0119`, `b0a156692`; SSR `bb8e88f18`; DOF `8cef2a4fc`; motion blur `12600ae43`; volumetric fog `3de159ab3`); 2026-08-20 (particles `8e1df5503`, decals `f4af43467`, GPU culler `ed3a34494`, GPU timer `b8aafcc7b`, light probes `c1965af97`/`3fbff28ef`/`ba72ed2d3`, OIT `e82e449f0`, HDR output `17004ad41`, debug draw `4d90b3417`); 2026-09-09 (ShaderCodeEXT `8fdeb92f2`, ShaderPackageEXT `51c966aab`, ConstantBuffer `5eeb4db9f`, Texture2DArray `cac0f7408`, StorageTexture2D `8af3adc65`). **Caution:** these author dates precede the alpha.1 tag time (2026-08-20 19:06) because the work lived on side branches merged after the tag; BASE tree (which IS the tag) contains none of it (12 headers). 233 commits touch `modules/graphics-ext` in `BASE..TARGET`; 107 touch `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md`; 2877 commits total in the range.

### 5. Version / stability

- `#define CNA_CNAEXT_ENGINE_VERSION 18` (`modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/EngineLayerVersion.hpp:26`); `getEngineLayerVersion()` / `getEngineLayerVersionString()` -> `"CNA engine layer 18"` (`:39,46`). C header carries the same marker (`engine_layer.h:42`) and `cna_engine_layer_get_version` returns 0 when the layer is absent (`:50` doc). None of these existed at BASE (no `EngineLayerVersion.hpp` there).
- Quoted promise (`EngineLayerVersion.hpp:18-20`): "**Not an ABI guarantee.** `CNAEXT.md` §9 says the engine layer may change until it stabilizes, and this number does not soften that: it records which revision you have, it does not promise that two revisions are compatible." Design doc `misc/CNAEXT.md:820-870` (§9/§9.1, dated 2026-08-18): "**It is still moving**"; settled = shape of the layer, ownership rules, two-part support question, naming/accessor conventions; still moving = per-renderer behaviour, capability queries, compute/storage; advice = pin a CNA revision and read `docs/cnaext-engine-changelog.md`.
- Revision history (`docs/cnaext-engine-changelog.md`, doc but consistent with header): rev 1 2026-08-18; rev 2 2026-08-18 **incompatible** (six renames to lowerCamelCase, `:304-320`) and a behaviour change (shadow/skybox `isSupported()` now asks capability AND `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()`); revs 3-9 2026-09-09 (texture arrays, storage textures, storage-buffer roles, base-instance draws, indirect draws); revs 10-18 2026-09-10 (GPU timing, storage-image bridges, explicit shader language/code/packages/selection/diagnostics, typed constant buffers).
- C ABI 0.29.0 is independent of the engine-layer revision (`abi.h:34-40`).
- Ownership contract (`docs/cnaext-ownership.md`, orientation): three public shapes (owned `unique_ptr`, borrowed `T*`, attached `T&`), nothing may outlive its `GraphicsDevice`, not thread-safe, no public `shared_ptr`. Consistent with header signatures spot-checked (`RenderPipeline::addUserPass(PostProcessPass*)`, `setSkybox(Skybox*)`, `ScopedRenderTarget`).

### 6. Renderer requirements (which of the 25 public identities can run the engine layer)

Gate structure (code): (a) `ThreeD` for shadows/prepass; (b) `CustomEffects`; (c) a usable shader package for the live device - `ShaderPackageEXT::selectFor(device)` (`src/ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:326`) picks among GlslDesktop / GlslEs / SpirV / Wgsl payloads (`src/PostProcessShaderPackages.cpp:34-90`; no Hlsl/Msl/Dxil packages exist); (d) for base-class passes `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` (`src/PostProcessPass.cpp:20-21`); (e) `MultipleRenderTargets`, `ComputeShaders`, `IndirectDraw`, `Instancing`+`MultiStreamVertexInput` for the subsystems in section 3.

Per-renderer answers read from code (default `IGraphicsRenderer` answers are FALSE for `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT`, compute, compiled effects: `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3109,3118,3123`):

| Identity (family) | CustomEffects | ExecutesShaderEffectSource | Shader language reported (`SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`) | Shadow sampling | IBL | Compute |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OPENGL33` (EasyGL) | true (EasyGL switch) | true (`EasyGLRenderer.hpp:2136`) | GlslDesktop (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6779-6784`; `GlProfile.hpp:152` desktop core = OPENGL33 only) | true (`:2137`) | true (`:2138`) | runtime: GL >= 4.3 (`EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6798-6806`) |
| `OPENGLES2`,`OPENGLES3`,`WEBGL1`,`WEBGL2` (EasyGL) | true | true | GlslEs | true | true | ES >= 3.1 native only; **never WebGL** (`:6802`) |
| `OPENGL4` | true (`OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1555`) | true (`OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:629`) | GlslDesktop (`OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1577-1583`) | true (`:639`) | true (`:645`) | GL >= 4.3 + native compute/SSBO (`OpenGL4Modern.cpp:909-917`) |
| `VULKAN` | true (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:3105`) | **false** (no override; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3109` comment: Vulkan compiles SPIR-V, not the GLSL text) | SpirV (`VulkanRenderer.cpp:3158-3173`) | true (`VulkanRenderer.hpp:2884`) | true (`:2876`) | true when supported (`:4280`) |
| `SDL_GPU` | **conditional**: true only if built with `CNA_SDL_GPU_SHADER_EFFECTS`, i.e. non-Windows/non-Apple/non-Emscripten and libshaderc found (`SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2688-2693`; `modules/renderers/sdl-gpu/CMakeLists.txt:30-70`) | true when device exists (`SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:2928`) | SpirV (`SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2938-2953`) | true (`.hpp:3006`) | true (`:3014`) | true (`.cpp:2733-2740`) |
| `WEBGPU` | inherits shared default (true) | true (`WebGPURenderer.hpp:1972`) | Wgsl only (`WebGPURendererModern.cpp:215-222`) | true (`:2050`) | true (`:2061`) | true (`:2139`) |
| `DIRECTX11` | true (`DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1305`) | true (`.hpp:167`; real `D3DCompile`) | reports **HLSL dialect** (`.hpp:179`) - no engine-layer HLSL packages, so package selection is unusable | false (default) | false | false |
| `DIRECTX12` | true (`DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2889`) | true (`.hpp:151`) | HLSL dialect (`.hpp:163`) | false | false | false |
| `DIRECTX9` | inherits shared default true (no `SupportsCapability` override) | **false** (no override) although it compiles HLSL `ShaderEffect` source at runtime with `D3DCompile` (`DirectX9Renderer.cpp:1346-1355`) | none declared | false | false | false |
| `METAL` | **false** (`MetalPolicy.hpp:182`) | false | none | false | false | false |
| `FNA3D` | **false** (`Fna3dRenderer.cpp:979-980`) | false | none | false | false | false |
| `SOFTWARE` | **false** (`SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:454-458`: the switch returns false with the comment that the source is accepted for resource compatibility but never executed) [corrected by the parent: the inventory fork wrote "reports true"; the switch and the capability-matrix fork say false] | false | none | false | false | false |
| `HEADLESS`, `STUB` | HEADLESS: default true (accepts, records, never rasterises; `HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-738`); STUB: every capability false (`StubRenderer.hpp:175-178`) | false | none | false | false | false |
| `PORTABLEGL` | false (`PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1679-1705` comment: shader stage is two C function pointers) | false | none | false | false | false |
| `SDL_RENDERER`, `DIRECT2D`, `CANVAS`, `HTML_DOM`, `SVG_DOM`, `GDI`, `FREEDIRECT` | 2D-only (no `ThreeD`); engine layer passes through / reports unsupported | false | none | false | false | false |

Consequences:
- **Engine-layer shader subsystems can execute on:** EasyGL family (5 identities), `OPENGL4`, `VULKAN` (via SPIR-V packages; but base-class `PostProcessPass::isSupported` is false there, see below), `SDL_GPU` (where libshaderc/ShaderEffect enabled), `WEBGPU`. **They cannot on** `[retired renderers]`, `METAL`, `FNA3D`, `SOFTWARE`, `HEADLESS`, `STUB`, `PORTABLEGL` and all 2D-only identities (no compatible package language or no shader execution). Caveat found in code: the base `PostProcessPass::isSupported` requires `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` (false on Vulkan), but the built-in passes (`BloomPass`, `FxaaPass`, `TonemapPass`, `SsaoPass`, shadows, skybox ...) override it with `CustomEffects` + valid-package checks, so Vulkan runs them; only user-authored passes that keep the default would report unsupported on Vulkan.
- `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md` "### Every renderer identity" is stale vs code in places (e.g. it says `OpenGL4` "Accepts an effect without executing its source; no float targets, compute, shadow sampling or IBL" and `DirectX12`/`DirectX9` "not implemented"; code shows OpenGL4 executes source, samples shadows, IBL and supports compute, and D3D12 reports ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT true). Also lists retired identities (`[retired]`, `[retired]`, ...) - the matrix checker derives rows from the enum, so it was updated, but prose is not authoritative. Use code.
- Float/HDR: `RenderPipeline` picks the best available scene format; on a renderer with only 8-bit `Color` targets it still runs but is not HDR (`src/RenderPipeline.cpp:110-124`). Per-renderer float-RT truth is in the graphics-feature fact sheet, not repeated here.
- Presets: `CMakePresets.json:232-247` (`cnaext`): OPENGLES3 only; examples registration allows OPENGLES3/OPENGL33/VULKAN for the CRT/Depth demos (`modules/graphics-ext/examples/CMakeLists.txt:36,59`); ASCII oracles run on every renderer (`:84-118`); shadow/skybox tests register for every renderer and self-SKIP (exit 77) when unsupported (`:121-130`).

### Notes for the parent (not facts for the site)
- Two docs disagree with code and should not be quoted: `docs/cnaext-nova3d.md` (EasyGL-only) and the renderer matrix in `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md`; C ABI number in `docs/cnaext-engine-changelog.md` (0.26.0) vs `abi.h` (0.29.0).
- Tutorials 115 and 116 assert BASE truths ("what the option covers today" table; "the three post-process effects ... are what ships today"): section 3 above is the replacement inventory. 115's rule "a new member on a type real XNA also has gets an EXT suffix" is contradicted by `SetOwnedTexture`, `Apply`, `GetTypeName` (section 1.5). 115's strict-API section is correct in mechanism but the harness covers Devices/Sensors only (section 1.4).
- (Temp header-inventory files were deleted.)

Columns: page | old claim (short quote, as published at BASE) | TARGET truth | evidence (TARGET-relative). "Matrix" refers to the capability-matrix section below; **A-D, F, G, H** = sections of "Advanced graphics facts" (A.0-A.4 = the capability matrix); **E1-E5** = sections of "Effects & compiled-effect matrix"; **V#** = "Verified facts" rows. Renderer counts throughout: 25 identities / 21 families (V2). Every page that carries the footer "Documentation snapshot for CNA 0.1.0-alpha.1 ... 50 renderer identities across 46 implementation families" needs the snapshot line replaced; TARGET is a post-alpha.1 development snapshot (V1).

## Tutorial audit (31-70, 110-117, 128)

Classification legend: **OK** = no change needed; **small fix** = localised edits, structure and technique stay valid; **substantial rewrite** = support narrative largely wrong and/or the central example fails as written; **delete** = whole purpose depends on removed functionality (none among these 49). Counts: small fix 27, substantial rewrite 22 (of 49 audited).

**Cross-cutting fixes that apply to (almost) every tutorial in range:** (a) footer "50 renderer identities across 46 implementation families" -> 25 / 21 and the alpha.1 snapshot wording; (b) the "13 2D-only renderers (... [retired])" boilerplate -> 7 identities (`SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, FREEDIRECT, GDI, SVG_DOM`); (c) "Effect Framework bytecode on FNA3D and opt-in EasyGL, SDL_GPU, Vulkan" -> 14 identities and nine options; (d) every full example that ends `Draw()` with `gd.Present()` presents twice (the `Game` presents in `EndDraw`); (e) `Load<T>` returns by value and `Game` has no `Content` member (`getContentProperty()`); (f) the default graphics profile is Reach (HiDef needed for MRT, occlusion queries, 32-bit indices, float targets, cubes > 512); (g) CNA C++ spelling is `getXProperty()/setXProperty()`, `effect->getCurrentTechniqueProperty()->getPassesProperty()[0]->Apply()`, not C# property syntax.

### Slice 31-40 (3D basics, BasicEffect, models, buffers, depth, primitives)

Method: each tutorial read in full; every API, enum, renderer name, count and behavioural claim checked against TARGET headers/implementation. All paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`). "X#" rows are cross-cutting findings that apply to several tutorials; the per-tutorial tables reference them.

Legend: BASE = alpha.1 (`1bb2145`). "Delta" says whether the truth changed between BASE and TARGET or was already wrong at BASE.

#### Cross-cutting findings (apply to the tutorials named in each row)

| ID | Applies to | Quote (all ten tutorials unless noted) | TARGET truth | Evidence | Delta |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| X1 | 31-40 (intro "Before you start" note; 31 also has a warning box) | "Requires a 3D-capable renderer such as OPENGLES3 or VULKAN; the 2D-only renderers (SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, [retired], FREEDIRECT, [retired], GDI, SVG_DOM, [retired]) throw on 3D calls." | The 2D-only set is now 7 identities: SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, HTML_DOM, FREEDIRECT, GDI, SVG_DOM. [retired] are retired and refused at configure time. STUB reports every capability false (no-op renderer). HEADLESS accepts 3D calls (records a trace, draws nothing). Default 2D-only behaviour is still `Unsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior::Throw`; `WarnAndStub` is an opt-in per device (`GraphicsDevice::SetUnsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior`). 3D pipeline is available on 16 rasterising identities (OPENGLES2, OPENGLES3, OPENGL33, WEBGL1, WEBGL2, OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU, DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, METAL, FNA3D, PORTABLEGL, SOFTWARE) plus HEADLESS. OPENGLES3/VULKAN are just examples. | `cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-43`; `docs/removed-renderers.md:24-50`; 2D-only `SupportsCapability`: `modules/renderers/sdl-renderer/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/SdlRenderer/SdlRenderer.hpp:196-201`, `direct2d/src/Direct2DRenderer.cpp:324-348`, `canvas/include/.../CanvasRenderer.hpp:93-98`, `freedirect/include/.../FreeDirectRenderer.hpp:177-182`, `gdi/src/GdiRenderer.cpp:881-891`, `svg-dom/include/.../SvgDomRenderer.hpp:229-232`, `html-dom/include/.../HtmlDomRenderer.hpp:351-354`; STUB `stub/include/.../StubRenderer.hpp:175-178`; HEADLESS `headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-738`; 3D true: `vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3095-3098`, `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2680`, `directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1299`, `directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2883`, `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1542`, `metal/include/.../MetalPolicy.hpp:175`, `portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1685`; permissive default for EasyGL/FNA3D/WebGPU/DX9/Software: `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3780-3805`, `webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:10707-10754`; policy enum `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Unsupported3DGraphicsCallBehavior.hpp:15-38`, `GraphicsDevice.hpp:1732-1737` | Delta: six names retired since BASE |

CNA exposes 50 renderer identities across 46 implementation families" | 25 public identities over 21 implementation families | `cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-27`; `docs/removed-renderers.md:24-50`; family dirs under `modules/renderers/` (21 + `common`) | Delta |

The manual call presents a second time per frame (XNA idiom: never call Present in Draw). | `modules/runtime/src/Game.cpp:729-734`, `1123-1128`; `modules/runtime/src/GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:392-408`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/GraphicsDevice.cpp:818-830` | Already wrong at BASE (`Game.cpp` EndDraw path unchanged) |

The XNA member is `GraphicsDevice::setIndicesProperty(const IndexBuffer*)`. Range-for over `EffectPassCollection`/`ModelMeshCollection`/`ModelMeshPartCollection` uses CNAEXT `begin()/end()`; `Vector3::operator+=/-=/*=` are CNAEXT. Worth a one-line purity note (tutorial 115 explains the mechanism). | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/GraphicsDevice.hpp:263`, `:500`; `EffectPassCollection.hpp` (`CNAEXT iterator begin()`); `ModelMeshCollection.hpp:102`; `modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Vector3.hpp:135,143,166` | Unchanged since BASE |
| X5 | 36, 38, 40 (and any tutorial that creates NPOT textures, 32-bit indices, >65,535 primitives, separate-alpha blends or Texture3D) | (no mention of graphics profiles anywhere in 31-40) | TARGET now enforces XNA `GraphicsProfile` rules in the shared device layer, and the default project profile is **Reach** (`GraphicsDeviceManager` takes `CNA::GetProjectGraphicsProfileEXT()`, default Reach). Under Reach: 32-bit `IndexBuffer` and 32-bit user indices throw `NotSupportedException`; draws above 65,535 primitives throw (HiDef 1,048,575); NPOT `Texture2D` with mip chain or DXT throws; NPOT texture sampled with non-Clamp addressing throws at draw time; separate alpha blend / `SourceAlphaSaturation` destination throws; `GetBackBufferData` throws; Texture3D unsupported. Opt in with `GraphicsDeviceManager::setGraphicsProfileProperty(GraphicsProfile::HiDef)` before device creation (or `CNA::SetProjectGraphicsProfileEXT`). | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/IndexBuffer.cpp:44-47`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1305` (primitive limit, user index width), `:1325-1347` (blend), `:4676-4684` (NPOT wrap), `:5146-5150`; `Texture2D.cpp:167-180`; `Texture3D.cpp:81`; default `modules/runtime/src/ProjectGraphicsProfile.cpp:12`, `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:52`, `GraphicsDeviceManager.hpp:104`; commits `6c1172e30` (SOFTWARE-208), `61f1f641f` (209), `0898bd9d3` (219), 2026-09-09 | Delta: enforcement added after BASE (`IndexBuffer.cpp` had no Reach check at BASE) |

#### 31 Your First 3D Triangle
Classification: small fix

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Intro note + warning box: "3D rendering in CNA requires the OPENGLES3 or VULKAN renderer. It is not available on SDL_RENDERER." and the 2D-only list | X1: 3D exists on 16 identities; only the 7 listed 2D-only identities refuse it | X1 |
| "SpriteBatch restores all graphics state it changes, so it won't corrupt your 3D setup as long as you set the effect matrices again after End()." | False. `SpriteBatch::Begin/End` writes BlendState, SamplerStates[0], DepthStencilState and RasterizerState onto the device (`applyRenderState`) and never restores them (same as XNA/FNA). After a HUD pass the game must re-set `DepthStencilState::Default`, `BlendState::Opaque`, `SamplerState`, `RasterizerState` before the next 3D draw (tutorial 39 already does this for depth). | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/SpriteBatch.cpp:169-177` (state written), `:378-420` (End: flush only, no restore) |
| `BufferUsage::None // None = CPU can read back; WriteOnly = GPU-only (faster)` | Semantics are: `WriteOnly` makes every `GetData` throw `System::NotSupportedException("Calling GetData on a resource that was created with BufferUsage.WriteOnly is not supported.")`; `SetData` is still allowed repeatedly. "faster" is not a CNA-verified claim. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/VertexBuffer.cpp:96-101` |
| Table: "Clip / NDC space ... maps to [-1,1] NDC" | XNA/D3D clip depth is [0,w] (NDC z in [0,1], x/y in [-1,1]); CNA follows the D3D convention (EasyGL stock shaders apply `z*2-w` conversion). Minor wording. | `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md` 10.1 (untrusted; behaviour proven by `BackBufferDepthStencilContractTest.StockEffectUsesXnaClipDepthConvention`) |
| X2 footer, X3 (`gd.Present()` in both Draw snippets) | see X2, X3 | |

Claims verified OK: `VertexBuffer(GraphicsDevice&, const VertexDeclaration&, int, BufferUsage)` ctor (`VertexBuffer.hpp:68-71`); `VertexPositionColor` fields/ctor and `getVertexDeclarationStatic()` (`VertexPositionColor.hpp:25-52`); `vb->SetData(const VertexPositionColor*, int)` (`:145`); `BasicEffect::VertexColorEnabled` public field, `setLightingEnabledProperty`, `setWorld/View/ProjectionProperty` (`BasicEffect.hpp`); `Matrix::CreateLookAt`, `CreatePerspectiveFieldOfView`, `CreateRotationY`, `Vector3::Zero/Up`, `MathHelper::PiOver4/TwoPi`, `Color::Lime/Red/Blue`, `Color(int,int,int,int)`; `Game::Update(GameTime&)`/`Draw(const GameTime&)`; `GraphicsDeviceManager(Game*)`, `setPreferredBackBufferWidth/HeightProperty`; `GraphicsDevice::SetVertexBuffer(const VertexBuffer*)`, `DrawPrimitives(PrimitiveType,int,int)`, `Clear(const Color&)`; right-handed axes; the drawn triangle winding (top, bottom-right, bottom-left) is clockwise = front-facing under the XNA default cull mode (see 40).

#### 32 BasicEffect and 3D Lighting
Classification: small fix

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "VertexColorEnabled and TextureEnabled are mutually exclusive" (Key Points), "BasicEffect has three surface colour modes. Only one can be active at a time" (table + intro) | False. They combine: the shader index is `+2` for vertex colour and `+4` for texture, i.e. texture multiplied by vertex colour is a supported permutation (XNA behaviour). Tests exist for the combination. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/BasicEffect.cpp:202-215`; `modules/renderers/vulkan/examples/vulkan_basiceffect_texture_vertexcolor_enabled_test.cpp`, `vulkan_basiceffect_coloredtextured3d_fog_test.cpp` |
| `effect_->setTextureProperty(*myTexture_);` | `setTextureProperty(Texture2D* value)` takes a pointer, not a reference (tutorial 36 passes a pointer correctly). | `BasicEffect.hpp` (`void setTextureProperty(Texture2D* value)`), `BasicEffect.cpp:440-444` |
| "EnableDefaultLighting() ... a bright white key from upper-right, a dim blue fill from the left, and a very soft back" | Actual XNA rig: ambient (0.053,0.099,0.182); key light warm white (1,0.961,0.808) dir (-0.527,-0.574,-0.628) with specular; fill light orange (0.965,0.761,0.408) dir (0.720,0.342,0.604), no specular; back light blue-grey (0.323,0.361,0.394) dir (0.455,-0.766,0.455) with specular. It also sets `LightingEnabled = true`. Fill is warm orange, not blue. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/BasicEffect.cpp:468-487` |
| Code comments: "Key light: warm sun from upper-right" for direction `(1,-1,-0.5)`, "Fill light: cool blue from the left" for `(-1,-0.3,0.5)` | The tutorial's own rule (direction = the way light travels) makes those two lights come from upper-LEFT and from the RIGHT respectively; comments are reversed. | prose vs the text under "DirectionalLight0/1/2 Setup" |
| Complete example `BuildCube`: faces built with outward-facing counter-clockwise winding (e.g. +X face corners `(1,-1,-1),(1,1,-1),(1,1,1)`; right-hand normal = +X = outward) | XNA/CNA default `RasterizerState::CullCounterClockwise` culls CCW faces; front faces are clockwise as seen from outside. With this index order the visible outside faces are culled and the cube renders inside-out. Reverse the index order (`base, base+2, base+1, base, base+3, base+2`) or use `CullClockwise`. (Derived by winding analysis, not executed.) | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/RasterizerState.hpp:19-20,117`; `modules/renderers/software/src/SoftwareRenderer.cpp:2693-2706` ("keep the clockwise-as-displayed winding visible"); `modules/renderers/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:7321` (`VK_FRONT_FACE_CLOCKWISE`) |
| "BasicEffect supports exactly three directional lights" and the whole lighting story | Still true for the XNA surface. TARGET adds (CNAEXT, always compiled, not behind `CNA_CNAEXT`) one extra shadowed punctual light and a shadow-map receiver interface: `setPunctualLightEXT(const PunctualLightEXT&)` (Point or Spot), `setShadowMapEXT`, `setLightViewProjectionEXT`, `setShadowsEnabledEXT`, `setShadowDepthBiasEXT`, `setShadowFilterRadiusEXT`, `setShadowCascadesEXT`. Worth a forward pointer. | `BasicEffect.hpp` (IShadowReceiverEXT), `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/PunctualLightEXT.hpp:14-95` |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 | see cross-cutting rows | |

#### 33 Matrices and Transformations
Classification: small fix

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `bool ok = Matrix::Invert(someMatrix, result);` "Some overloads return a bool (false if matrix is singular/non-invertible)" | `Matrix::Invert(const Matrix&, Matrix&)` returns `void` (XNA behaviour); `Matrix::Invert(Matrix)` returns the matrix. There is no singular-matrix signal (check `Determinant()` yourself). Snippet does not compile. | `modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Matrix.hpp:764-773` |
| `clip_pos = Projection * View * World * vertex_pos;` | CNA/XNA use row vectors: `clip = vertex * World * View * Projection` (the tutorial's later TRS order S*R*T is already row-vector). `Vector3::Transform` computes `p*M11+... + M41`. | `modules/math/src/Vector3.cpp:441-452`; `Matrix.hpp:168` (translation is a property of row 4) |
| "Passing degrees directly gives a rotation 57x smaller than expected." | Reversed: passing degrees (e.g. 45) as radians rotates 45 rad, which is 57x LARGER than intended. | arithmetic; `MathHelper::ToRadians` `MathHelper.hpp:153` |
| Matrix::Invert example: `cameraWorld = CreateTranslation(cameraPos) * CreateRotationY(yaw)` | Contradicts the tutorial's own TRS rule (translate-then-rotate orbits the origin). Camera world should be `CreateRotationY(yaw) * CreateTranslation(cameraPos)`. | prose |
| Solar-system demo: `moonWorld = moonLocal * moonOrbitMat * earthOrbitMat * CreateTranslation(6,0,0)` | Logic bug: the earth's orbit rotation is applied before the `Translation(6,0,0)`, so the moon orbits a fixed point at (6,0,0) instead of following the earth. Correct row-vector chain: `moonLocal * moonOrbitMat * CreateTranslation(6,0,0) * earthOrbitMat`. | prose/maths vs `earthWorld = earthLocal * earthOrbitMat` in the same demo |
| X1, X2, X3 | see cross-cutting rows | |

Claims verified OK: `CreateTranslation(float,float,float)`/`(Vector3)`, `CreateRotationX/Y/Z`, `CreateScale(float)`/`(Vector3)`/`(x,y,z)`, `CreateLookAt`, `CreatePerspectiveFieldOfView`, `MathHelper::ToRadians`, `VertexBuffer::getVertexCountProperty()` (`VertexBuffer.hpp:138`), `Color::Yellow`, `VertexPositionColor(Vector3, Color)`; cube winding in `BuildCube` is clockwise from outside (front-facing under the default cull mode); row-major/row-vector convention statement.
