#### 35 Loading 3D Models
Classification: small fix (the "Content formats" section needs a paragraph-level rewrite; the code samples need an interface downcast)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "There are three real paths into Load<Model>, and ContentManager tries them in a fixed order" (.xnb, .cnj, .gltf/.glb) | There is a fourth tier: `.cnb`, CNA's compiled binary container, ranked immediately below `.xnb` and above `.cnj`/native sources. Order: `.xnb` -> `.cnb` -> `.cnj` -> reader-declared extensions (Model: `.cnj`, `.gltf`, `.glb`). `Load<T>` also accepts an explicit `Foo.cnb` name. | `modules/content/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Content/ContentManager.hpp:494-548` (tiers), `:828-841` (.cnj first); `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:4306` (`{".cnj", ".gltf", ".glb"}`); `.cnb` count in BASE header = 0 |
| "CNA has no runtime FBX or .x importer, and it does not run the XNA Content Pipeline." | Runtime: still no FBX/.x loading (`Load<Model>` extensions above). But CNA now ships a build-time XNA-compatible Content Pipeline: `cna-content` (`cna_content_tool`, `cmake/ToolContentPipeline.cmake:20`) with `.fx`/`.fxb`/`.x`/`.fbx`/`.gltf`/`.spritefont`/etc. routes, XNB and CNB writers, plus new tools `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnj_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnb_info`. The sentence must be rewritten to "no runtime FBX/.x importer; offline conversion via cna-content or gltf_to_cnj/gltf_to_cnb". | `CMakeLists.txt:391-398`; `tools/content/content.cpp:3327-3333`; `modules/content-pipeline/include/CNA/Content/Pipeline/XnaModelSourceContentPipeline.hpp:1-35` |
| "the normal path" for `.cnj` | `.cnj` still works and `gltf_to_cnj` is still built unconditionally (usage unchanged), but the compiled artefact tier is now `.cnb` (`cna-content build ... --format cnb`); `.cnj` is the source/descriptor form. | `CMakeLists.txt:391`; `tools/gltf_to_cnj/gltf_to_cnj.cpp:1678-1692` |
| "each ModelMeshPart carries its own Effect*, typically a BasicEffect" and every sample that does `dynamic_cast<BasicEffect*>(part->getEffectProperty())` on a model loaded from glTF | For glTF (direct load or the `.cnj`/`.cnb` produced from it) the material MODEL selects the effect: `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect` for everything except `KHR_materials_unlit` (which gets `BasicEffect`); a primitive with no material is metallic-roughness by default. So the `dynamic_cast<BasicEffect*>` guards silently skip the part (no matrices, no lighting) for typical glTF. Use `dynamic_cast<IEffectMatrices*>` (as `Model::Draw` does) and `IEffectLights` for lighting. Same rule was already in BASE. | `modules/content/src/GltfImport/GltfImportCore.cpp:2919-2935`; `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:2568-2580,3387`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/Model.cpp:451-461` |
| "The whole Draw() body above is what model_.Draw(world, view, proj) does internally." | Close but not identical: `Model::Draw` throws `InvalidOperationException` if any effect is not `IEffectMatrices`, and a mesh with no parent bone uses bone index 0, not the bare `world`. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/Model.cpp:433-464` |
| "They also support integer and by-name indexing" (collections) | `ModelMeshCollection` and `ModelBoneCollection` have `operator[](int)` and `operator[](const std::string&)`; `ModelMeshPartCollection` has only `operator[](int)` (parts are unnamed). | `ModelMeshCollection.hpp:65,74`; `ModelMeshPartCollection.hpp:64` (no name overload) |
| "Content ... Both carry skeletal and rigid animation, morph targets..." | Unchanged and fine, but new since BASE: `.cnb` carries the same model data (`CnbModelFromCnj`), and the loader now decodes LZ4-compressed XNB. | `modules/content/src/Cnb/CnbModelFromCnj.cpp`; commit `caa86f6ca` |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 | see cross-cutting rows | |

Claims verified OK: `ContentManager::Load<T>` returns `T` by value and throws `ContentLoadException` (`ContentManager.hpp:473`, `Xna/ContentManager.cpp` reader wrapper); `Model::getMeshesProperty/getBonesProperty/getRootProperty`, `CopyAbsoluteBoneTransformsTo(std::vector<Matrix>&)`, `CopyBoneTransformsTo/From`, `Draw(const Matrix&, const Matrix&, const Matrix&)` (`Model.hpp:219-237,381-401`); `ModelMesh::getParentBoneProperty/getMeshPartsProperty/Draw()` (`ModelMesh.hpp:74-109`); `ModelMeshPart::getEffectProperty` (`ModelMeshPart.hpp:142`); `ModelBone` accessors (`ModelBone.hpp:32-62`); `getSkinsEXTProperty`, `getGltfImportReportEXTProperty` (`Model.hpp:285,303`); `ModelAnimationsEXT` present; cgltf 1.15 (`third_party/cgltf/cgltf.h:4`); no runtime `unitScale` (`Xna/ContentManager.cpp:2838`); Draco build-dependent (`CNA_DRACO_AVAILABLE`, `cmake/ToolGltfToCnj.cmake`); accepted model vertex strides 16/20/24/32/48/52/56/60/68/76/80 and refusal otherwise (`Xna/ContentManager.cpp:2170-2226`); `.cnj` keys `vertices/indices/vertexStride/parentBone`.

See 32. | `BasicEffect.cpp:202-215` |

Mip chains come from: an XNB Texture2D that carries them (build with `cna-content`), or constructing `Texture2D(device, w, h, /*mipMap=*/true, SurfaceFormat)` and uploading levels with `SetData(level, ...)`. Loose PNG/JPEG always import with one level. Note the Reach rule: mipmapped NPOT `Texture2D` throws (X5). | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/Texture2D.hpp:56-91,130`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/Texture2D.cpp:167-180`; `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:751-758` (extensions), `:3826-3842` ("imports glTF PNG/JPEG images with one texture level") |
| "ContentManager loads mipmaps automatically when the source asset is a KTX or DDS file" | No KTX support at runtime; `Texture2D` loose-file reader accepts `.png .jpg .jpeg .bmp .gif .tga .tif .tiff .qoi`. `.dds` is read by the `TextureCube` reader and by the build-time pipeline, not by `Load<Texture2D>`. | `Xna/ContentManager.cpp:756` (Texture2D), `:813` (`{".dds"}` TextureCube); `modules/graphics/src/Internal/ImageLoader.cpp` (no dds/ktx) |
| "Use SamplerState::LinearMipLinear (trilinear filtering)" | No such preset. Presets are `AnisotropicClamp/Wrap`, `LinearClamp/Wrap`, `PointClamp/Wrap`; XNA `TextureFilter::Linear` (used by `LinearClamp`) is already trilinear when the texture has mips. | `SamplerState.hpp:18-28` |
| "Bilinear + wrap - use for tiling terrain/floor textures" (`SamplerState::LinearWrap`) | Under the default Reach profile, sampling an NPOT `Texture2D` with non-Clamp addressing throws `NotSupportedException("Reach requires Clamp addressing for non-power-of-two Texture2D resources.")`. Use power-of-two textures or select HiDef. | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:4676-4684` (X5) |
| Full example `BuildCube` faces: `face(0, Vector3::Forward, Vector3::Up, Vector3::Right)` etc. with vertices TL,TR,BR and indices `v,v+1,v+2` | For every face the right-hand normal of TL->TR->BR equals the outward normal `n` (e.g. front: `right x up = +Z`, `n = -Z`), i.e. CCW from outside; the default `CullCounterClockwise` culls those faces and the cube renders inside-out. Swap the winding (`v, v+2, v+1, v, v+3, v+2`). (Winding analysis, not executed.) | `RasterizerState.hpp:19-20,117`; `SoftwareRenderer.cpp:2693-2706` |
| "UV (0,0) is the top-left of the texture, (1,1) is the bottom-right under the XNA convention" | Correct for XNA content; note glTF imports flip V into this convention. No change needed. | n/a |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 | see cross-cutting rows | |

#### 38 Vertex Buffers and Index Buffers
Classification: substantial rewrite (buffer-usage semantics, dynamic-update pattern, partial upload, index width; the demo throws at run time)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Demo: `dynamicVB_->SetData(gridVerts_.data(), ...)` every `Update()` while `Draw()` leaves the same buffer bound via `gd.SetVertexBuffer(dynamicVB_.get())` | `VertexBuffer::SetData` without options throws `InvalidOperationException("The vertex buffer resource is in use.")` when the buffer is currently bound on the device; only `SetDataOptions::Discard`/`NoOverwrite` (on `DynamicVertexBuffer`/`SetDataWithOptions`) or unbinding first are allowed. The demo throws on the frame after the grid is shown. Same rule for `IndexBuffer` and for `Texture` bound to a sampler slot. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/VertexBuffer.cpp:386-402`, `IndexBuffer.cpp:411-423`, `Texture.cpp:240-254`; commit `b7437e4a7` (SOFTWARE-247, 2026-09-09); BASE has 0 occurrences |
| Table "WriteOnly: Write-only, once at creation ... None: Read and write at any time ... Particle systems ... dynamic" and "Use None whenever you call SetData after the buffer has been drawn at least once" | `BufferUsage` only controls readability: `WriteOnly` makes `GetData` throw `NotSupportedException` (not "undefined behaviour"); `SetData` on a `WriteOnly` buffer is allowed any number of times (subject to the in-use rule). The XNA idiom for per-frame data is `DynamicVertexBuffer`/`DynamicIndexBuffer` with `SetDataOptions::Discard`/`NoOverwrite` (both exist), which the tutorial never mentions. | `VertexBuffer.cpp:96-101`, `:868-870`; `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/DynamicVertexBuffer.hpp:31,55,104,195,227` |
| `vb->SetData(vertices + offset, count, offset);` "Partial upload - update only elements [offset, offset+count)" | Third parameter is `elementCount`, second is the SOURCE `startIndex`: `SetData(const T*, int startIndex, int elementCount)`. That call means "read `count` elements starting at index `count`... for `offset` elements". A destination window is expressed with the 5-argument XNA form `SetData(int offsetInBytes, const T*, int startIndex, int elementCount, int vertexStride)`. | `VertexBuffer.hpp:399-436` (templates), `:441-462` |
| "For large meshes that change every frame, use partial updates to avoid uploading unchanged regions" | Not true in TARGET: the renderer contract has no sub-range upload; every `SetData`/`SetDataRawAtEXT` copies into a CPU shadow and re-submits the whole logical buffer ("Documented deviation... about cost"). A window changes what bytes change, not what is transferred. | `VertexBuffer.cpp:358-383`; `VertexBuffer.hpp:566-577` |
| "The element stride is derived automatically from sizeof(T)" | For the built-in vertex structs (`VertexPositionColor` etc.) CNA packs to the declaration layout; for custom trivially-copyable types `sizeof(T)` defines only the source span and the declaration stride governs drawing. | `VertexBuffer.hpp:387-393` |
| IndexElementSize table "ThirtyTwoBits uint32_t 4,294,967,295" and "IndexBuffer (16-bit vs 32-bit)" | Under the default Reach profile a 32-bit `IndexBuffer` (and 32-bit user indices) throws `NotSupportedException`; HiDef required. Index buffers are also capped at 67,108,863 bytes, and draws above 65,535 (Reach) / 1,048,575 (HiDef) primitives throw. | `IndexBuffer.cpp:44-57`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1305` (X5) |
| Line "VertexPositionNormalTexture::getVertexDeclarationStatic() // Position + Normal + UV" listed twice | Duplicate line (typo). | prose |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 | see cross-cutting rows | |

Claims verified OK: `IndexBuffer(GraphicsDevice&, IndexElementSize, int, BufferUsage)` and `SetData(const uint16_t*, int)`; `DrawIndexedPrimitives` 6-argument form; `Color::Lerp`, `Color::Cyan`; quad/grid index winding is clockwise (front-facing); "never recreate buffers per frame" advice; `SetData` template accepts trivially-copyable app-defined vertex types.

Rewrite notes: teach `DynamicVertexBuffer` + `SetDataOptions::Discard` for streaming; state that `BufferUsage` is a read/write hint only; drop the partial-upload cost claim; add the profile/32-bit-index note.

#### 39 Depth Buffer and Z-Fighting
Classification: substantial rewrite of the DepthFormat setup, the state table, the skybox trick and the full demo

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `PresentationParameters pp; pp.DepthStencilFormat = DepthFormat::Depth24;` | No public field; `PresentationParameters::setDepthStencilFormatProperty(DepthFormat)`, and its default is `DepthFormat::None`. In a `Game` the device is built by `GraphicsDeviceManager`, whose default is Depth24 and which is configured with `graphics_.setPreferredDepthStencilFormatProperty(DepthFormat::Depth24Stencil8)` then `ApplyChanges()`. | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/PresentationParameters.hpp:114-120`; `PresentationParameters.cpp:11`; `modules/runtime/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/GraphicsDeviceManager.hpp:206`; `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:58,600` |
| Table rows "Read - On (Less) / Off - Transparent objects, particles" and "DepthRead - Alias for Read in some XNA versions" | Only three presets exist: `Default`, `DepthRead`, `None`. `DepthStencilState::Read` does not exist; `DepthRead` is the real name of the test-on/write-off preset. | `DepthStencilState.hpp:18-22` |
| Skybox trick: "render last with CompareFunction::Always and no depth write, so it fills only undrawn pixels" (Key Points, section title, demo `skyboxDSS_`) | Wrong: `Always` passes every fragment, so the sky quad overwrites all earlier opaque and transparent geometry. The standard trick is to draw the sky last with `CompareFunction::LessEqual` (write off) and force the sky to the far plane (`z = w` in the vertex shader, or projection with far plane at 1.0 depth), or draw the sky first with depth write off. | prose vs `DepthStencilState` semantics (`setDepthBufferFunctionProperty`, `DepthStencilState.hpp:77`) |
| Demo: three "transparent" quads tinted with `effect_->setDiffuseColorProperty(c.ToVector3())`, `Color(255,0,0,160)`, `BlendState::AlphaBlend` | `Color::ToVector3()` drops alpha, the vertex colours are opaque `Color::White`, and `setAlphaProperty` is never called, so effective alpha is 1.0 and the quads render opaque. Set `effect_->setAlphaProperty(c.A/255.0f)`; note `BlendState::AlphaBlend` is premultiplied (One, InverseSourceAlpha) and `BasicEffect` premultiplies diffuse by alpha. | `modules/math/src/Color.cpp:384-390`; `BasicEffect.cpp:303-310`; `BlendState.cpp:10` |
| "gd.setDepthStencilStateProperty(DepthStencilState::Default)" ... "Restore for next frame's 3D pass" | Correct and necessary: SpriteBatch leaves its own DepthStencil/Blend/Rasterizer/Sampler state on the device (contradicts tutorial 31's "restores all graphics state"). | `SpriteBatch.cpp:169-177` |
| Z-fighting "Fix 1 - Small translation bias ... `CreateTranslation(0, 0.001f, 0)` ... toward camera" | Translating along Y is not "toward the camera". XNA/CNA provide `RasterizerState::DepthBias` and `SlopeScaleDepthBias` for coplanar decals, not mentioned. | `RasterizerState.hpp:61-66,105` |
| Depth values "normalised depth in clip space: 0.0 at the near plane, 1.0 at the far plane" | Correct (D3D convention). | n/a |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 | see cross-cutting rows | |

#### 40 Primitive Types
Classification: small fix (critical correctness: the winding section and the demo's ribbon)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "CNA exposes the same five primitive types as XNA 4.0: ... PointList" and `PrimitiveType::PointList` | XNA 4.0 has four; CNA adds a fifth as CNAEXT: `PrimitiveType::PointListEXT = 4`. `PrimitiveType::PointList` does not compile. | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/PrimitiveType.hpp:9-18`; identical in BASE |
| "PointList - each vertex is a rendered point (size controlled by rasterizer)" | No point-size state exists in `RasterizerState`; stock shaders write `gl_PointSize = 1.0` (1-pixel points). Point lists need a custom shader (`ShaderEffect`) to size points. | `RasterizerState.hpp` (no PointSize); `modules/renderers/vulkan/src/shaders/colored3d.vert.glsl:55` |
| "CNA follows the XNA / OpenGL convention: counter-clockwise winding is front-facing. The default RasterizerState culls back faces (CullMode::CullCounterClockwiseFace in XNA terms maps to GL GL_BACK culling with CCW front)." + snippet "0 -> 1 -> 2 is counter-clockwise when viewed from front: visible" + Key takeaway "Counter-clockwise winding is front-facing in CNA" | Wrong. XNA's default `CullCounterClockwise` CULLS counter-clockwise-wound triangles; front faces are CLOCKWISE (the D3D convention; every CNA renderer implements it, e.g. Vulkan `frontFace = VK_FRONT_FACE_CLOCKWISE`; Software culls positive/CCW screen area under the default). The tutorial's example triangle (BL, BR, top) is CCW and would be culled; draw top, BR, BL (as tutorial 31 does) or use `CullClockwise`/`CullNone`. | `RasterizerState.hpp:19-20,117`; `CullMode.hpp`; `SoftwareRenderer.cpp:2693-2706`; `VulkanRenderer.cpp:7321,7734,8080` |
| Demo: TriangleStrip "ribbon" with vertices alternating `(x, cy+0.05)` then `(x, cy-0.05)`, drawn with the default rasterizer | First strip triangle is (top_i, bottom_i, top_{i+1}) = counter-clockwise on screen, so under the default `CullCounterClockwise` the whole ribbon is culled. Alternate the pair order (bottom first) or set `RasterizerState::CullNone` before the ribbon. (Winding analysis, not executed.) | see previous row |
| `gd.DrawIndexedPrimitives(PrimitiveType::TriangleList, /*baseVertex=*/0, /*startIndex=*/0, /*primitiveCount=*/numTriangles);` | No 4-argument overload exists; the only form is `(type, baseVertex, minVertexIndex, numVertices, startIndex, primitiveCount)`. (Tutorials 32/36/37/38/39 use the correct 6-argument form.) | `GraphicsDevice.hpp:538-540` |
| "DrawIndexedPrimitives reads through an IndexBuffer of uint16_t or uint32_t indices" | 32-bit indices need HiDef (Reach default rejects them), and primitive counts are capped at 65,535 under Reach. | X5 |
| Table `primitiveCount` formulas | Correct (TriangleList N/3, Strip N-2, LineList N/2, LineStrip N-1, PointListEXT N). | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1250-1268` (`PrimitiveType::PointListEXT` case at `:1264`), `ModelMeshPart.hpp:51` |
| Statement "Required by DrawIndexedPrimitives in most scenes" (TriangleList) | Harmless but not a rule; all five types are legal with indexed draws. | n/a |
| X1, X2, X3, X4 (`SetIndexBuffer` in snippet) | see cross-cutting rows | |

Claims verified OK: `PrimitiveType::{TriangleList,TriangleStrip,LineList,LineStrip}` semantics; `RasterizerState::CullNone` (`RasterizerState.hpp:22`); `Matrix::CreateOrthographic(width,height,near,far)` (`Matrix.hpp:415`); `MathHelper::TwoPi`; `Color::Orange`, `Color::Yellow`; grid LineList count = vertices/2; `gd.setRasterizerStateProperty(const RasterizerState&)` (`GraphicsDevice.hpp:212`).

#### 43 Quaternions for Smooth Rotation
Classification: small fix

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "Order matters: q1 * q2 applies q1 first, then q2 (same as matrix multiplication with row vectors)." | `operator*`/`Quaternion::Multiply` is the Hamilton product. Rotating a vector by `q1 * q2` applies **q2 first, then q1**; equivalently `Matrix::CreateFromQuaternion(q1*q2) == CreateFromQuaternion(q2) * CreateFromQuaternion(q1)` (row-vector matrices). "q1 then q2" is `Quaternion::Concatenate(q1, q2)` (== `q2 * q1`). | `modules/math/src/Quaternion.cpp:137-152` (Concatenate: cross term = v2 x v1), `:449-470` (Multiply), `modules/math/src/Vector3.cpp:481-489` (Transform by quaternion = q v q^-1), `modules/math/src/Matrix.cpp:501-524` |
| `// Apply tilt first, then spin (think: local then world)` `Quaternion combined = tiltX * spinY;` | As written this applies spin first, then tilt. Use `Quaternion::Concatenate(tiltX, spinY)` (or `spinY * tiltX`) for "tilt then spin" | same |
| `orientation_ = Quaternion::Slerp(orientation_, targetOrient_, slerpT_);` (SpacecraftDemo) | Logic note, not an API mismatch: re-slerping from the already-advanced orientation with a growing t eases out and is not constant angular velocity; keep a fixed `startOrient_` and slerp from it. | sample code |

#### 48 Fixed vs Variable Timestep
Classification: **substantial rewrite** (physics-with-interpolation section, its learning objective and the "Draw uncapped" claims); rest is small fixes

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `if (gameTime.IsRunningSlowly)` and takeaways "gameTime.IsRunningSlowly" / "gameTime.TotalGameTime" | `GameTime` has no public `IsRunningSlowly`/`TotalGameTime` members (compile error); use `getIsRunningSlowlyProperty()` and `getTotalGameTimeProperty()`. | `modules/runtime/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/GameTime.hpp:41,53,62` |
| "gameTime.getElapsedGameTimeProperty() is always exactly TargetElapsedTime here" / `// always 1/60` | The game's **first** `Update` sees `ElapsedGameTime == 0`; later fixed-step updates see exactly `TargetElapsedTime`. `TotalGameTime` is the time *before* the step and advances after `Update` returns (XNA 4.0 clock; FNA/BASE advanced it before and used the target step for update 1). | `Game.cpp:653-670` (657), commit 47035d0d8 (2026-08-27) |
| "Fixed physics at 60 Hz; Draw is uncapped (variable)" and "Called as fast as the GPU allows - may be faster than 60 Hz (e.g. 144 Hz display, 60 Hz physics)" with `IsFixedTimeStep = true` | With a fixed step `Tick()` sleeps/spins until at least one `TargetElapsedTime` has accumulated, runs the update loop, then calls `Draw` **once per Tick**. Draw can never outpace Update in fixed mode. | `Game.cpp:619-635` (wait loops), `:640-707` (step loop), `:733` (Draw) |
| `float alpha = ElapsedGameTime / TargetElapsedTime; alpha = Clamp(alpha, 0, 1); Lerp(prev, cur, alpha)` ("interpolation factor ... near 1.0") | In `Draw`, `ElapsedGameTime == TargetElapsedTime * stepCount` (integer >= 1), so `alpha >= 1` and clamps to 1: the interpolation is a no-op with the built-in fixed step. Real render interpolation needs `IsFixedTimeStep=false` plus your own accumulator (alpha = accumulator / step), or `SuppressDraw()`. | `Game.cpp:692-695` |
| "If the game cannot keep up, IsRunningSlowly is set" | Correct; precisely when the update-frame lag reaches 5 and cleared when it returns to 0; catch-up is capped at `MaxElapsedTime` = 500 ms. | `Game.cpp:669-690`, `:273`, `:638-640` |
| `player_.position += player_.velocity * dt;` | Compound `+=` on `Vector3` is a CNAEXT operator (XNA has none); XNA-pure spelling: `position = position + velocity * dt`. Informational. | `modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Vector3.hpp` (`CNAEXT Vector3& operator+=`) |
| Footer 50/46 | G1 | |

Claims verified OK: `setIsFixedTimeStepProperty(bool)`, `setTargetElapsedTimeProperty(TimeSpan)`, `getTargetElapsedTimeProperty()`; default target = `TimeSpan(166667L)` = 1/60 s (`Game.cpp:301`); `TimeSpan::FromSeconds` resolves under `using namespace Microsoft::Xna::Framework` because `GameTime.hpp` has `using System::TimeSpan;` (`GameTime.hpp:9`); variable step passes the measured elapsed time (`Game.cpp:698-723`).

| "Requires -DCNA_DEVICES=ON: the Microsoft::Devices sensor layer is gated behind that CMake option, which defaults to OFF" and the warning box "The Microsoft::Devices layer is gated behind -DCNA_DEVICES=ON ... so a stock build has no sensor types at all." | Wrong at TARGET (and at BASE). `Microsoft::Devices` (Accelerometer, Gyroscope, Compass, Motion, VibrateController, `Environment`/`DeviceType`) is module `cna_devices`, always built (`add_subdirectory(devices)` unconditional) and part of the `CNA` umbrella; no `#ifdef CNA_DEVICES` anywhere in `modules/devices`. `CNA_DEVICES` (default OFF) gates only the CNA-specific `CNA::Devices` headers in `modules/devices-ext` (battery, clipboard, locale, message box ...). | `modules/CMakeLists.txt:358,385`; `CMakeLists.txt:177`; `modules/CMakeLists.txt:239`; `grep CNA_DEVICES modules/devices` = only CMake target names/tests |
| "getIsSupportedProperty() genuinely opens the SDL sensor subsystem and looks for an SDL_SENSOR_ACCEL / SDL_SENSOR_GYRO device" | The probe goes through `CNA::Platform::IPlatformSensors`; only the **SDL3 platform** implements it (`SDL_SENSOR_ACCEL/GYRO` in `Sdl3DeviceServices`); `CNA_PLATFORM` = SDL2, X11, WAYLAND, WIN32, HEADLESS, TERMINAL return no sensors, so `getIsSupportedProperty()` is false there. Target platforms accepted: Android, **iOS**, Desktop (browser excluded). | `modules/devices/src/Sensors/Accelerometer.cpp:37-56`; `Gyroscope.cpp:36-54`; `modules/devices/include/Microsoft/Devices/Sensors/Detail/PlatformSensorSubsystem.hpp:111-120`; `modules/platform/src/Sdl3/Sdl3DeviceServices.cpp:24-27`; `GetSensors()` returns nullptr in `Sdl2Platform.cpp:423`, `HeadlessPlatform.cpp:214`, `Win32Platform.cpp:330`, `TerminalPlatform.cpp:362`, `X11Platform.hpp:173`, `WaylandPlatform.hpp:136` |
| "Acceleration in each axis, expressed in units of g" | Correct: the platform reports m/s^2 and the Devices layer divides by 9.80665; Android additionally remaps to the XNA landscape convention (opt-out available). | `Accelerometer.cpp:433-435,451-470` |
| `AccelerometerReading reading = accelerometer.getCurrentValueProperty();` after the support check | `getCurrentValueProperty()` throws `System::InvalidOperationException` when the sensor instance is unsupported; the guard-first pattern is required, say so explicitly. | `modules/devices/include/Microsoft/Devices/Sensors/SensorBase.hpp:636-650` |
| MarbleGame `Draw` uses `spriteBatch_` and `marbleTex_` | Both are never created/loaded (no LoadContent) - null deref/blank texture; add creation. | sample code |
| Footer 50/46 | G1 | |

Claims verified OK: `Accelerometer::getIsSupportedProperty()` static; default constructor; `Start()`/`Stop()`; `getCurrentValueProperty()` returns the reading by value; `getIsDataValidProperty()`; `AccelerometerReading::getAccelerationProperty()` (`const Vector3&`) and `getTimestampProperty()` (`DateTimeOffset`) - `Accelerometer.hpp`/`AccelerometerReading.hpp` are unchanged BASE->TARGET; Compass and Motion are Android-only (`#if defined(__ANDROID__)` at `Compass.cpp:17`, `Motion.cpp:17`; other platforms return false); Gyroscope shares Accelerometer's platform path; `UnloadContent()` override as the Stop point compiles.

---


#### Facts learned for the fact sheet

Verified TARGET facts relevant to this slice (all TARGET-relative):

1. Math API: unchanged BASE->TARGET except additive members (`modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Vector2.hpp`, `Vector3.hpp`, `Matrix.hpp`, `Quaternion.hpp` diffs). `Vector3::Backward` = (0,0,1), `Forward` = (0,0,-1) (`modules/math/src/Vector3.cpp:86-87`). `Matrix::Invert(const Matrix&, Matrix&)` is void (`Matrix.hpp:772`). Quaternion product order: `q1*q2` = Hamilton, applies q2 first; `Concatenate(a,b)` = a then b (`Quaternion.cpp:137-152,449-470`).
2. Game loop (`modules/runtime/src/Game.cpp`): components and `FrameworkDispatcher::Update()` are driven by base `Game::Update/Draw` (`:836-884`), so overrides must call the base; fixed-step follows XNA 4.0's clock (first update elapsed 0, TotalGameTime advanced after Update; `:653-670`, commit 47035d0d8); Draw runs once per Tick and Draw's ElapsedGameTime = Target x stepCount (`:692-695,733`); IsRunningSlowly at lag >= 5 (`:682`); MaxElapsedTime 500 ms (`:273`); default target 166667 ticks (`:301`). `Game` construction registers all built-in XNB readers (`:333-338`).
3. `GameComponentCollection::Add(std::shared_ptr<IGameComponent>)` / `Insert(index, shared_ptr)` are new CNAEXT ownership overloads (`GameComponentCollection.hpp:66-93`); default UpdateOrder/DrawOrder = 0 (`GameComponent.cpp:11-14`, `DrawableGameComponent.cpp:13-17`).
4. Content (`modules/content`): load tiers `.xnb` -> `.cnb` -> loose (`ContentManager.hpp:494-551`); `Unload()` = cache clear only (`ContentManager.cpp:244-247`); `Load<SoundEffect>` uncached explicit specialization (`ContentManager.cpp:6419`); root directory defaults: `Game::Content` and `ContentManager()` = "Content", `ContentManager(IServiceProvider*[, root])` = "" (`ContentManager.cpp:103-120`); Video reader unconditional (`XnbBuiltInReaders.cpp:34-38`); LZ4 XNB decode (commit caa86f6ca); reflective XNB readers (`ReflectiveTypeReader.hpp`); runtime loose readers: Texture2D, TextureCube(.dds), Texture3D(.cnj, shared_ptr), SoundEffect(.wav), Effect(.cnj, shared_ptr; type may be Effect or a stock effect), SpriteFont(.cnj), Model(.cnj .gltf .glb), AnimationClipEXT(.cnj), Curve(.cnj), SkinnedModelEXT(.skinnedmodel.json, shared_ptr), Song, Video (`ContentManager.cpp:6303-6317`).
5. Build-time content pipeline is new: `cna-content` CLI (`tools/content/content.cpp:283-308`), XNA-shaped importers/processors, `.fx` via external fxc `fx_2_0` (Windows compiler, wine launcher on other hosts), `.fxb` direct, Effect only in `--format xnb` (`modules/content-pipeline/src/EffectCompilerService.cpp`, `docs/content-pipeline.md` "Effects" section corroborated by code). Relevant to the effects fact sheet: HLSL `.fx` source is still never compiled at runtime and CNA embeds no HLSL compiler; the offline route delegates to Microsoft fxc.
6. Video: link-complete in all builds; FFmpeg backend optional via `CNA_ENABLE_VIDEO` (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:14-56`), unsupported toolchains: MinGW, Win32, Emscripten, Android, iOS.
7. Touch/input: `TouchLocation`/`GestureSample` are accessor-only (`getXProperty()`); CNAEXT `TouchPanel::MouseTouchEmulationEnabledEXT` (default false) and `InputSuppressedEXT`; ReadGesture throws when empty; all ten gestures implemented; Pinch sample carries current positions (`GestureDetector.cpp:122-146`).
8. Sensors: `Microsoft::Devices` is always built (no CNA_DEVICES gating); sensors only through the SDL3 platform; Accelerometer/Gyroscope report g on Desktop, Android and iOS target platforms; Compass/Motion Android-only; `Microsoft::Devices::Environment`/`DeviceType` are new files at TARGET (`modules/devices/include/Microsoft/Devices/Environment.hpp`).
9. CNAEXT-tagged APIs that the audited tutorials use in passing (matters for CNA_STRICT_XNA_API pages): `Vector3/Vector2 operator+=`, `GraphicsDevice::SetIndexBuffer`, `Texture2D(assetName, device)`, `TouchCollection::begin/end`, `EffectPassCollection::begin/end`, `ContentManager()` default ctor, `LooseFileContentTypeReader`, `RegisterCnjLoader`, `TouchLocation::getPressureEXT`.
10. Site-wide: footer text "50 renderer identities across 46 implementation families" -> 25 / 21 (`cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-27`, `ls modules/renderers`); iOS statement still accurate (`cmake/ApplePlatform.cmake:234-248`); `CNA_PLATFORM` values now SDL3, SDL2, X11, WAYLAND, HEADLESS, TERMINAL, WIN32 (`cmake/PlatformSelection.cmake:15-16`).

Unverified / open (not resolvable read-only): (a) exact count of built-in XNB readers at TARGET (claimed "50" on the page); (b) whether AAC actually plays through SDL3_mixer (page claim not re-checked); (c) Android sensor CI coverage claim on page 50.


### Slice 51-60 (custom vertex, shaders, effects, normal mapping, shadows, instancing)

Scope: docs/tutorials/51-custom-vertex ... 60-instancing (site state = alpha.1 / BASE 1bb2145d). All evidence is TARGET
source unless it says BASE. CNA Markdown was treated as untrusted and used for orientation only; where it is cited it
is marked (untrusted) and was cross-checked against code. No code was compiled or run (rules forbid it), so
"does not compile" statements are derived from headers/impl/tests, not from a compiler.

Path legend (all TARGET-relative):
- `G/`   = `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/`
- `GS/`  = `modules/graphics/src/Xna/`
- `GX/`  = `modules/graphics-ext/`
- `R/`   = `modules/renderers/`
- `GLSL` = `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/GlStockShaderSources.hpp`
- `IGR`  = `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/IGraphicsRenderer.hpp`

[retired] are retired identities (no longer selectable; configure refuses them by name). OPENGL4's effect renderer does the same. Apply-first is still harmless portability advice, but the stated failure no longer happens on those renderers. BASE has no `MakeProgramCurrent`. #### 51 Custom Vertex Types

Classification: **substantial rewrite**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| "CNA ships with built-in vertex types (VertexPositionColor, VertexPositionTexture, VertexPositionNormalTexture)" | Seven ship: VertexPositionColor, VertexPositionTexture, VertexPositionColorTexture, VertexPositionNormalTexture, plus CNAEXT VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture, VertexPositionNormalTextureSkinned, VertexPositionNormalTangentTextureSkinned. | `G/VertexPosition*.hpp` (7 files, all `: public IVertexType`) |
| `struct VertexElement { int offset; VertexElementFormat format; VertexElementUsage usage; int usageIndex; };` | Members are private; access is `getOffsetProperty()/setOffsetProperty()`, `getVertexElementFormatProperty()`, `getVertexElementUsageProperty()`, `getUsageIndexProperty()`. The 4-arg constructor `VertexElement(offset, format, usage, usageIndex)` is correct. | `G/VertexElement.hpp:32-70,158-161` |
| VertexElementFormat table lists 8 values (Single ... Short4) | 12 values: adds NormalizedShort2, NormalizedShort4, HalfVector2, HalfVector4. Reach accepts up to NormalizedShort4, HiDef up to HalfVector4 (else NotSupportedException). | `G/VertexElementFormat.hpp:10-32`; `GS/VertexDeclaration.cpp:198-209` |
| VertexElementUsage table lists 8 usages with a "GLSL attribute" column (aPosition, aTexCoord0, aTangent ...) | Enum has 13 usages (adds Depth, Fog, PointSize, Sample, TessellateFactor). The GLSL attribute names are not a CNA contract. A custom ShaderEffect on EasyGL/OPENGL4 gets attribute location = element index (per-vertex elements first, per-instance after). Stock programs resolve inputs from (usage, usageIndex) semantics and their attribute names (aPos, aUV, aUV1, aNormal, aTangent, aBoneWeights, aBoneIndices) are internal. | `G/VertexElementUsage.hpp:12-34`; `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:8752-8766,11803-11806`; `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Graphics/StockVertexSemantics.hpp:11-30,63-95` |
| Struct `VertexPositionNormalTextureTangent : public IVertexType` under `#pragma pack(push,1)` with `static_assert(sizeof(...) == 56)` and `offsetof(..., Normal) == 12` etc., uploaded with `SetDataRaw(verts.data(), n, sizeof(T))` | `IVertexType` is polymorphic (virtual destructor + pure virtual `getVertexDeclarationProperty`), so a struct implementing it carries a vtable pointer: `sizeof` is larger than 56, member offsets shift, `offsetof` on a non-standard-layout type is only conditionally supported, and a raw byte upload of `sizeof(T)` would copy the vptr. CNA's own test states this ("each carries a vtable pointer and the C++ object is larger than the vertex data") and its custom vertices are plain PODs with an externally built `VertexDeclaration(stride, {...})`. The `static_assert` lines fail and the upload layout is wrong. | `G/IVertexType.hpp:11-18`; `modules/graphics/tests/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/BufferTypeConstructorTests.cpp:224-236`; `modules/graphics/tests/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/GenericUserArrayDrawTests.cpp:55-80`; `R/easygl/examples/easygl_dualtextureeffect_independent_uv_test.cpp:36-60` |
| `static const VertexDeclaration VertexDeclaration;` (member named like its type, first use unqualified) | A class member named `VertexDeclaration` changes the meaning of the type name inside the class ([basic.scope.class]); GCC diagnoses it. Tutorial 60's own code comment says the member must not be called `VertexDeclaration`, and CNA's convention is `static const VertexDeclaration& getVertexDeclarationStatic()`. Not compiled here; language-rule derivation. | `G/VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture.hpp` (uses `getVertexDeclarationStatic()`); site tutorial 60 comment |
| "SetData has overloads only for the seven stock vertex types. A custom struct ... goes through SetDataRaw" | New since BASE: `template<typename TVertex> SetData(const TVertex*, int)` (plus slice and windowed forms) for application-defined trivially-copyable vertex types (`static_assert(is_trivially_copyable)`), so a polymorphic IVertexType struct cannot use it. `SetDataRaw` remains (CNAEXT). BASE has no template overload. | `G/VertexBuffer.hpp:388-460,560` |
| "For normal mapping ... you need to define your own vertex layout" | TARGET ships CNAEXT `VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture`: Position (0), Normal (12), Vector4 Tangent (24, xyz + handedness w), TextureCoordinate (40); stride 48. | `G/VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture.hpp`; `GS/VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture.cpp:10-26` |
| (omission) declaration limits | A declaration is rejected above 255 bytes stride, above 16 elements, or with usage index outside 0..15. | `GS/VertexDeclaration.cpp:187-217` |
| (omission) typed constructor | `VertexBuffer(device, System::Type, count, usage)` resolves the layout through `CNA::Graphics::VertexTypeRegistryEXT`; a game's own struct registers itself once. | `G/VertexBuffer.hpp:60-98` |

Claims verified OK: `VertexDeclaration(int stride, std::initializer_list<VertexElement>)`; `VertexBuffer(GraphicsDevice&, const VertexDeclaration&, int, BufferUsage)`; `SetVertexBuffer(const VertexBuffer*)`; `DrawPrimitives(PrimitiveType, int, int)`; `for (auto& pass : ...getPassesProperty()) pass.Apply()` yields `EffectPass&` (`G/EffectPassCollection.hpp`); usage index 0..15.

Rewrite notes: lead with a plain POD struct + `VertexDeclaration(stride, {...})` + `SetData<T>`; show the shipped `VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture`; drop the IVertexType/pack/static_assert pattern, or explain that IVertexType structs are for CNA's built-in packing overloads only.

They refuse with `std::invalid_argument` (bad stage/language/entry point) or `CNA::ShaderCompilationExceptionEXT` (no usable variant). | "[retired] and SOFTWARE accept a ShaderEffect and silently ignore the source" | [retired] no longer exists. SOFTWARE now reports `CustomEffects == false` (its effect object still accepts source and never runs it). HEADLESS reports `CustomEffects == true` (base default) and also accepts and ignores. The correct question is `GraphicsDevice::ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT()` (new since BASE). | `R/software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:454-458`; `R/headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-741`; `IGR:3101-3109`; `G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:1625-1638` |
| "Renderers that execute a ShaderEffect: the five GL profile identities, [retired], OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, [retired], DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11 and DIRECTX12. HEADLESS records" | [retired] (and [retired]) are retired. TARGET set: EasyGL 5 identities (GLSL), OPENGL4 (GLSL), VULKAN (SPIR-V), SDL_GPU (SPIR-V; GLSL only in libshaderc builds), WEBGPU (WGSL, new), [retired renderers] (HLSL). See the matrix in section J of Advanced graphics facts. | section J2 of Advanced graphics facts |
| Language table: "VULKAN, SDL_GPU: SPIR-V bytes, not GLSL text" | VULKAN: SPIR-V only (correct). SDL_GPU: a SPIR-V payload is accepted in every build; GLSL text is compiled at runtime through the target `libshaderc` only where the build found one (Linux/Android; not Windows, Apple or Emscripten), otherwise `CustomEffects` is false and the GLSL path reports "SDL_GPU ShaderEffect is unavailable". | `R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3150-3157`; `R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:12975-13045`; `R/sdl-gpu/CMakeLists.txt:30-65`; `R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2688-2693` |
| Language table lacks WEBGPU | WEBGPU takes WGSL (vertex, fragment, compute). | `R/webgpu/src/WebGPURendererModern.cpp:215-222` |
| "DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12: HLSL, compiled at runtime" | Correct: entry point `main`, D3DCompile targets vs/ps_2_0 (Reach) or _3_0 (HiDef) on D3D9, vs/ps_5_0 on D3D11/12. Note none of the three answers `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(Hlsl, ...)` true, so the typed ShaderCode/Package constructors cannot be used there (only the string constructor). No renderer answers true for Hlsl, Msl or Dxil. | `R/directx9/src/D3D9EffectRenderer.cpp:35-52`; `R/directx11/src/D3D11EffectRenderer.cpp:44-57`; `R/directx12/src/D3D12EffectRenderer.cpp:48-61`; `grep ShaderLanguageEXT::Hlsl` finds only `GX/src/ShaderPackageEXT.cpp`/`ShaderCodeEXT.cpp` |
| "GDI, METAL, HTML_DOM, SVG_DOM throw" | Correct (GDI: unsupported-feature exception; METAL: NotSupportedException; HTML_DOM/SVG_DOM: "not yet implemented"). Missing: FNA3D and PORTABLEGL return a null effect renderer (no exception, `IsEffectValid()` false); SDL_RENDERER, DIRECT2D, CANVAS, FREEDIRECT, STUB use the base default (null). | `R/gdi/src/GdiRenderer.cpp:826-830`; `R/metal/src/MetalRenderer.mm:3382-3388`; `R/html-dom/src/HtmlDomRenderer.cpp:851-855`; `R/svg-dom/src/SvgDomRenderer.cpp:626-630`; `R/fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:1025-1029`; `IGR:3036-3040` |
| GLSL samples begin `#version 300 es`; "GLSL is what the GL family expects" | EasyGL passes custom source verbatim to the GL driver. The dialect follows the profile: GlslDesktop on OPENGL33, GlslEs on OPENGLES2/3 and WEBGL1/2. `#version 300 es` is valid on OPENGLES3/WEBGL2 only; OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 need ES 1.00 and OPENGL33 needs desktop GLSL. CNA's ES300-to-ES100 rewrite is applied only to its own stock shaders. Runtime query: `GraphicsDevice::GetShaderDialectEXT()`. | `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2287-2316,6773-6795`; `GLSL:101-104`; `G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:1491-1494` |
| ".cnj Effect descriptor ... exactly two shader fields" | Correct for `"type": "Effect"`. The same reader also accepts `BasicEffect`, `AlphaTestEffect`, `DualTextureEffect`, `EnvironmentMapEffect`, `SkinnedEffect` descriptors. | `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:1046-1111` |
| "Building with CNA_STRICT_XNA_API" (in 53; same idea here via the CNAEXT warning) | CNAEXT becomes `[[deprecated]]`; it is a warning unless `-Werror=deprecated-declarations`. | `modules/core/include/CNA/CNAHelper.hpp:14-25` |

Rewrite notes: replace the renderer/language tables with the F2 matrix; document `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`/`SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`/`GetShaderDialectEXT`, typed ctors and `ShaderPackageEXT` (with the offline generator `tools/shader_package/README.md`), diagnostics, and the compiled-effect option list.

#### 53 Shader Uniforms and EffectParameter

Classification: **substantial rewrite**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3, C4 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| "There is no vec3, vec4 or mat4 array setter ... a bone palette has to be flattened and pushed through SetUniformFloatArray" and "There is no matrix-array setter on ShaderEffect" | `SetUniformVec3Array` and `SetUniformMat4Array` exist (the latter also accepts the `name[0]` spelling). Both are new since BASE. A vec4-array setter still does not exist. | `G/ShaderEffect.hpp:165,179` |
| "The int subscript returns a reference, by position" / `EffectParameter& first = effect->getParametersProperty()[0];` | `EffectParameterCollection::operator[](int)` now returns `EffectParameter*` (nullptr when out of range); BASE returned a reference. The example does not compile at TARGET. | `G/EffectParameterCollection.hpp:87-96` (BASE: same lines return `EffectParameter&`) |
| "EffectParameter ... is populated by ... AlphaTestEffect, DualTextureEffect, EnvironmentMapEffect, SkinnedEffect and the PBR effects ... nothing populates it for ... a bare BasicEffect" | BasicEffect populates its own parameters (Texture, DiffuseColor, EmissiveColor, SpecularColor, SpecularPower, DirLight0..2 Direction/Diffuse/Specular, EyePosition, FogColor, FogVector, World, WorldInverseTranspose, WorldViewProj, ShaderIndex); true at BASE too. Compiled XNA effects reflect a full graph (parameters, array elements, struct members, annotations, techniques, passes) into the same collections. | `GS/BasicEffect.cpp:69-` (BASE `BasicEffect.cpp:29` also calls `CacheEffectParameters()`); `G/EffectParameter.hpp:34-70` |
| "Use it to inspect or drive a stock effect the XNA way" and `effect->getParametersProperty()["DiffuseColor"]->SetValue(Vector3(...))` | On stock effects the parameters are mostly outputs: `OnApply()` writes them from the effect's property fields and `FillGpuDrawParams()` reads the fields (e.g. BasicEffect forwards `diffuseColor_`), so a manual `SetValue` on `DiffuseColor` is overwritten or unused. Only a few getters read back from parameters (SpecularColor, SpecularPower, FogColor, Texture, Bones). `DiffuseColor` is a 1x4 vector, so `SetValue(Vector3)` is shape-mismatched on compiled effects. Medium confidence on "ignored"; verify with a pixel test before publishing a positive statement. | `GS/BasicEffect.cpp:~303,415-454`; `GS/SkinnedEffect.cpp:173-183`; `GS/AlphaTestEffect.cpp:124,187-241`; `GS/EffectParameter.cpp:313-321` |
| SetValue table notes: "Texture2D* ... Binds the texture to the next available texture unit and sets the sampler uniform"; "int ... explicit sampler unit binding"; "Matrix ... Uploaded column-major" | For non-compiled effects `SetValue` only stores the value (`texture2DData_ = v`, `floatData_ = {...}`); there is no GL binding or uniform upload at `SetValue`. For compiled effects values go to compiled storage and are uploaded at `EffectPass::Apply()` when dirty. | `GS/EffectParameter.cpp:921-929,1103-1119,1140-1156` |
| "The constructor does not throw when the shader fails to compile. IsEffectValid() is the only signal" | See 52: `GetCompileErrorEXT()`/`GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT()` exist; typed constructors throw. | `G/ShaderEffect.hpp:98-112`; `GX/src/ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:472-522` |
| "Building with CNA_STRICT_XNA_API turns any use of it into a compile error" | Only with `-Werror=deprecated-declarations`; otherwise a deprecation warning (tutorial 115 says this correctly). | `modules/core/include/CNA/CNAHelper.hpp:14-25` |
| Renderer/language paragraph ([retired], SPIR-V/HLSL) | See 52. | |

Claims verified OK: `operator[](const std::string&)` returns a pointer or nullptr; `GetParameterBySemantic`; element pointers stay valid across `Add()` (unique_ptr storage); `SetValue` overload set (bool/int/float/Vector2-4/Matrix/Quaternion/std::string/`std::vector<T>`/`Texture*`,`Texture2D*`,`Texture3D*`,`TextureCube*`; `SetValueTranspose`); no `SetValue(const Texture&)`; iteration yields `EffectParameter&`; `Matrix::ToColumnMajor` (`modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Matrix.hpp:1014`); `SkinnedEffect::MaxBones == 72`.

Rewrite notes: split into (a) ShaderEffect uniforms, (b) stock-effect parameters (read-mostly mirrors), (c) compiled-effect parameters (the real EffectParameter use case: reflection, arrays, structs, annotations, dirty upload at Apply). Fix the subscript return type.

#### 55 DualTextureEffect for Lightmaps

Classification: **small fix (many point edits)**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| `fragColor = albedo * lightmap * u_diffuseColor;` and `vec4 result = c1 * c2 * u_diffuseColor;` | The stock program doubles the first texture's RGB: `base = texture(Texture); base.rgb *= 2.0; color = base * texture(Texture2) * Diffuse` (XNA/FNA behaviour). An identity lightmap is therefore mid-grey (0.5), not white. Same in the vertex-colour variant. | `GLSL:834-836,895-897`; `R/vulkan/examples/vulkan_dualtextureeffect_doubling_test.cpp` |
| "CNA applies gamma decoding automatically when the textures are created with SurfaceFormat::Color and the effect's linear-space flag is enabled" | DualTextureEffect has no linear-space flag (public API: matrices, DiffuseColor, Alpha, fog, Texture, Texture2, VertexColorEnabled, SetOwnedTexture/2) and the stock shader does no gamma decode. | `G/DualTextureEffect.hpp` (whole public surface); `GLSL:790-846` |
| "preserves the second UV channel as TextureCoordinate2" / `v_texcoord2` | No such name. The second UV is `VertexElementUsage::TextureCoordinate` with usageIndex 1; the stock program reads TEXCOORD0 and TEXCOORD1 independently (fixed by SAMPLE-073; regression `EasyGL_DualTextureEffect_IndependentUV`); a single-UV mesh leaves the second input at (0,0,0,1). | `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Graphics/StockVertexSemantics.hpp:78-84,96-105`; `GLSL:793-800`; `R/easygl/examples/easygl_dualtextureeffect_independent_uv_test.cpp` |
| "Export the mesh with both UV channels to OBJ or FBX ... CNA's content loader ingests the mesh" | No OBJ reader exists in TARGET. FBX and .x are build-time XNA content-pipeline importers (`FbxImporter`, `XImporter`). Runtime `ContentManager` resolves `.xnb`, `.cnj`, `.gltf`, `.glb`. Whether glTF `TEXCOORD_1` reaches a DualTextureEffect is unverified (glTF import builds PBR/Skinned materials). | `modules/content-pipeline/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Content/Pipeline/ModelImporters.hpp:37,110`; `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:4302-4313` |
| Info box: "Most renderer families defer to the framework's SurfaceFormat::Color-only gate. [retired] has a promoted HdrBlendable texture path" | [retired] is retired. At TARGET EasyGL, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, OPENGL4, WEBGPU, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 and SOFTWARE classify surface formats themselves (subject to the GraphicsProfile Reach/HiDef rule, checked first); only renderers that defer get the Color-only rule. | `GS/Texture2D.cpp:104-130`; `GS/Texture.cpp:204-210`; `GS/GraphicsDevice.cpp:3790-3806`; overriders of `ClassifySurfaceFormatEXT` in `R/{easygl,vulkan,sdl-gpu,opengl4,webgpu,directx11,directx12,software}` |
| "CNA's own vulkan_dual_texture_test and [retired]_dual_texture_test" | [retired] is retired and `[retired]_dual_texture_test` is gone. Surviving: `R/vulkan/examples/vulkan_dual_texture_test.cpp`, `R/easygl/examples/easygl_dual_texture_test.cpp`. | file listing |
| "SetData is overloaded only for the stock vertex types; a custom struct goes through SetDataRaw" (also 60) | Generic `SetData<T>` exists for plain trivially-copyable structs; `SetDataRaw` still exists. | `G/VertexBuffer.hpp:399-460,560` |

Claims verified OK: `setTextureProperty`/`setTexture2Property`; `VertexPositionDualTexture` layout (stride 28, UV0 usage 0, UV1 usage 1) is a valid declaration (`VertexDeclaration({...})` explicit init-list ctor `G/VertexDeclaration.hpp:36`); the seven shipped vertex types list; `VertexColorEnabled` works (there is a dedicated colored dual-texture program and a test) even though `docs/dualtextureeffect-support.md` (untrusted, stale) still says it is a no-op (`GLSL:849-900`; `modules/graphics/examples/dualtextureeffect_vertexcolor_test.cpp:1-20`); `IndexBuffer(gd, IndexElementSize::SixteenBits, n, usage)` and `IndexBuffer::SetData(const uint16_t*, int)` (`G/IndexBuffer.hpp:51,125`).

Additions: an unbound Texture or Texture2 samples opaque black, not white (XNA-measured; `docs/dualtextureeffect-support.md` s.4 untrusted; tests `easygl_dualtextureeffect_null_texture0_test.cpp`, `..._null_texture2_test.cpp`, Vulkan equivalents).

#### 56 EnvironmentMapEffect for Reflections

Classification: **substantial rewrite (explanatory sections; the API code is right)**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| "implements a per-vertex Phong lighting model" and "supports one directional light (inherited from the built-in BasicEffect lighting model)" | The effect implements `IEffectLights` (DirectionalLight0, 1, 2; `EnableDefaultLighting`); the stock shader sums three directional Lambert terms per fragment (`NdotL0..2`), no Phong lobe. DirectionalLight0 is enabled by default. | `G/EnvironmentMapEffect.hpp` (`: IEffectLights`); `GLSL:985-1008`; `GS/EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp:38-42` |
| Fresnel: `pow(1.0 - abs(dot(N, V)), u_fresnelFactor * 5.0); reflectWeight = u_envMapAmount + fresnelWeight * (1.0 - u_envMapAmount);` and "Values between 0 and 1 blend between the two extremes" | Per-vertex (not per-fragment): `vFresnel = clamp(FresnelEnabled ? pow(max(1 - abs(N.V), 0), FresnelFactor) * EnvironmentMapAmount : EnvironmentMapAmount, 0, 1)`. FresnelFactor is an exponent (default 1.0, unbounded), and 0 disables Fresnel (`fresnelEnabled_ = factor != 0`). The vertex clamp reproduces D3D9's clamp of COLOR outputs before interpolation. | `GLSL:942-960`; `GS/EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp:274-284` |
| "EnvironmentMapAmount ... a scalar in [0, 1]" | Default 1.0. Values above 1 are legal (the per-vertex weight is what is clamped to [0,1]; the code comment cites XNA amounts of 5). | `GS/EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp:40`; `GLSL:942-957` |
| "EnvironmentMapSpecular ... a stylised Blinn-Phong lobe aimed at making the surface appear shiny under the directional light" | The specular term is `EnvironmentMapSpecular * envSample.a * combinedAlpha`, i.e. it is driven by the cube map's alpha channel, not by light direction. Default Zero. | `GLSL:1009`; `GS/EnvironmentMapEffect.cpp:41` |
| Combining snippet: `color = mix(diffuse.rgb, envSample, reflWeight) + u_envMapSpecular * specularIntensity; fragColor = vec4(color, diffuse.a)` | `base = (lightSum * DiffuseColor + EmissiveColor) * texture.rgb; rgb = mix(base, env.rgb * combinedAlpha, vFresnel) + specular * env.a * combinedAlpha; alpha = DiffuseColor.a * texture.a` (so DiffuseColor, EmissiveColor and Alpha properties participate). | `GLSL:1000-1010`; `G/EnvironmentMapEffect.hpp` |
| "The sampler uses trilinear filtering with mipmaps by default ... CNA does not generate them for you" | Unverified: no default cube-sampler choice found in the stock path. Do not publish as fact without a renderer-specific check. | not found |

Claims verified OK: all property names (`setEnvironmentMapProperty(TextureCube*)`, `setEnvironmentMapAmountProperty`, `setEnvironmentMapSpecularProperty(Vector3)`, `setFresnelFactorProperty`, `setTextureProperty`, ambient/directional-light setters, world/view/projection) (`G/EnvironmentMapEffect.hpp`, `G/DirectionalLight.hpp:41-90`); `TextureCube` is copyable and loadable with `Load<TextureCube>` from `.dds`, `.cnj`(`sourceFile`), and `.xnb` (`G/TextureCube.hpp:75`, `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:811-846`; CNJ TextureCube compiler `modules/content/src/Cnb/CnjToCnb.cpp:163-181`); cube face order table.

Rewrite notes: replace the Overview, Fresnel, Amount, Specular and Combining sections with the code-derived formulas above.

#### 58 Normal Mapping

Classification: **substantial rewrite**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3, C4 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| "CNA does not have a built-in NormalMapEffect. Normal mapping requires a custom GLSL shader compiled through ShaderEffect" | True only for the XNA stock effects (none exposes a normal map). `PbrEffect` and `SkinnedPbrEffect` (CNAEXT, always compiled) provide tangent-space normal mapping: `setNormalMapProperty(Texture2D*)`, `setNormalScaleEXTProperty(float)`. Compiled XNA/FNA effects that normal-map also run on the compiled-effect renderers. PBR is implemented by [retired renderers], EasyGL, METAL, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU (untrusted doc, cross-checked in renderer sources) and refused by FNA3D and PORTABLEGL. | `G/PbrEffect.hpp:165-170,271-286`; `docs/gltf-renderer-pbr-fallbacks.md:9-20` (untrusted); `R/fna3d/src/Fna3dDraw.cpp:80-97`; `R/portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:436-437` |
| "OPENGLES3 and VULKAN both can [execute a custom ShaderEffect]" (with the GLSL shown) | VULKAN's ShaderEffect accepts SPIR-V only, so the shown GLSL cannot be passed as text (needs glslc/SPIR-V or a ShaderPackageEXT). | `R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3150-3157` |
| "Note that [retired] accepts one and silently ignores it" | [retired] is retired (see 52 for SOFTWARE/HEADLESS behaviour). | `docs/removed-renderers.md` |
| "CNA's model loading generates tangents during import when the mesh has UV coordinates" | glTF import computes a per-vertex tangent (Vector4: xyz + handedness) when the file has no `TANGENT` accessor, for PbrEffect's normal mapping (Lengyel-style, not exact MikkTSpace); legacy `_TANGENT/_BINORMAL` are handled; the tangent is dropped (with a diagnostic) if the chosen layout has no tangent slot. The result is the 48-byte tangent layout, not the tutorial's vec3 + float layout. | `modules/content/src/GltfImport/GltfImportCore.cpp:851-925,1287-1302` |
| Vertex layout `a_tangent` (vec3, loc 3) + `a_tangentW` (float, loc 4) | The shipped tangent type is Position, Normal, Vector4 Tangent (w = handedness), TexCoord; on EasyGL a ShaderEffect sees locations 0,1,2,3 in that declaration order (`vec3, vec3, vec4, vec2`). | `GS/VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture.cpp:10-26`; `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:8752-8766` |
| "Export them as PNG or DDS (BC5 / ATI2 compressed format ...)" | There is no BC5/ATI2 SurfaceFormat; the DDS reader keeps Color, Dxt1, Dxt3, Dxt5 only (SurfaceFormat also has Bc7EXT/Bc7SrgbEXT, no BC5). The BC5 recommendation and reconstruction shader are not loadable content. | `G/SurfaceFormat.hpp:10-60`; `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Graphics/DdsSurfaceReader.hpp:12-25` |
| "Call Apply() first" | see C4 | |

Claims verified OK: `ShaderEffect` has `SetUniformMat4/Vec3/Float/Int` and no mat3 setter; `Matrix::Transpose`, `Matrix::Invert`, `Vector3::Normalize`, `Matrix::ToColumnMajor`; `SetTexture(unit, Texture2D&)`; `.cnj` Effect descriptor fields; `DrawIndexedPrimitives(type, baseVertex, minVertexIndex, numVertices, startIndex, primitiveCount)`.

Rewrite notes: present three options (PbrEffect normal map + `VertexPositionNormalTangentTexture`; custom ShaderEffect with per-renderer payload; compiled XNA effect) and drop the BC5 advice.

#### 59 Shadow Mapping

Classification: **substantial rewrite**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3, C4 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| Premise: shadow mapping "requires an off-screen RenderTarget2D and custom GLSL shaders" | TARGET ships a shadow subsystem (CNAEXT engine layer, needs `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`): `CNA::Graphics::ShadowMap` (directional, quality presets 512..4096), `CascadedShadowMap` (2..4 cascades, sphere-fitted, atlas), `SpotShadowMap`, `CubeShadowMap`; receivers via `IShadowReceiverEXT` on BasicEffect, SkinnedEffect, PbrEffect, SkinnedPbrEffect (`setShadowMapEXT`, `setLightViewProjectionEXT`, `setShadowsEnabledEXT`, `setShadowDepthBiasEXT`, `setShadowFilterRadiusEXT`, `setShadowCascadesEXT`, `setPunctualLightEXT`); AlphaTest/DualTexture/EnvironmentMap effects are not receivers. None of it exists at BASE (graphics-ext had 12 headers, ShadowQuality only). | `G/IShadowReceiverEXT.hpp:44-`; `G/BasicEffect.hpp:401`, `G/SkinnedEffect.hpp:403`, `G/PbrEffect.hpp:494`, `G/SkinnedPbrEffect.hpp:494`; `GX/include/CNA/Graphics/ShadowMap.hpp:50`, `CascadedShadowMap.hpp:61-65`, `SpotShadowMap.hpp:38`, `CubeShadowMap.hpp:59`, `ShadowQuality.hpp:13-25`; `G/ShadowCascadeStateEXT.hpp` |
| "Point lights require six shadow maps ... or dual-paraboloid, but that is beyond the scope" | `CubeShadowMap` exists and `PunctualLightEXT` carries `ShadowCube` (point) or `ShadowMap` + `ShadowViewProjection` (spot). | `GX/include/CNA/Graphics/CubeShadowMap.hpp:59`; `GS/BasicEffect.cpp:222-258` |
| "compute tight bounds around the current camera frustum (a technique called cascaded shadow maps or fitted shadow maps)" | Implemented as `CascadedShadowMap` (bounding-sphere fit for stability, texel snapping, atlas, max 4 cascades). | `GX/include/CNA/Graphics/CascadedShadowMap.hpp:20-65` |
| "[retired] and [retired]'s promoted format exceptions do not make float targets portable" (x3); "OPENGLES3/Vulkan defer to Texture::ValidateFormat, which accepts only SurfaceFormat::Color" (x3); "VULKAN: ... The same SurfaceFormat::Color restriction applies" | [retired] and [retired] are retired. EasyGL and Vulkan do not defer: they classify render-target formats (EasyGL runtime-probes float and normalised formats). Float targets are a queryable capability (`FloatRenderTargets`, `HalfFloatRenderTargets`, derived from `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT`). CNA's own `ShadowMap` uses `SurfaceFormat::Single` when the renderer can render to it and packs into Color otherwise. | `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6746-6771`; `GS/GraphicsDevice.cpp:3408-3425,3790-3806`; `G/GraphicsCapability.hpp:146,158`; `GX/src/ShadowMap.cpp:90-96` |
| Platform note: on OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 "the technique needs nothing beyond an RGBA8 render target and standard GLSL ES 3.00, it carries across without extension checks" | Self-contradictory: those two profiles are GLSL ES 1.00 and a custom ShaderEffect's source is passed to the driver verbatim, so `#version 300 es`, `in/out` and `textureSize` (ES 3.00-only) do not compile there; only CNA's own stock shaders are rewritten to ES 1.00. | `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2287-2316`; `GLSL:101-104` |
| "OPENGLES3 and VULKAN both qualify" (custom GLSL ShaderEffect) | VULKAN needs SPIR-V for a custom ShaderEffect. | `R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3150-3157` |
| XNB compiled-effect note: "FNA3D and explicitly enabled SDL_GPU, EasyGL-family or Vulkan" | Ten backends (see 52). | `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:308-525` |

Claims verified OK: `RenderTarget2D(gd, w, h, mipMap, SurfaceFormat, DepthFormat, msaa=0, usage)` (`G/RenderTarget2D.hpp:45-53`); `SetRenderTarget(RenderTarget2D*)` (`nullptr` selects the 1-arg overload); `setRasterizerStateProperty(RasterizerState::CullCounterClockwise)`; `Matrix::CreateOrthographic/CreateLookAt`; render target usable as `Texture2D` via `SetTexture(unit, Texture2D&)`.

Shadow-sampling renderer set (declared, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT()`): EasyGL (5 identities), OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU. Every other renderer accepts and ignores the state.

Rewrite notes: keep a shortened "how it works" with the custom-shader path for education, then add the engine-layer path (`ShadowMap.begin/end`, `getCasterEffect()`, `BasicEffect::setShadowMapEXT` ...) with the support matrix above; remove the [retired]/Color-only narrative.

#### 60 Instanced Rendering

Classification: **substantial rewrite**

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| C1, C2, C3, C4 | see cross-cutting rows | |
| "DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 and SDL_GPU supply no override, so a positive instancing answer from them means only 'not known to be unsupported'" | DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 and SDL_GPU have exhaustive capability switches with an explicit `Instancing: true`; DIRECTX9 has no override (default true) but has a real instanced path. False: METAL, PORTABLEGL, STUB, the seven 2D-only, OPENGLES2 and WEBGL1. FNA3D answers from `FNA3D_SupportsHardwareInstancing` and refuses instancing with stock effects. | `R/directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1296-1320`; `R/directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2879-2905`; `R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2675-2716`; `R/directx9/src/D3D9InstancedDraw.cpp:74-`; `R/metal/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Metal/MetalPolicy.hpp:170-190` (Instancing false at :185); `R/fna3d/src/Fna3dRenderer.cpp:982-988`; `R/portablegl/src/PortableGLRenderer.cpp:1685-1710` |
| "Most base capability entries default to true; only multi-stream input and compiled effects opt out and stencil delegates" | The false-by-default set is larger: also `FloatRenderTargets`, `HalfFloatRenderTargets` (derived), and compute/indirect/half-float-filtering are derived by `GraphicsDevice`; the enum has 19 members. `DrawInstancedPrimitives` itself does not consult `Instancing`: it validates arguments and defers to the renderer. | `IGR:3775-3796`; `G/GraphicsCapability.hpp:14-207`; `GS/GraphicsDevice.cpp:2010-2110,3405-3445` |
| "vertexOffset -- byte offset within the buffer" | It is an element (vertex) offset: "Each binding's vertex offset is an element offset." | `G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:545-552`; `G/VertexBufferBinding.hpp` |
| "Attribute indices 4-8 are used (0-3 are reserved for per-vertex data)"; shader inputs `layout(location = 4..8)` for `i_row0..i_color` | For a custom ShaderEffect on EasyGL/OPENGL4 the per-instance attributes start immediately after the last per-vertex attribute location, in declaration element order. With a 3-element mesh declaration they occupy locations 3..7, not 4..8. The usage indices 4..8 written into the declaration have no effect on placement. (CNA's own stock instanced shaders use fixed locations 12-15.) | `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:8752-8776,8804-8828,11841-11845`; `R/opengl4/src/OpenGL4StockDraw.cpp:1550-`; `GLSL:78-92` |
| `mat4 world = transpose(mat4(i_row0, i_row1, i_row2, i_row3));` with "XNA/CNA matrices are row-major; transpose gives the column-major form required by GLSL" (two shaders) | CNA's own stock instanced shader uses `mat4(cnaInstanceCol0..3)` with no transpose, because XNA matrix rows placed as GLSL columns already give the column-vector matrix (M^T) that every other CNA upload produces (`Matrix::ToColumnMajor` copies row-major memory). The extra `transpose()` yields M, moving the translation (row 3) into the bottom row, so instances would be mis-transformed. Derived from code, not rendered. | `GLSL:84-92`; `modules/math/src/Matrix.cpp:1268-1286`; `modules/math/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Matrix.hpp:1009-1014` |
| "GrassInstance is a custom struct, so it goes through SetDataRaw -- SetData is overloaded only for the seven stock vertex types" | Generic `SetData<T>` works for this plain struct. | `G/VertexBuffer.hpp:399-460` |
| "recreate or map the instance buffer each frame ... SetDataRaw" | Per-frame streaming has `DynamicVertexBuffer` with `SetDataOptions` (Discard/NoOverwrite) and `SetDataRawWithOptionsEXT`. Improvement rather than an error. | `G/DynamicVertexBuffer.hpp:107-128,268-294` |
| Example ShaderEffect + instancing presented as generally available | Per renderer: EasyGL and OPENGL4 place custom per-instance streams (above); VULKAN handles custom programs in instanced draws; WEBGPU builds per-instance layouts for custom effects; SDL_GPU: "CNAEXT ShaderEffect instancing is not implemented". D3D9/D3D11/D3D12 custom-effect + per-instance stream not verified in this slice. | `R/vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:21077-21165`; `R/webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:8816-8820`; `R/sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2956-2963` |
| (omission) stock effects can be instanced | BasicEffect and the other stock families accept an instanced draw: D3D11/D3D12 read the per-instance world matrix from TEXCOORD1..4 of the per-instance stream (throws NotSupportedException if all four are absent); EasyGL from locations 12..15. | `R/directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:3263-3285`; `GLSL:78-92` (locations 12-15, `R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:8761`); `modules/graphics/tests/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/InstancedDiffuseColorTests.cpp:1-60` |
| Code comment: "NotImplementedException unconditionally. See Tutorial 52." | Leftover sentence fragment inside the example (stale claim about bytecode). Delete. | site HTML |
| Performance table ("~15 ms" vs "~0.1 ms") | No measurement in the tree supports these numbers. | none found |

Claims verified OK: `DrawInstancedPrimitives(PrimitiveType, baseVertex, minVertexIndex, numVertices, startIndex, primitiveCount, instanceCount)` (`G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:573-577`); it throws InvalidOperationException if no effect, no index buffer or no vertex buffer is bound (`GS/GraphicsDevice.cpp:2029-2038`); `SetVertexBuffers(const std::vector<VertexBufferBinding>&)` (`G/GraphicsDevice.hpp:489`); `VertexBufferBinding(VertexBuffer*, int vertexOffset, int instanceFrequency)`; instanceFrequency semantics (0 per-vertex, N advances every N instances, divisor equals frequency) (`R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:11883-11890`); one per-vertex + one per-instance stream needs no `MultiStreamVertexInput` capability (`G/GraphicsCapability.hpp:74-75`); OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 have no instancing (`R/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6190-6200`); `GrassInstance` declaration (5 x Vector4, stride 80) is a valid declaration.

Rewrite notes: fix location/transpose in both shaders (or use per-renderer payloads), replace the capability paragraph with the per-renderer table, document stock-effect instancing, `DynamicVertexBuffer`, and the SDL_GPU ShaderEffect limitation.

### Slice 61-70 (occlusion, MRT, stencil, cubemaps, MSAA, bloom, deferred, terrain, water, procedural)

All citations are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`). "BASE" = `/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-base`.
Rubric: **small fix** = localized edits (about 10 or fewer), structure and technique stay valid; **substantial rewrite** =
support narrative largely wrong and/or the central example fails as written; **delete** = none.

DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, DIRECT2D, PORTABLEGL, METAL, GDI, STUB all answer explicitly (several with no `default:` arm). `GraphicsCapability` has 19 members. The shared base returns false for MultiStreamVertexInput, CompiledEffects, FloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatRenderTargets (and `StencilBuffer` delegates to `SupportsStencilBuffer()`); MRT is additionally gated by the profile ceiling at device level. Answers are runtime-probed on EasyGL (GL_MAX_SAMPLES, ES2 profile), Vulkan (device limits), DX11/12 (MSAA clamp), SDL_GPU, WebGPU (4x MSAA probe), FNA3D. | `modules/graphics/include/CNA/GraphicsCapability.hpp:14-207`; `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/Common/IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3775-3800`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/GraphicsDevice.cpp:3400-3440`; renderer switch functions listed in "section H of Advanced graphics facts" |

#### 61 Occlusion Queries
Classification: **substantial rewrite** (capability/support narrative wrong in 6 places; the default-profile example throws; core API names still valid)

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Prereq box: thirteen 2D-only list; footer 50/46 | See G1, G2 | G1/G2 |
| "OcclusionQuery relies on real GPU query objects — GL_ARB_occlusion_query on the GL family" | EasyGL uses core query objects: it probes `GL_SAMPLES_PASSED` (0x8914) once per process and keeps it if the driver accepts (desktop GL, OPENGL33); otherwise falls back to boolean `GL_ANY_SAMPLES_PASSED` (OPENGLES3/WEBGL2). No ARB extension is referenced. OPENGL4 always uses `GL_SAMPLES_PASSED` exactly. | `modules/renderers/easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2745-2817`; `modules/renderers/opengl4/include/CNA/Internal/Renderers/OpenGL4/OpenGL4Renderer.hpp:218`; `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1554` |
| "A renderer that has none inherits CreateOcclusionQuery()'s default, which returns nullptr." | The public `OcclusionQuery` constructor never returns an unusable object: it throws `NotSupportedException` if the profile is Reach OR `SupportsCapability(OcclusionQuery)` is false, and again if the renderer factory returns null. SDL_GPU and METAL factories themselves throw NotSupportedException. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/OcclusionQuery.cpp:11-27`; `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:4640-4646`; `metal/src/MetalRenderer.mm:3389-3394` |
| "Two concrete negatives: OPENGLES2 and WEBGL1 lose occlusion queries entirely" | True for those two, but the negative set is larger: SDL_GPU (no query API in vendored SDL_gpu 3.5.0), METAL (disabled until macOS proof), PORTABLEGL, STUB, and all seven 2D-only identities. Positive: OPENGLES3, OPENGL33, WEBGL2, OPENGL4, VULKAN, [retired renderers], FNA3D, WEBGPU (exact count), SOFTWARE (exact CPU count), HEADLESS (trace only). | Capability switches cited in "section H of Advanced graphics facts" |
| "The base is explicit only for multi-stream input, compiled effects and stencil ... DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 and SDL_GPU supply no override" | See G6. In particular SDL_GPU explicitly answers **false** for OcclusionQuery; [retired renderers] explicitly true. | `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2676-2717`; `directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1293-1326` |
| (missing) example constructs `OcclusionQuery` on default settings | Throws NotSupportedException on the default Reach profile. Also note `GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability(OcclusionQuery)` does NOT reflect the profile, so a `true` answer still throws under Reach. Set `graphics_.setGraphicsProfileProperty(GraphicsProfile::HiDef)` (or `CNA::ProjectGraphicsProfileEXT`) first. | `OcclusionQuery.cpp:18-24`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3400-3440` (no profile term for OcclusionQuery) |
| `ColorWriteChannels saved = gd.BlendState.ColorWriteChannels;` | No public `BlendState` member on `GraphicsDevice`; it is `gd.getBlendStateProperty().getColorWriteChannelsProperty()`. (Variable is also unused.) | `GraphicsDevice.hpp:185-194`; `BlendState.hpp:120` |
| "You must not nest queries — only one query may be active (between Begin and End) at a time per GraphicsDevice." | Enforced per object, not per device: `Begin()` throws InvalidOperationException "End must be called before Begin is called again." if that query is already in a pair, and "IsComplete must be queried before beginning this occlusion query again." if the previous Begin was never followed by an `IsComplete` observation (also new since BASE, where all four methods just forwarded to the renderer). Any device-wide single-active rule is renderer-specific. | `OcclusionQuery.cpp:72-90`; BASE `cna-base/modules/graphics/src/Xna/OcclusionQuery.cpp:1-45` |
| "If you call PixelCount before the result is ready, the driver must stall the CPU" | `getPixelCountProperty()` calls `getIsCompleteProperty()` and throws `InvalidOperationException` ("The occlusion query has not completed; query IsComplete before reading PixelCount.") when not complete; it does not stall. | `OcclusionQuery.cpp:52-62` |
| "On MSAA framebuffers the count may reflect sample coverage ... Normalise by MultiSampleCount"; "GL_SAMPLES_PASSED counts individual MSAA samples; divide by PresentationParameters.MultiSampleCount" | Only valid where the count is precise. On OPENGLES3/WEBGL2 (boolean fallback) the value is 0 or 1. New `OcclusionQuery::isPixelCountPreciseEXT()` (CNAEXT) says which you have; Vulkan precise only with the `occlusionQueryPrecise` device feature and `VK_QUERY_CONTROL_PRECISE_BIT`; OPENGL4 always precise. | `OcclusionQuery.hpp` (`getPixelCountProperty` doc, `isPixelCountPreciseEXT`); `VulkanRenderer.cpp:4636-4642,14856-14857,19119-19122`; `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:2808-2815` |
| Vulkan note: "VkQueryPool with VK_QUERY_TYPE_OCCLUSION ... VK_QUERY_RESULT_WITH_AVAILABILITY_BIT" | Verified. | `VulkanRenderer.cpp:23310,23501` |

Claims verified OK: `OcclusionQuery(GraphicsDevice&)`, `Begin/End`, `getIsCompleteProperty`, `getPixelCountProperty`; `Viewport::Project(source, projection, view, world)` argument order; `SpriteBatch::Begin(SpriteSortMode, BlendState)`; `BlendState noColor = BlendState::Opaque; noColor.setColorWriteChannelsProperty(ColorWriteChannels::None)` (fresh mutable copy); Vulkan implementation note.
Rewrite notes: add HiDef prerequisite and `isPixelCountPreciseEXT()`; replace the support section with the per-renderer facts; keep the ring-buffer pattern (it does not trip the new "IsComplete must be queried" rule because each query is observed before it is re-begun).

#### 62 Multiple Render Targets (MRT)
Classification: **substantial rewrite** (default-profile example throws; cube-face list and binding-constructor prose wrong; format note names retired renderers)

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|

| Example: `gd.SetRenderTargets(targets)` with three bindings on default settings | Throws `NotSupportedException` ("SetRenderTargets: 3 render targets exceeds GraphicsProfile.Reach's own maximum of 1") because the default profile is Reach; set HiDef (max 4). BASE had no enforcement outside DIRECTX9. Also throws if more than 4 (MAX_RENDERTARGET_BINDINGS). | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:5368-5392`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2655-2658` (BASE `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:1657-1661`) |
| "MRT ... requires GL_ARB_draw_buffers on the GL family" | EasyGL uses core `glDrawBuffers` (ES3/GL3+); `maxMrtTargets_` = 1 on the OPENGLES2 profile, else min(GL limits, 4). WEBGL1 also 1. No ARB extension. | `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:5770,5781,7342-7350` |
| "Two concrete negatives: OPENGLES2 and WEBGL1 lose MRT entirely" | Also false: METAL (any count > 1 throws NotSupportedException), PORTABLEGL (no render targets at all), STUB, all seven 2D-only. | "Cube faces inside an MRT set are unimplemented on eight ... DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, [retired], the EasyGL GL family, SDL_GPU, [retired] and OPENGL4." | [retired] and [retired] are gone. Cube faces in a multi-target set are REFUSED on DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU (NotSupportedException) and HEADLESS (runtime_error), and METAL refuses any multi-target set. They are IMPLEMENTED on the EasyGL family (OPENGLES2 excepted, single target), OPENGL4 (test `opengl4_rendertargetcube_mrt_test.cpp`), VULKAN (tests `vulkan_cube_mrt_binding_test.cpp`), SOFTWARE, and FNA3D (descriptor handled, up to 4). | refusals: `directx9/src/DirectX9Renderer.cpp:1293`, `directx11/src/DirectX11Renderer.cpp:1532`, `directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2275`, `sdl-gpu/src/SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:5068`, `webgpu/src/WebGPURenderer.cpp:12464`, `headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:683,700`; support: `easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:7321-7400`, `opengl4/src/OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:2405-2470`, `vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:22182-22300`, `software/src/SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:541-625`, `fna3d/src/Fna3dResources.cpp:1263-1330` |
| "Its two-argument constructor is explicit and takes a Texture* — explicit RenderTargetBinding(Texture*, int arraySlice = 0); RenderTargetBinding(Texture*, CubeMapFace)"; "Because the constructor is explicit, brace-initialising a vector of bindings from bare pointers does not compile" | Those two forms are now marked CNAEXT. The XNA-shaped constructors are `RenderTargetBinding(RenderTarget2D*)` (implicit, not explicit) and `RenderTargetBinding(RenderTargetCube*, CubeMapFace)`; a `RenderTarget2D*` therefore converts implicitly. `getArraySliceProperty()` is CNAEXT. (The example itself still compiles.) | `RenderTargetBinding.hpp:14-46`; BASE `cna-base/.../RenderTargetBinding.hpp` had only the Texture* forms |
| "CNA exposes capability queries but no public numeric maximum for simultaneous render targets" | `GraphicsDevice::GetRendererLimitEXT(CNA::RendererLimit::MaxColorAttachments)` exists; populated (known) on VULKAN, SDL_GPU (4), WEBGPU, OPENGL4; 0/unknown elsewhere. `GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability(MultipleRenderTargets)` = renderer answer AND profile ceiling > 1. | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3430-3440,3647-3648`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3270`; `VulkanRenderer.cpp:18380`; `SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:3119`; `OpenGL4Modern.cpp:1010`; `WebGPURendererModern.cpp:367` |
| "[retired] and [retired] expose explicit format promotions ... common shader-capable configurations ... defer to the framework's Color-only public gate" | [retired] retired. Non-Color render targets are now a probed per-renderer feature: Supported/Unsupported verdicts on EasyGL, VULKAN, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE; Color-only (Defer) on FNA3D, DIRECTX9, METAL, HEADLESS etc. Under Reach a non-Color preferred format silently becomes Color. Mixed formats across MRT slots: Vulkan test `vulkan_mrt_mixed_formats_test.cpp`; SDL_GPU header says "four independently writable, mixed-format color targets". | `RenderTarget2D.cpp:46-63`; `Texture.cpp:110-140`; classifiers in "section H of Advanced graphics facts" |
| "All render targets in a single MRT bind must have matching width, height and applied sample count, and the same subresource cannot occupy two slots" | Verified (EasyGL, OPENGL4 throw on each). Also: EasyGL rejects distinct per-slot `ColorWriteChannels` when the profile lacks indexed color masks. MRT + MSAA is supported (EasyGL "true MRT MSAA + resolve"; Vulkan `vulkan_mrt_msaa_test.cpp`). | `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:7373-7395`; `easygl/examples/easygl_mrt_test.cpp:12`; `vulkan/examples/vulkan_mrt_msaa_test.cpp` |

Same at BASE. | `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:808-846`; `modules/content/include/CNA/Internal/Xnb/TextureCubeContentTypeReader.hpp`; grep of `modules/content`,`modules/graphics/src`,`modules/runtime/src` for `_px` finds nothing |
