Available: VertexPositionColor, VertexPositionColorTexture, VertexPositionNormalTexture, VertexPositionTexture (+ CNAEXT skinned/tangent), or a custom declaration (tutorial 51). Static accessor is `getVertexDeclarationStatic()`, not `::VertexDeclaration`. | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/` (file list); `VertexPositionColor.hpp:52` |
| `sky->SetData(CubeMapFace::PositiveX, 0, nullptr, facePixels.data(), 0, static_cast<int>(facePixels.size() * sizeof(Color)))` | The last argument is an ELEMENT count of `Color`, "exact number ... required for the selected region" (size*size), not bytes; a byte count throws ArgumentException/ArgumentOutOfRange. | "All six faces must be square and the same power-of-two resolution. Common choices: 256, 512, 1024, 2048" | Profile-dependent: Reach max cube 512 and POT-only (`TextureCube: size N exceeds GraphicsProfile.Reach's own maximum cube size of 512`; NPOT refused on Reach); HiDef max 4096, NPOT allowed. 1024/2048 need `GraphicsProfile::HiDef`. (Also "96 MB for RGB8" at 2048 is RGBA8 arithmetic.) | `TextureCube.cpp:67-80,110-125`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2626-2630`; `RenderTargetCube.cpp:73-88` |

#### 65 MSAA Anti-Aliasing
Classification: **substantial rewrite** (a non-existent property is the core configuration call; FXAA example does not compile; renderer/readback claims stale)

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `graphics_.setMultiSampleCountProperty(4);` ("The sample count can be specified explicitly") | `GraphicsDeviceManager` has no such property (nor at BASE). Only `setPreferMultiSamplingProperty(bool)` exists. When true, the manager requests **8** samples unless the app changed `PresentationParameters.MultiSampleCount` in the `PreparingDeviceSettings` event (`pp.setMultiSampleCountProperty(n)`); when false it forces 0. The renderer clamps. | `GraphicsDeviceManager.hpp:152-158`; `GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:606-614`; `GraphicsDeviceManager.hpp:80`; `PresentationParameters.hpp:138-144` |
| "CNA picks the highest supported count up to and including the requested value" | Renderer-specific clamp: Vulkan intersects framebuffer color+depth sample counts; SDL_GPU clamps; WebGPU only knows 4x (probe); DX11/12 probe up to 4; GDI only opt-in 4x CPU; SDL_RENDERER ignores; METAL/PORTABLEGL/2D-only false. | capability switches (see Facts) |
| "Reading back the achieved sample count ... is the answer you can trust"; "pp.getMultiSampleCountProperty() reports what the driver actually gave us" | Only true on renderers overriding `GetAppliedMultiSampleCountEXT`: VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU, GDI, EasyGL, DIRECTX12. All others (FNA3D, DX9, DX11, OPENGL4, METAL, SOFTWARE, HEADLESS...) echo the request. | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3851-3852,4443-4444`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2586-2589`; overrides: `vulkan/.../VulkanRenderer.hpp:3400`, `sdl-gpu/.../SdlGpuRenderer.hpp:3173`, `webgpu/.../WebGPURenderer.hpp:1625`, `gdi/.../GdiRenderer.hpp:73`, `easygl/.../EasyGLRenderer.hpp:2050`, `directx12/.../DirectX12Renderer.hpp:198` |
| "Ask GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability(MultiSampleAntiAliasing) ... most base entries fail open. DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12 and SDL_GPU supply no override" | See G6; DX11/12/SDL_GPU/VULKAN/EasyGL/FNA3D/WEBGPU answer from real probes. Only DIRECTX9 inherits the permissive default. | G6 |
| "OPENGLES3 and VULKAN implement MSAA, while SDL_RENDERER accepts the flag and ignores it" | Verified for SDL_RENDERER (`ApplyMultiSampleCount` logs and ignores). OPENGLES2/WEBGL1 report false (no ES2 multisample). METAL reports false. | `sdl-renderer/src/SdlRenderer.cpp:571-586`; `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6122-6140`; `MetalPolicy.hpp:171-186` |
| "GL family ... enables MSAA via SDL_GL_SetAttribute(SDL_GL_MULTISAMPLEBUFFERS ...) and glEnable(GL_MULTISAMPLE)" | The request travels in the platform GL context description (`IPlatformGlContext` `multisampleSamples`), honoured by the SDL3, SDL2 and X11 (GLX_SAMPLES) platform back ends; SDL attributes are only one implementation. | `modules/platform/include/CNA/Platform/IPlatformGlContext.hpp:55`; `platform/src/Sdl3/Sdl3GraphicsServices.cpp:154-157`; `platform/src/Sdl2/Sdl2Platform.cpp:290-293`; `platform/src/X11/X11GraphicsServices.cpp:441` |
| "The VULKAN renderer — creates the swap chain images with the requested VkSampleCountFlagBits" | Swapchain images stay single-sample; Vulkan keeps a separate multisampled color+depth target and resolves. Capability is the intersection of `framebufferColorSampleCounts` and `framebufferDepthSampleCounts`. | `VulkanRenderer.cpp:3081-3090` |
| "FXAA ... Works on any renderer with render-target support, including SDL_RENDERER" and the `SamplerState::LinearClamp` call | SDL_RENDERER throws on any custom SpriteBatch Effect ("no programmable shader stage"). A ShaderEffect FXAA needs `CustomEffects` and the renderer's shader dialect (G3). Also `SpriteBatch::Begin` takes `const SamplerState*`, `const DepthStencilState*`, `const RasterizerState*`: the call must be `Begin(SpriteSortMode::Immediate, BlendState::Opaque, &SamplerState::LinearClamp, nullptr, nullptr, fxaaEffect_.get())`; as written it does not compile. | `sdl-renderer/src/SdlRenderer.cpp:97-102`; `SpriteBatch.hpp:145-238` |
| (omission) | TARGET ships a supported FXAA: `CNA::Graphics::FxaaPass` (CNA_CNAEXT), `isSupported()` = `CustomEffects` && shader compiled; and `RenderPipeline` wraps scene-target + passes. | `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/FxaaPass.hpp:71-77`; `modules/graphics-ext/src/FxaaPass.cpp:80-85` |
| G4 Alpha.1 box, G2 footer | See G4, G2 | |

Claims verified OK: `setPreferMultiSamplingProperty`, `setPreferredBackBufferWidthProperty/HeightProperty`, `GraphicsDeviceManager(Game*)`, `getPresentationParametersProperty().getMultiSampleCountProperty()`, `BasicEffect::VertexColorEnabled` field + `setLightingEnabledProperty/setWorldProperty/...`, `VertexPositionColor::getVertexDeclarationStatic()`, `Update(GameTime&)`/`Draw(const GameTime&)` signatures, `ApplyChanges()`.
Rewrite notes: replace the configuration section with Prefer + `PreparingDeviceSettings`/PresentationParameters and per-renderer clamp table; fix FXAA snippet; point to `FxaaPass`; only trust readback on the six overriding renderers, elsewhere query `SupportsCapability(MultiSampleAntiAliasing)`.

Classification: **small fix** (plus strong recommendation to cross-link the new engine-layer BloomPass)

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "Watch out for [retired], which accepts a ShaderEffect and silently ignores it, so a bloom pass there composites nothing" | [retired] retired. Current accept-and-ignore cases: HEADLESS (records, draws nothing). SOFTWARE/FNA3D/METAL/PORTABLEGL report `CustomEffects` false; DIRECTX9 has no override (no dialect declared). | `docs/removed-renderers.md`; `headless/src/HeadlessRenderer.cpp:722-738`; G3 |
| "the thirteen 2D-only renderers do not" | See G1 | |
| "CNA's BlendState::Additive sets SourceBlend = One and DestinationBlend = One, which simply adds the RGB values" | `BlendState::Additive` = color/alpha source `SourceAlpha`, color/alpha destination `One` (XNA definition). Additive still adds, but scaled by source alpha. (`Blend::One/One` would be a custom state.) Same at BASE. | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/BlendState.cpp:9` |
| "This portable bloom chain is LDR. The EasyGL/Vulkan ... defer to the framework's SurfaceFormat::Color-only gate ... [retired] promotes HdrBlendable/Rgba64 and [retired] promotes Single" | [retired] retired. HDR chain is possible on HiDef with renderers that probe float targets (EasyGL profiles that pass the probe, VULKAN, [retired renderers], OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE); ask `GraphicsDevice::SupportsCapability(FloatRenderTargets)` / `(HalfFloatRenderTargets)` first; `RenderTarget2D(..., SurfaceFormat::HdrBlendable, ...)` silently yields Color where unsupported or on Reach (check `getFormatProperty()`). | `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3403-3436`; `RenderTarget2D.cpp:46-63`; `Texture.cpp:110-140` |
| "Neither constructor throws on a compile failure -- this is the only signal." | See G7 | |
| G4 Alpha.1 box | See G4 | |
| (new since BASE) | `CNA::Graphics::BloomPass` (extract with soft knee, half-res pyramid up to 8 levels, additive composite), `RenderPipeline`, `TonemapPass`, `FxaaPass`, `PostProcessChain` exist under `CNA_CNAEXT`; `BloomPass::isSupported()` = `CustomEffects` and all four shaders compiled, otherwise it copies its input. Portable shader packages select GLSL ES/desktop GLSL/SPIR-V/WGSL by `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`; renderers without a matching variant (DX11/DX12: HLSL only via string ctor, no package selection) get the copy-through. | `BloomPass.hpp:29-140`; `RenderPipeline.hpp:60-120`; `easygl/src/EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6779-6800`; `vulkan/src/VulkanRenderer.cpp:3158-3172` |

#### 68 Terrain Rendering
Classification: **substantial rewrite** (code as written does not compile / does not run on default settings, and the limits it discusses are now enforced)

| Quote from tutorial | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| G1 list, G2 footer | See G1, G2 | |
| `IndexBuffer(gd, IndexElementSize::ThirtyTwoBits, ...)` | NotSupportedException on the default Reach profile ("Thirty-two-bit index buffers are not supported by the Reach graphics profile."); also 32-bit *user* indices. Needs HiDef. New since BASE. | `IndexBuffer.cpp:43-48`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1295-1305` |
| "a 4097×4097 heightmap ... is sufficient" / one `VertexBuffer` and one `IndexBuffer` for the whole heightmap | Single vertex or index buffer is capped at 67,108,863 bytes (about 2.09M vertices at 32 B), a draw at 1,048,575 primitives (HiDef) / 65,535 (Reach), and a texture at 2048 (Reach) / 4096 (HiDef) - so 4097 cannot even be loaded as a Texture2D. Chunk the terrain. | `VertexBuffer.cpp:202-208`; `IndexBuffer.cpp:50-56`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1293`; `Texture2D.cpp:63-72` |
| `effect_->setTextureProperty(*grassTexture_);` | `BasicEffect::setTextureProperty(Texture2D*)` takes a pointer: `grassTexture_.get()`. (Same at BASE.) | `BasicEffect.hpp` (`setTextureProperty(Texture2D* value)`) |
| `CalculateNormals(); // fills vertices[i].Normal in-place` while the function writes an undeclared `normals_` member | Tutorial code defect: normals are never copied into the vertices, so lighting sees zero normals. | tutorial text |
| Blend fragment shader + "Tutorial 52 ShaderEffect (the blend shader)" but `Draw` uses `BasicEffect` with only the grass texture | Shader is never wired in; either use `ShaderEffect` or drop the shader section. | tutorial text |
| "GPU tessellation — ... not yet exposed directly in CNA's Effect system but accessible through raw GL calls on the GL family" | No tessellation stage and no raw-GL escape hatch in the public API (only the `VertexElementUsage` enumerator exists). Compute shaders exist (CNAEXT `GraphicsCapability::ComputeShaders`) as the modern alternative. | grep of `modules/graphics`, `graphics-ext`, `renderers` for tessellation; `GraphicsCapability.hpp:189` |
| `BufferUsage::None` "static ... optimised for GPU reads" | XNA meaning: None = read/write allowed, WriteOnly = GetData forbidden ("Calling GetData on a resource that was created with BufferUsage.WriteOnly is not supported."); neither implies static/dynamic. | `VertexBuffer.cpp:100` |

Claims verified OK: `Texture2D(const std::string&, GraphicsDevice&)` (CNAEXT), `GetData(Color*, n)`, `Color::getRProperty()`, `VertexPositionNormalTexture` fields and 3-arg ctor, `getVertexDeclarationStatic()`, `IndexBuffer::SetData(const uint32_t*, n)`, `getVertexCountProperty()`, `MathHelper::Lerp`.

### Slice 110-117 and 128 (glTF, CNJ, animation, morph, PBR, CNAEXT, post-process, devices, compiled effects)

All paths are TARGET-relative (`/rv/tmp/libcna-v2/cna-target`). "pre-existing" means the same defect is
already true at BASE (alpha.1), so it is a site error rather than a delta. Site pages were read from
`docs/tutorials/*.html`. Site-wide, every tutorial footer says "50 renderer identities across 46 implementation
families"; TARGET has 25 identities over 21 families (`cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-27`,
`modules/renderers/` has 21 family directories) - not repeated below.

#### 110 Loading glTF 2.0 Models at Runtime
Classification: small fix

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `Model* house = Content->Load<Model>("models/house");` and `house->Draw(...)` | `ContentManager::Load<T>` returns `T` by value; a `Game` has no `Content` member, use `getContentProperty()` (returns `ContentManager&`). Correct: `Model house = getContentProperty().Load<Model>("models/house"); house.Draw(world, view, projection);` (pre-existing) | `modules/content/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Content/ContentManager.hpp:472-473`; `modules/runtime/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Game.hpp:91`; test usage `modules/content/tests/CNA/Internal/GltfImport/GltfExternalBufferTests.cpp:128,155`; `Model::Draw` `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/Model.hpp:401` |
| Load-order table "1 .xnb always wins; 2 literal path; 3 .cnj; 4 .cnj/.gltf/.glb" | New tier: after `.xnb` comes `.cnb` (CNA compiled container), and a literal name ending `.cnb` is also honoured, both ahead of the literal path/`.cnj`/reader extensions. Order: `.xnb` -> `.cnb` -> literal path -> `.cnj` -> reader extensions (Model: `.cnj`, `.gltf`, `.glb`). A stray `.cnb` therefore shadows a `.glb` | `ContentManager.hpp:509-516` (xnb), `:527-535` (cnb), `:538-547` (literal .cnb), `:808-848` (ResolveAssetPath: literal 822, .cnj 833, reader extensions 840); `modules/content/src/Xna/ContentManager.cpp:4300-4306` |
| "Draco mesh compression - Optional - needs libdraco at build time" | `CNA_ENABLE_DRACO` defaults ON and builds the pinned `third_party/draco` submodule (Draco 1.5.7); defaults OFF only under Emscripten; `CNA_USE_SYSTEM_DRACO` switches to a system package; with it OFF, compressed primitives throw at import | `modules/CMakeLists.txt:66-82,125-149` |
| "Use the offline converter when you need a scale multiplier ... cna_tool_gltf_to_cnj" (only tool named) | Still true (unit scale is hard-wired to 1.0f at runtime). TARGET adds `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnb ... --unit-scale` and the `cna-content build` pipeline (ModelProcessor accepts only `generateChildAssets`, no scale parameter) | runtime 1.0f: `ContentManager.cpp:3084,3569,4011,4252`; `tools/gltf_to_cnb/gltf_to_cnb.cpp:108,126,204`; `modules/content/src/Pipeline/ModelContentPipeline.cpp:344-356`; `tools/gltf_to_cnj/gltf_to_cnj.cpp:1680` |

Claims verified OK: one `Load<Model>()` for `.gltf`/`.glb`; only glTF 2.0 accepted (`ContentManager.cpp:2926`, "only glTF 2.0 is supported"); all mesh groups combined into one Model with `getSkinsEXTProperty()` and first skin also on `Tag` (`Model.hpp:279-291`); `getGltfImportReportEXTProperty()` (`Model.hpp:302`); rigid clips via `ModelAnimationsEXT` (`AnimationPlayer.hpp:200-207`) and mixed skin+rigid drop with a named diagnostic (`ContentManager.cpp:3187-3227`); external-URI containment and the `extensionsRequired` gate exist (`ContentManager.cpp:2907,2937`); `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnj` is built unconditionally (`CMakeLists.txt:391`, `cmake/ToolGltfToCnj.cmake:12-22`); usage line `<input> <outDir> <baseName> [unitScale]` (`gltf_to_cnj.cpp:1680`).
Note (CNA-side stale comment, not a site mismatch): `ContentManager.cpp:2833-2838` still says "only the file's FIRST mesh group is imported"; the implementation loops over every group (`:3084`, `:3569`).

#### 111 The CNJ Format and the gltf_to_cnj Tool
Classification: substantial rewrite (the framing of what CNA content formats/tools exist is now wrong)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "It is not a compiled binary container and it is not a content pipeline" | CNA now has a compiled binary container, CNB (`.cnb`, magic `CNB\x1A`, little-endian, per-chunk CRC32C, optional zstd), a `.cnj`->`.cnb` compiler, and a build-time content pipeline behind the `cna-content` tool | `modules/content/include/CNA/Content/Cnb/CnbFormat.hpp:74`; `modules/content/include/CNA/Content/Cnb/CnjToCnb.hpp`; `cmake/ToolContentPipeline.cmake:8-21` (`cna_content_tool`, OUTPUT_NAME `cna-content`); tools `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnj_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_source_to_cnb`, `cna_tool_cnb_info` (`cmake/ToolGltfToCnb.cmake:5`, `ToolCnjToCnb.cmake:6`, `ToolSourceToCnb.cmake:7`, `ToolCnbInfo.cmake:5`) |
| ".xnb - Read-only - CNA reads it and will never write it - there is no ContentImporter or ContentProcessor anywhere in the project" | False. `cna-content build --format xnb` writes XNA-compatible XNB (`--xnb-platform`, `--xnb-version 4\|5`, `--xnb-compress none\|lzx\|lz4`, `--xnb-profile`, `--xnb-reader-names`), and both a CNA-native `CNA::Content::Pipeline::ContentImporter/ContentProcessor` and an XNA-compat `Microsoft::Xna::Framework::Content::Pipeline::ContentImporter/ContentProcessor` (+ EffectImporter, FontDescriptionImporter, WavImporter, Mp3Importer, ...) exist | `tools/content/content.cpp:281-300`; `modules/content/src/Pipeline/XnbOutputContentPipeline.cpp:379-383,968`; `modules/content/include/CNA/Content/Pipeline/ContentPipeline.hpp:1052,1121`; `modules/content-pipeline/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Content/Pipeline/ContentImporter.hpp:28`, `ContentProcessor.hpp:34` (37 headers in that directory) |
| "Formats CNA can read side by side" table (`.xnb`, `.cnj`, native files) | Needs a `.cnb` row (read+write, self-describing by header type id, precedence right after `.xnb`, ahead of `.cnj`/native sources) and a note that `.fx`/`.fxb` are build inputs to `.xnb` only | `ContentManager.hpp:509-547`; `modules/content/src/Pipeline/EffectContentPipeline.cpp:66-147`; `tools/content/content.cpp:330-338` |
| Reader-extension map (Model `.cnj,.gltf,.glb`; Texture2D `.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.bmp,.gif,.tga,.tif,.tiff,.qoi`; TextureCube `.dds`; SoundEffect `.wav`; Song ...; Video ...; SpriteFont/Effect/Texture3D/Curve/AnimationClip `.cnj` only) | Verified correct. | "When you need the converter ... 1. separate assets per mesh group 2. not authored in metres ... `cna_tool_gltf_to_cnj`" | Still valid for `.cnj`; a `.cnb` route exists (`cna_tool_gltf_to_cnb ... --unit-scale`, or `cna-content build` on a `.glb`/`.gltf`, which generates child assets per skin group with `generateChildAssets`) | `tools/gltf_to_cnb/gltf_to_cnb.cpp:87,126`; `ModelContentPipeline.cpp:104-111,344-356` |
| `Model* hero = Content->Load<Model>("hero"); hero->Draw(...)`; `AnimationClip walk = Content->Load<AnimationClip>("hero_Walk");`; `Content->RegisterCnjLoader<GameData>(...)` | Same accessor/return-type defect as 110 (pre-existing): use `getContentProperty()`, `Load<Model>` returns `Model` by value. `AnimationClip` is `using AnimationClip = AnimationClipEXT` (returns by value OK). `RegisterCnjLoader<T>(typeName, CnjLoaderFn<T>)` signature and throw semantics are correct | `ContentManager.hpp:313,343-388`; `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/AnimationPlayer.hpp:33` |

#### 112 Skeletal Animation from glTF
Classification: small fix (one real correctness bug in the sample)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `Model* hero = Content->Load<Model>("hero"); auto* skinning = static_cast<SkinningData*>(hero->getTagProperty()); if (skinning == nullptr) {...}` and the `HeroActor` sample (`Model* model_ = nullptr; model_ = content.Load<Model>("hero"); static_cast<SkinningData*>(model_->getTagProperty())`) | (a) Same by-value/`Content->` defect as 110. (b) Correctness bug: `Tag` holds `SkinningData` for skinned models but `ModelAnimationsEXT` for unskinned animated ones, so `static_cast<SkinningData*>` returns a non-null wrong-type pointer for a rigid model and the `nullptr` guard never fires. Both types derive `System::Object` with a virtual `GetTypeName`, so use `dynamic_cast` (Tutorial 113 already does for morph data). Same for the `static_cast<ModelAnimationsEXT*>` sample | `AnimationPlayer.hpp:35-49` (SkinningData : System::Object), `:200-207` (ModelAnimationsEXT); `Model.hpp:237,243` (`getTagProperty/setTagProperty` on `System::Object*`); `ContentManager.cpp:3209-3229` |
| `GetSkinTransforms()` "InverseBindPose already composed with the world transform" etc. | Unchanged; API verified | `AnimationPlayer.hpp:107-140` |

Claims verified OK: `AnimationPlayer(const SkinningData&)`, `StartClip`, `Update(TimeSpan, bool relativeToCurrentTime, bool loop = true)`, `GetBoneTransforms/GetWorldTransforms/GetSkinTransforms`, `getCurrentPositionProperty/getCurrentClipProperty`; `AnimationClip.Duration/Tracks/Keys` fields (`SkinnedModelEXT.hpp:27-98`); `SkinnedEffect::MaxBones = 72`, `WeightsPerVertex` 1/2/4, `SetBoneTransforms(const std::vector<Matrix>&)` (`SkinnedEffect.hpp:29,327-344`; `SkinnedPbrEffect.hpp:46,437-455`); import-time union-of-key-times resampling, STEP/CUBICSPLINE evaluation and the un-imported `weights` channel (`modules/content/src/GltfImport/GltfImportCore.cpp:1795-1873`); rigid clips via `ApplyClipToBonesEXT` (`AnimationPlayer.hpp:227-228`); mixed skin+rigid boundary (`ContentManager.cpp:3187-3227`); `ModelMesh*`/`ModelMeshPart*` iteration (`ModelMeshCollection.hpp`, `ModelMesh.hpp:74,109`, `ModelMeshPart.hpp:142`). | "Stride - Byte stride of one vertex - 32, 52 or 56." and "Normal deltas are only applied for the strides that actually have a normal slot, which is all three of 32, 52 and 56." | `Stride` is any stride in the canonical table whose layout has `Normal` (Vector3, usage 0): rigid PBR 48/60 and skinned PBR 68/76/80 as well. The predicate is a query against the stride table, not a literal list | `modules/graphics/src/Xna/MorphTargetEXT.cpp:30-56`; stride table `modules/graphics/include/CNA/Internal/Graphics/VertexDeclarationFidelity.hpp:329-403,444-450` |

| Member table of `MorphTargetDataEXT` lists BaseVertexBytes, Stride, PositionDeltas, NormalDeltas, Weights, WeightTrack | Missing members: `TangentDeltas`, `RecomputeFlatNormalsEXT`, `TriangleIndicesEXT` | `modules/graphics/include/Microsoft/Xna/Framework/Graphics/MorphTargetEXT.hpp` (struct `MorphTargetDataEXT`) |

| "morph targets work unchanged with every effect and every shader on every renderer, including the 2D-only and non-shader ones ... no renderer on which the feature quietly does nothing" | Imprecise: the blend is renderer-independent CPU work, but 2D-only identities have no 3D pipeline to draw the mesh; qualify with `GraphicsCapability::ThreeD` | `modules/graphics/include/CNA/GraphicsCapability.hpp:23`; `MorphTargetEXT.cpp:234-249` |

#### 116 CNAEXT Post-Process Effects: ASCII, CRT, Depth and Colour Matrix
Classification: substantial rewrite (the renderer boundary paragraph is wrong in four ways, and the "four effects" framing is now a small part of the layer)

| Quote | Correct API/fact at TARGET | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| "These run only where custom ShaderEffects run. That is the five GL-profile identities, plus [retired], OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, [retired], DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11 and DIRECTX12. Elsewhere ... [retired] and SOFTWARE accept ... GDI, METAL, HTML_DOM and SVG_DOM throw; FNA3D ... HEADLESS records" | [retired] no longer exist. `CRTEffect`/`DepthEffect` build `ShaderEffect(device, package, fallbackVert, fallbackFrag)` from a `ShaderPackageEXT` with four fragment variants (GLSL ES, desktop GLSL, Vulkan SPIR-V, WGSL). A variant runs only where `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT` accepts it: EasyGL (desktop GLSL for OPENGL33; GLSL ES for OPENGLES2/3 and WEBGL1/2), OPENGL4 (desktop GLSL), VULKAN and SDL_GPU (SPIR-V), WEBGPU (WGSL). [retired renderers] do NOT run them: their `ShaderEffect` intake is HLSL via `D3DCompile`, no HLSL variant exists, and the GLSL ES fallback text fails to compile (error logged, `GetCompileErrorEXT()`). SOFTWARE reports `CustomEffects == false` (source accepted, never executed); METAL and FNA3D report false; HEADLESS reports false | `modules/graphics-ext/src/CRTEffect.cpp:17-22`; `modules/graphics-ext/src/PostProcessShaderPackages.cpp:211-221,244-251`; `modules/graphics-ext/src/ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:454-462,525-544`; `EasyGLRenderer.cpp:6779-6795`; `OpenGL4Renderer.cpp:1577-1584`; `VulkanRenderer.cpp:3158-3171`; `SdlGpuRenderer.cpp:2938-2951`; `WebGPURendererModern.cpp:215-221`; `modules/renderers/directx11/src/D3D11EffectRenderer.cpp:41-65`, `directx9/src/D3D9EffectRenderer.cpp:35-57`, `directx12/src/DirectX12Renderer.cpp:2954-2961`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/ShaderEffect.cpp:24,40-52`; `SoftwareRenderer2DState.cpp:454-458`; `MetalPolicy.hpp:182`; `Fna3dRenderer.cpp:979-980`; `HeadlessRenderer.cpp:703-708` |

#### Facts learned for the fact sheet

Identity and capability surface
- 25 public renderer identities (`cmake/RendererIdentities.cmake:24-27`); retired list with reserved C-ABI values, next free value 52 (`:35-43`, `docs/removed-renderers.md`). 21 renderer family directories (`modules/renderers/`).
- `GraphicsCapability` has 19 members: ThreeD, DepthStencilBuffer, MultiSampleAntiAliasing, MultipleRenderTargets, AnisotropicFiltering, WireFrame, OcclusionQuery, CustomEffects, Texture3D, MultiStreamVertexInput, Instancing, StencilBuffer, AdditiveBlending, CompiledEffects, FloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatRenderTargets, HalfFloatTextureLinearFiltering, ComputeShaders, IndirectDraw (`modules/graphics/include/CNA/GraphicsCapability.hpp:14-207`). Default `SupportsCapability` is permissive except MultiStream, CompiledEffects, float RTs (`IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3773-3800`). A richer snapshot exists: `GraphicsDevice::GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()`, `GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT()`, `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT`, `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT` (`GraphicsDevice.hpp:1506,1547,1561,1599,1650,1660`; `RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:25-92` with 32 `RendererFeature` entries).

Compiled effects
- Ten families can report `CompiledEffects == true`: FNA3D always; opt-in (all default OFF): EasyGL (5 identities), OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, VULKAN, WEBGPU, DIRECTX9, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12, SOFTWARE (option list and citations under Tutorial 128). CNA's own `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md` table omits OPENGL4, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE and DIRECTX9 (code has all four; `plans/plan_fx.md` says "seven backends" and also still lists SOFTWARE/WEBGPU as unsupported - stale).
- `Effect(GraphicsDevice&, bytes)` is not a stub: it validates, checks `SupportsCapability(CompiledEffects)`, calls `IGraphicsRenderer::CreateCompiledEffect` (`Effect.cpp:780-824`). XNB `EffectReader` bounded at 64 MiB (`EffectContentTypeReader.cpp:21,38`).
- Build-time `.fx` route exists via external `fxc` (`EffectCompilerService.cpp`), Windows-only compiler, `fx_2_0`; the XNB Effect writer refuses non-Windows targets (`EffectCompilerService.cpp:30-38`).

Content
- `.cnb` tier order: `.xnb` -> `.cnb` -> literal -> `.cnj` -> reader extensions (`ContentManager.hpp:509-547,808-848`). CNB magic bytes 43 4E 42 1A (`CnbFormat.hpp:74`). `cna-content build` CLI (`tools/content/content.cpp:281-350`). Runtime glTF unit scale remains hard-coded 1.0f (`ContentManager.cpp:3084,3569,4011,4252`). Draco default ON except Emscripten (`modules/CMakeLists.txt:74-82`).

Engine layer / CNAEXT
- `CNA_CNAEXT` default OFF (`CMakeLists.txt:176`); 98 gated headers in `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics`; engine version 18; 19 PostProcessPass subclasses; master include `CNAEXT.hpp`. `CNA_DEVICES` default OFF (`CMakeLists.txt:177`); `cna_devices_ext` routes through the platform contract; platforms selectable: SDL3 (default), SDL2, X11, WAYLAND, HEADLESS, TERMINAL (POSIX), WIN32 (Windows) (`cmake/PlatformSelection.cmake:15-52`).
- Per-renderer engine-layer support (from renderer overrides): shadow sampling and IBL honoured by EasyGL family, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU only; `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT` true for EasyGL, OPENGL4, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU, DIRECTX11, DIRECTX12; compute and indirect draw implemented by EasyGL (runtime-probed), OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU ([retired renderers] only delegate to the false default); shader-language intake: EasyGL GLSL desktop/ES, OPENGL4 GLSL desktop, VULKAN and SDL_GPU SPIR-V, WEBGPU WGSL, no other renderer overrides it (`SupportsShaderLanguageEXT` overrides listed in Tutorial 116 evidence); [retired renderers] ShaderEffect source is HLSL (`D3DCompile`). CNA's own `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md` matrix is stale on OPENGL4 (says no shadow sampling/IBL; code says true) and on "49 identities".
- PBR: nine full families + SOFTWARE reduced; Metal factor-only for specular maps and refuses vertex-coloured PBR; FNA3D/PORTABLEGL refuse PBR draws (see Tutorial 114 evidence).
- ColorMatrixEffect: GDI and SOFTWARE execute, PORTABLEGL refuses (see Tutorial 116).
- `CNA_STRICT_XNA_API` mechanism unchanged (`CNAHelper.hpp:22-26`); the shipped positive compile check only exercises `Microsoft::Devices`/`Sensors` (`tools/devices/StrictXnaApiSurfaceCheck.cpp`).

Stale CNA comments found (do not trust when writing pages): `PbrEffect.hpp:29-37` ("not image-based lighting", "WebGPU unskinned only"), `SkinnedPbrEffect.hpp:35-38` (WebGPU skinning), `ContentManager.cpp:2833-2838` (first mesh group only), `ContentManager.hpp` unaffected.


## Proposed new pages/tutorials

Only features that TARGET genuinely adds and that have executable-shaped evidence in the source (an example/test that drives the API the outline names). "Verified sequence" = the call sequence appears in a checked-in example or test at the cited path. None of these were run; each outline states its prerequisites so a writer can gate it. **Engine-layer items (N4-N9) sit behind `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`, which is OFF by default, is built by no CI workflow and is documented as "still moving" (engine layer revision 18, "Not an ABI guarantee", `modules/graphics-ext/include/CNA/Graphics/EngineLayerVersion.hpp:18-26`): publish them under an explicit "experimental" banner, pinned to the CNA revision.**

| # | Page / tutorial | Why it is new at TARGET | Verified outline | Prerequisites and renderer scope | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| N1 | **Graphics profiles: Reach vs HiDef** (tutorial, and a prerequisite box linked from tutorials 38-40, 61-70, 110-114) | TARGET enforces XNA profile ceilings in the shared device layer (BASE enforced them only on DIRECTX9) and the default is **Reach**: 32-bit indices, >65,535 primitives per draw, more than one render target, `OcclusionQuery`, Texture3D, cubes above 512 or non-power-of-two, 2048+ textures, float/HDR/packed formats, separate alpha blend and `GetBackBufferData` all throw or degrade | 1. What Reach/HiDef mean and the default; 2. select `graphics_.setGraphicsProfileProperty(GraphicsProfile::HiDef)` before device creation, or `CNA::SetProjectGraphicsProfileEXT(...)` (or constructing a `CNA::ProjectGraphicsProfileEXT` object, whose constructor calls it) before the `Game`/`GraphicsDeviceManager` is created; 3. table of ceilings (see fork facts F1 in the 61-70 audit, reproduced in section "Advanced graphics facts"); 4. errors you will see (`NotSupportedException` texts); 5. `SupportsCapability(MultipleRenderTargets)` = renderer AND profile > 1; `GetMaxRenderTargetsForProfileEXT` | none (all 3D renderers) | `modules/runtime/src/ProjectGraphicsProfile.cpp:12-30`; `modules/runtime/src/GraphicsDeviceManager.cpp:52,606-616`; `modules/graphics/src/Xna/GraphicsDevice.cpp:1281-1347,3433-3437,5368-5392`; `IndexBuffer.cpp:43-57`; `OcclusionQuery.cpp:18-24`; `Texture.cpp:110-202`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2615-2657` |
| N2 | **Reading what your renderer can do** (reference page + short tutorial) | New `RendererCapabilityProfile` (32 features, 22 limits, per-`SurfaceFormat` usage masks) plus `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT`, `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`, `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT`, `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT`, `SupportsSurfaceFormatAsRenderTargetEXT`; text report for diagnostics | 1. `device.SupportsCapability(GraphicsCapability::X)` (19 members) and its derived answers; 2. `device.GetRendererCapabilityProfileEXT()` (feature -> Unknown/Unsupported/Supported/Restricted, limits with `known` flag, format masks); 3. `device.GetRendererCapabilityReportEXT()` for logs (do not parse); 4. ask "which shader dialect?" with `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(ShaderLanguageEXT::SpirV, ShaderStageEXT::Fragment)`; 5. caveat: Vulkan/SDL_GPU report all six `ShaderDialect*` features Unsupported because SPIR-V has no feature entry (fact sheet A.4 quirk 1) | none | `modules/graphics/include/CNA/RendererCapabilityProfile.hpp:25-192`; `GraphicsDevice.hpp:1506,1547-1638`; `GraphicsDevice.cpp:3378-3403,3521-3578`; C ABI `cna_graphics_device_get_renderer_feature_support_ext` etc. (`docs/renderer-capability-profiles.md`, orientation) |
| N3 | **Getting your shaders into an XNB: `.fxb`, `.fx` and `cna-content`** (tutorial companion to 128) | New build-time route; tutorial 111 and 45 currently say no pipeline exists | 1. Choose: pre-compiled `.fxb` (no compiler) or `.fx` source (external legacy fxc `fx_2_0`, DirectX SDK June 2010, `d3dx9_43.dll` + `D3DCompiler_43.dll` beside it); 2. run `cna-content build <Content dir> -o <out> --format xnb [--fx-compiler <fxc.exe> [--fx-compiler-launcher wine]]` (order: `--fx-compiler` > `CNA_FXC` env > `-DCNA_FXC_EXECUTABLE` > PATH); 3. processor parameters `profile` (reach/hidef), `defines`, `debug` in `.cna-content.json`; 4. `--format cnb` cannot carry an Effect; the XNB writer refuses non-Windows platform payloads; 5. load with `content.Load<std::shared_ptr<Effect>>("Effects/Bloom")` and guard with `SupportsCapability(CompiledEffects)`; 6. errors (`ContentLoadException` wrapping `NotSupportedException`/`ArgumentException`); 7. caveat that the `.fx` route is unverified against genuine Microsoft fxc (CNA's own doc) | `cna-content` built (`cmake/ToolContentPipeline.cmake`); a compiled-effect-capable renderer build (E3) | `tools/content/content.cpp:283-345,442,500,622-645`; `modules/content-pipeline/src/EffectCompilerService.cpp:1-60,960-990`; `modules/content/src/Pipeline/EffectContentPipeline.cpp:25-147`; `XnbAssetTypeWriters.cpp:1243-1259` |
| N4 | **Post-processing with `RenderPipeline`** (HDR scene target, bloom, tonemapping, FXAA; replaces the hand-rolled bloom in tutorial 66 as the recommended path, keeps 66 as the portable/LDR route) | New engine layer | Verified sequence (`modules/graphics-ext/examples/cnaext_render_pipeline_test.cpp:201-222,251-263,323-329`): `CNA::Graphics::RenderPipeline pipeline(device); pipeline.resize(w,h); auto& s = pipeline.getSettings(); s.setHDREnabled(true); s.setBloomEnabled(true); s.setBloomThreshold(0.5f); s.setBloomIntensity(1.5f); s.setFXAAEnabled(true); s.setTonemappingMode(TonemappingMode::Aces); s.setRenderQuality(RenderQuality::High); s.applyRenderQualityPresetEXT(); pipeline.begin(Color::Black); /* draw scene */ pipeline.end();` Then: scene format choice HdrBlendable > Vector4 > Color (`RenderPipeline.cpp:110-124`), SSAO needs `setDepthNormalInputs(...)` from `DepthNormalPrepass`, `addUserPass(PostProcessPass*)`, unsupported passes copy through (`RenderPipeline.hpp:291`) | `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`; shader passes need `CustomEffects` + a package variant the renderer accepts: EasyGL x5, OPENGL4, VULKAN, WEBGPU, SDL_GPU (libshaderc build); **not** [retired renderers], METAL, FNA3D, SOFTWARE, HEADLESS, PORTABLEGL, 2D-only (CNAEXT inventory section 6); HDR needs HiDef + float-RT capability | `RenderPipeline.hpp:60-147`; `BloomPass.hpp`; `TonemapPass.hpp`; `FxaaPass.hpp` |
| N5 | **Shadows: `ShadowMap`, `CascadedShadowMap`, receivers** (replaces the "shadow mapping needs a hand-written effect" premise of tutorial 59) | New generation classes (gated) plus always-compiled receiver interface `IShadowReceiverEXT` on Basic/Skinned/Pbr/SkinnedPbr | Verified sequences: directional map (`cnaext_shadowmap_test.cpp:294-302`) `ShadowMap sm(device, ShadowQuality::High); sm.begin(DirectionalLightEXT, BoundingBox); /* draw casters */ sm.end(); effect.setShadowMapEXT(sm.getShadowTexture()); effect.setShadowFilterRadiusEXT(sm.getFilterRadius()); effect.setShadowsEnabledEXT(true);` cascades (`cnaext_csm_test.cpp:171-189`) `CascadedShadowMap cs(device, ShadowQuality::Medium, 3); cs.update(sun, view, proj); for i < cs.getCascadeCount(): cs.begin(i); draw casters; cs.end(); cs.applyToReceiver(effect); effect.setShadowsEnabledEXT(true);` plus point/spot (`CubeShadowMap`, `SpotShadowMap`, `PunctualLightEXT`) | `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`; generation needs `ThreeD` + `CustomEffects` + package variant (same renderer set as N4); **sampling** is honoured only where `SupportsShadowSamplingEXT()` (EasyGL x5, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU); elsewhere the state is accepted and ignored (unshadowed image) | `ShadowMap.hpp:18-50`; `CascadedShadowMap.hpp` (`kMaxCascades = 4`); `IShadowReceiverEXT.hpp`; `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:3111-3123` |
| N6 | **Image-based lighting for PbrEffect** (replaces tutorial 114's "there is no image-based lighting" section) | `ImageBasedLightEXT` on `PbrEffect`/`SkinnedPbrEffect` (always compiled) + `EnvironmentProcessor` (gated) | Verified sequence (`cnaext_ibl_test.cpp:325-339`): `EnvironmentProcessor p(device); auto cube = p.convertEquirectangular(panorama, faceSize); auto irr = p.generateIrradiance(cube.get(), 32, 32); auto spec = p.generatePrefilteredSpecular(cube.get(), 128, 5, 64); auto lut = p.generateBrdfLut(128, 128); ImageBasedLightEXT ibl; ibl.Irradiance = irr.get(); ibl.PrefilteredSpecular = spec.get(); ibl.BrdfLut = lut.get(); ibl.PrefilteredMipCount = 5; ibl.Intensity = 1.0f; pbr.setImageBasedLightEXT(ibl);` (an invalid bundle falls back to flat ambient) | products need `CNA_CNAEXT=ON` (CPU-only processor, works on any renderer); float cubes need HiDef; honoured only where `SupportsImageBasedLightingEXT()` (EasyGL x5, OPENGL4, VULKAN, SDL_GPU, WEBGPU) | `EnvironmentProcessor.hpp`; `ImageBasedLightEXT.hpp:24-33`; `PbrEffect.hpp:533-534` |
| N7 | **Many lights: `ClusteredForwardEffect`** | up to 128 lights per fragment, CPU or compute light assignment | Verified sequence (`cnaext_clustered_lights_test.cpp:160-202`): `ClusteredForwardEffect effect(device); ClusteredLightSetEXT lights = ...; onCpu.assign(grid, view, bounds); ClusteredLightBuffer buffer(device); buffer.upload(lights, grid, onCpu); effect.getEffect()->Apply(); draw` | `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`; `isSupported()`; compute assignment needs `ComputeShaders` | `ClusteredForwardEffect.hpp:61-88` |
| N8 | **Compute shaders and GPU particles** (`ComputeShader`, `StorageBuffer`, `ParticleSystem`, `GpuInstanceCuller`, `GpuTimer`) | New | outline from `modules/graphics-ext/examples/cnaext_compute_particles_test.cpp` and `cnaext_gpu_driven_test.cpp` (writer must re-read them; only headers were verified here) | `CNA_CNAEXT=ON`; `GraphicsCapability::ComputeShaders` (EasyGL desktop GL >= 4.3 / ES >= 3.1, never WebGL; OPENGL4; VULKAN; SDL_GPU; WEBGPU), `IndirectDraw` for the culler | `ParticleSystem.cpp:265-273`; `GpuInstanceCuller.cpp:111-115` |
| N9 | **Authoring shaders for several renderers: `ShaderPackageEXT`** (replaces the single-dialect framing of tutorial 52; complements N2) | multi-language packages selected per live device, offline generator, structured diagnostics | 1. Payloads per language (`ShaderCodeEXT`); 2. `ShaderPackageEXT` (required stages, binding requirements); `package.selectFor(device)` and its deterministic diagnostic; 3. `ShaderEffect(device, package)` or with fallback sources; `GetSelectedShaderLanguageEXT()`, `GetCompileErrorEXT()`, `GetShaderDiagnosticsEXT()`; 4. offline: `python3 tools/shader_package/generate_shader_package.py <package.json> --output <X>.generated.hpp` (`--check` reproducibility gate; needs libshaderc only for Vulkan GLSL -> SPIR-V; runtime never links shaderc/DXC) | `CNA_CNAEXT=ON` for package/code constructors; languages that the engine's own packages ship: GlslDesktop, GlslEs, SpirV, Wgsl (no HLSL/MSL package exists) | `ShaderEffect.hpp:60-72`; `ShaderPackageEXT.hpp`; `tools/shader_package/README.md`; `ShaderPackageEXT.cpp:310-345` |
| N10 | **Effect paths at a glance** (decision page linked from effects.html, shader-effects.html, tutorials 52/128) | Four distinct custom-effect contracts with separate capabilities | The E4 table: stock / compiled XNA (`CompiledEffects`) / `ShaderEffect` source (`CustomEffects` + `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT`) / shader package / compute; and "what each refuses" | none | E1-E4 above |
| N11 | **Renderer feature tables generated from the capability code** (replace the hand-maintained renderer tables on 3d-rendering.html, features.html, render-targets.html) | The old tables mix retired renderers and counts that cannot be reproduced | Regenerate from the A.1 matrix (fork facts) and re-derive per release | none | capability-matrix section |
| N12 | Tutorial **"Bound resources and dynamic buffers"** (or fold into tutorial 38 rewrite) | New in-use rule: `SetData` on a currently bound vertex/index buffer or sampler-bound texture throws unless `SetDataOptions::Discard/NoOverwrite` on a `DynamicVertexBuffer` | see tutorial 38/68/70 audit rows | none | `VertexBuffer.cpp:386-402`; `IndexBuffer.cpp:411-423`; `Texture.cpp:228-254` |

Not recommended as new pages: Nova-3D (there is no Nova-3D code in the CNA repository; only three docs and a plan mention it, `docs/cnaext-nova3d.md`), avatar rendering (unchanged since BASE), devices-ext (17 headers unchanged since BASE; tutorial 117 needs correction, not a new page), a Metal compiled-effects page (no implementation).

Also recommended as **deletions/rewrites of existing pages whose whole subject is gone**: `docs/tutorials/86-[retired]-backend.html` ([retired] retired), `106-vector-renderers.html` ([retired] retired; only Direct2D/HTML_DOM-type 2D renderers remain), `104-directx-ladder.html` (only [retired renderers] remain), `72-backend-selection.html` and `docs/rendering-backends.html` (renderer tables), `108-fna3d.html`/`109-metal-macos.html` to be re-read against N-facts. These are outside the requested tutorial ranges; they are listed so the owner of those ranges can pick them up.


## Open questions

| # | Question | Why it is open / who can answer |
|---|---|---|
| O-1 | **Is TARGET going to be described as a release?** | TARGET is `next` at `009d40f5` and the product version string is still `0.1.0-alpha.1` (`CMakeLists.txt:12`). The site currently claims "documents the immutable v0.1.0-alpha.1 tag" on every page. The owner has to decide whether pages say "development snapshot of `next`" or wait for a new tag; the facts in this sheet are TARGET facts either way. |
| O-2 | **Do the cna-samples counts on index/showcase/demos still hold (63 of 86 build; 23 blocked by ".fx")?** | Not derivable from CNA source. CNA-side facts only: compiled-effect bytecode runs on 14 identities (E3) and `.fx` can be built at build time (E2); CNA commits for BloomSample, NormalMapping, PerPixelLighting, ColorReplacement and Spacewar Evolved exist (E5). Needs the `cna-samples` repository (out of scope for this sheet). |
| O-3 | **Has the `.fx` build route ever run against a genuine Microsoft fxc?** | `docs/content-pipeline.md:467-470` (CNA's doc) says no; the tests substitute or stand in for the compiler. Publish the route as "supported by design, not yet verified against genuine fxc". |
| O-4 | **Executed evidence.** | This audit read code and test registration only (no build, no test run, per the rules). Pass counts quoted from `plans/plan_fx.md`/`docs/*.md` (e.g. "23 passed", "266/266") are CNA claims. Windows/Metal-only claims ([retired renderers], METAL, Apple) rest on Wine/DXVK or cross-build evidence per those docs. Any page that says "verified" should say on what. |
| O-5 | **DIRECTX9 capability answers are inherited defaults**, not measured (no `SupportsCapability` override). Should the site list DIRECTX9 cells as "not declared" rather than "yes"? | Capability-matrix fork, A.0/A.1. A DIRECTX9 override would change the answer; ask the CNA owner whether one is planned. |
| O-6 | **Vulkan `ExecutesShaderEffectSourceEXT() == false` although VULKAN runs SPIR-V `ShaderEffect` objects** (documented as intentional: it takes SPIR-V, not the GLSL text the engine layer writes). The detailed profile therefore reports `ShaderEffectSourceExecution` unsupported and all six `ShaderDialect*` features unsupported on VULKAN and SDL_GPU. How should the site word the shader-language table? | Use `SupportsShaderLanguageEXT(SpirV, stage)` as the truthful query (A.4); the profile fields are known-misleading for these two renderers. |
| O-7 | **Engine-layer documentation ownership.** `docs/cnaext-engine-layer.md` (2,444 lines) has a per-renderer matrix that is stale on OPENGL4, [retired renderers] and lists retired renderers; `docs/cnaext-nova3d.md` says EasyGL-only. Which document should the site treat as the source: the code-derived section 6 of the CNAEXT report below? | This sheet's section 6 was derived from code. |
| O-8 | **Is the SDL_GPU D3D12/Metal route verified?** `CNA_SDL_GPU_SHADERCROSS` defaults ON on Windows/Apple, but this audit ran on Linux and only read `cmake/RendererSelection.cmake:472-504` and the renderer code. | needs Windows/macOS evidence. |
| O-9 | **`GraphicsProfile::HiDef` requirement** should probably become a site-wide prerequisite (N1). The affected tutorials are 38-40, 61-70 and glTF tutorials that use 32-bit indices (110-114). Is it acceptable to change tutorial code to call `graphics_.setGraphicsProfileProperty(GraphicsProfile::HiDef)` everywhere, or should tutorials teach the Reach limits? | editorial decision. |
| O-10 | **Winding claims** (tutorials 32, 36, 37, 40: cubes and a ribbon are back-face-culled under the default state because XNA front faces are clockwise) were derived by winding analysis plus the renderers' front-face setting, not executed. | Worth one Software/HEADLESS pixel check before rewriting the meshes. |
| O-11 | **Stale statements inside CNA** that a writer should not copy: `PbrEffect.hpp:29-37` ("not image-based lighting"; "WebGPU unskinned only"), `SkinnedPbrEffect.hpp:35-38`, `VulkanRenderer.hpp:2790` (Texture3D refused), `ShaderEffect.hpp` comments about [retired], `ContentManager.cpp:2833-2838` ("FIRST mesh group only"), `IGraphicsRenderer.hpp:2698-2704`, `Texture2D.cpp:107-110`, `Fna3dSurfaceFormatTests.cpp:5-8` ("only [retired] answers this"), `docs/fx-compiled-effects.md` section 10 (omits OPENGL4, WEBGPU, SOFTWARE, DIRECTX9), `plans/plan_fx.md:1311` (SOFTWARE unsupported), `docs/renderer-capability-profiles.md:14` (30 vs 32 features), `modules/c-api/include/CNA/C/effects.h:1214-1223` (lists four compiled-effect renderers), `cmake/SharedRuntimeLibrary.cmake:145` (targets "exist only with CNA_CNAEXT"), `docs/cnaext-engine-changelog.md` / `docs/removed-renderers.md` (C ABI number). Consider reporting them upstream. | report to the CNA owner. |
